By unlocking the full amplification power of large entangled states, this work offers an efficient method to estimate high-precision quantum phase on near-term quantum processors.

</details>


### [100] [QUFIG: GNN-Based Prediction of Quantum Fault Injection Vulnerabilities with Gate-Level Precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01777)
*Shihan Zhao,Qiying Li,Ben Dong,Qian Wang,Yuntao Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出QUFIG框架，将门级故障分析形式化为受限保真度预算下的学习引导优先级排序问题，用基于电路DAG的GNN预测每个门-故障对的脆弱性得分并排序，从而以更少检测量定位高影响脆弱门-故障对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子硬件规模扩大与可访问性提升，使云量子计算平台出现运行时故障注入攻击等可靠性与安全挑战。现有工作无法以门级精度识别脆弱点，也难适应运行时环境。

Method: 将门级故障分析建模为受限保真度预算下的学习引导优先级排序问题；采用基于电路DAG的GNN骨干，预测每个门对每类注入故障的脆弱性得分（即该门-故障对对电路保真度的影响），再按得分对门-故障对排序。

Result: 在QASMbench与HamLib MaxCut上，QUFIG比随机与基于深度的启发式方法用更少检测次数即可恢复高影响脆弱门-故障对；在维持有效故障识别的同时，将需检测的门数量减少2.9%–19.8%。

Conclusion: QUFIG可实现门级、运行时自适应的故障脆弱性优先级排序，帮助量子电路设计者更高效地识别脆弱点并施加针对性防御。

Abstract: The growing scale and accessibility of quantum hardware exposed new reliability and security challenges in the quantum computing workflow, such as the run-time fault injection attacks in cloud-based quantum computing platforms. However, existing works fail to identify vulnerabilities with gate-level precision or adapt to run-time environments. In this work, we formulate gate-level fault analysis as a learning-guided prioritization problem under restricted fidelity budgets. The framework uses a circuit-DAG-based GNN backbone to predict the vulnerability score of each gate to each type of injected fault, defined as the impact of the gate-fault pair on circuit fidelity. The gate-fault pairs are then ranked by their vulnerability score. Experiments on QASMbench and HamLib MaxCut show that QUFIG recovers high-impact vulnerable gate-fault pairs with fewer inspections than random and depth-based heuristics. Our results show that QUFIG can reduce the number of gates requiring inspection by 2.9--19.8% while maintaining effective fault identification, allowing quantum circuit designers to identify vulnerabilities and apply targeted defenses more efficiently.

</details>


### [101] [Non-Markovian effects on informational steady states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01782)
*Jacob Werner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在连续监测碰撞模型框架下研究非马尔可夫效应对信息稳态（ISSs）的影响，发现稳态每次测量的信息增益与非马尔可夫度呈负相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息稳态（ISSs）在马尔可夫情形下已有研究，但记忆效应如何影响其形成与稳态性质尚不清楚，因此需要探究非马尔可夫性对ISSs的作用。

Method: 采用连续监测的碰撞模型，将环境建模为一系列双量子比特辅助单元（two-qubit ancillas）。

Result: 对该特定模型，稳态每次测量的信息增益与非马尔可夫度呈负相关；数值结果进一步刻画了系统-辅助单元及辅助单元-辅助单元相互作用参数对非马尔可夫度和稳态信息增益的影响。

Conclusion: 记忆效应（非马尔可夫性）会抑制ISSs的稳态信息增益，且该增益可由碰撞模型中的相互作用参数调控。

Abstract: Informational steady states (ISSs) arise when the rate at which information is gained from continuous measurements is exactly balanced by the rate at which information from earlier measurements is lost to the environment. While ISSs have been studied in Markovian settings, relatively little is known about how memory effects influence their formation and steady-state properties. In this work, we investigate non-Markovian effects on ISSs within the framework of continuously monitored collision models. We model the environment as a sequence of two-qubit ancillas and find that, for this specific model, the steady-state information gain per measurement is negatively correlated with the degree of non-Markovianity. Numerical results further characterize how the system-ancilla and ancilla-ancilla interaction parameters influence both the degree of non-Markovianity and the steady-state information gain.

</details>


### [102] [Learnt Attacks on Quantum Key Distribution under Channel Noise and Device Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01792)
*Marcel Mordarski,Benjamin Gras,Abdelrahman Shehata,Daniel Budina,Roberto Bondesan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文将自适应窃听形式化为受约束的马尔可夫决策过程，攻击者每轮选择一个由联合搜索得到的门电路，噪声按Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程漂移，并以每块轮次预算为中断条件；给出了自适应价值的最优固定电路下界与动态规划上界；在E91和BB84上验证了强化学习攻击显著逼近上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QKD链路的安全性基于对静态信道的分析，但实际设备决定了重校准之间的信道漂移。已有研究未量化无法改变信道自身噪声、仅能跟随漂移的窃听者能获得多少增益。此外，Decker等人仅在固定门模板、固定信道上训练参数化电路，难以处理无已知模板的噪声模型。

Method: 将自适应窃听建模为受约束马尔可夫决策过程：攻击者每轮选择一个电路作为动作，噪声水平服从Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程，中断条件为每块轮次的预算约束。以强化学习搜索攻击动作，并联合搜索门结构与旋转角度，使电路足够紧凑以构成离散动作集，从而扩展到无已知模板的噪声模型（如振幅阻尼信道）。理论分析给出自适应价值的最优固定电路下界与动态规划上界。

Result: 在双侧去极化噪声下的设备无关E91协议中，强化学习攻击者将Holevo信息量从最优固定电路的0.135提升至零探测下的0.348，达到上界的98%。在漂移比特翻转信道下的BB84协议中，其保真度超过保守的噪声索引规则0.024，达到上界的99%。在静态噪声下，攻击者由基不对称获得的增益在平均约束与逐基错误率约束之间发生符号变化。从随机门序列出发的搜索能恢复解析克隆器和集体攻击密钥率，并从上方满足Winick-Lütkenhaus-Coles目标的下界。

Conclusion: 自适应窃听相比最优固定电路可获得显著增益，尤其在噪声漂移条件下接近理论动态规划上界；联合搜索门结构与角度的电路方法可推广至无已知模板的噪声模型；静态噪声下约束方式（平均或逐基）会改变基不对称增益的方向。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) links are provisioned from security analyses of stationary channels, whereas the devices that determine the channel drift between recalibrations. Whether an eavesdropper who cannot alter the channel's own noise gains by following that drift has not been quantified. Adaptive eavesdropping is posed here as a constrained Markov decision process in which the attacker selects one circuit per round while the noise level follows an Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process and the abort condition is a budget over each block of rounds. The value of adaptation is bounded by the best fixed circuit and a dynamic-programming upper bound. The actions are learnt attacks. Whereas Decker et al. trained a parametrised circuit on a fixed gate template against a fixed channel, here the gate structure and rotation angles are searched jointly. This yields circuits compact enough to form a discrete action set, extending the construction to noise models lacking a known template, including the amplitude damping channel. On device-independent E91 under bilateral depolarising noise, a reinforcement-learning attacker raises her Holevo information from $0.135$ for the best fixed circuit to $0.348$ at zero detection, $98\%$ of the upper bound. On BB84 under a drifting bit-flip channel, she exceeds a conservative noise-indexed rule by $0.024$ in fidelity, reaching $99\%$ of the upper bound. Under stationary noise, the attacker's gain from basis asymmetry changes sign between an averaged and a per-basis error-rate constraint. The search, started from random gate sequences, recovers the analytical cloners and the collective-attack key rate, and meets the lower bound of the Winick--Lütkenhaus--Coles objective from above.

</details>


### [103] [Collision models: Markovian and Non-Markovian impurity models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01814)
*Jacob Werner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一类新的碰撞模型——马尔可夫杂质模型，利用BLP非马尔可夫性度量证明非马尔可夫性随杂质数单调增加，并研究临界参数对相互作用强度的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碰撞模型是研究开放量子系统与引入非马尔可夫性的灵活框架。本文受信息回流被周期性阻断的物理场景启发，旨在构建一类新的碰撞模型来刻画这种周期性阻断效应。

Method: 提出马尔可夫杂质模型（Markovian impurity models）这一新型碰撞模型类别；采用Breuer-Laine-Piilo（BLP）非马尔可夫性度量进行分析；通过数值模拟研究临界杂质数 $p$ 与系统-辅助子（system-ancilla）及辅助子-辅助子（ancilla-ancilla）相互作用强度之间的关系；并简要引入互补的非马尔可夫杂质模型。

Result: 证明若 $p$-杂质模型是非马尔可夫的，则对应的 $(p+1)$-杂质模型也必然是非马尔可夫的，从而存在一个使过程从马尔可夫转变为非马尔可夫的临界 $p$。数值模拟表明，尽管存在例外，增大任一种相互作用强度通常会降低临界 $p$；而当辅助子-辅助子相互作用足够弱时，即使没有杂质，动力学也保持马尔可夫性。

Conclusion: 马尔可夫杂质模型提供了一类结构清晰、可调控的非马尔可夫性研究平台，其非马尔可夫性随杂质数单调演化并存在临界阈值；临界 $p$ 受相互作用强度调控，且弱辅助子-辅助子相互作用可导致无杂质下仍保持马尔可夫性的区域。文中还简要提出了互补的非马尔可夫杂质模型作为后续方向。

Abstract: Collision models provide a flexible framework for studying open quantum systems and introducing non-Markovianity. We present a novel class of collision models, which we term Markovian impurity models, motivated by physical scenarios in which information backflow is periodically blocked. Using the Breuer-Laine-Piilo (BLP) measure of non-Markovianity, we show that if a $p$-impurity model is non-Markovian, then the corresponding $(p+1)$-impurity model is also non-Markovian, implying the existence of a critical $p$ at which the process transitions from Markovian to non-Markovian. Numerical simulations are used to investigate how this critical parameter depends on the system-ancilla and ancilla-ancilla interaction strengths. We find that, altough exceptions exist, increasing either interaction strength generally lowers the critical $p$, while sufficiently weak ancilla-ancilla interactions lead to a regime in which the dynamics remain Markovian even in the absence of impurities. Finally, we briefly introduce the complementary non-Markovian impurity model.

</details>


### [104] [QPI-DeepONet-MAC: A Scalable and Stable Hybrid Classical-Quantum Architecture for Physics-Informed Deep Operator Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01824)
*Said Lantigua,José Valencia,Gilson Giraldi,Renato Portugal,Jonas Maziero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出QPI-DeepONet-MAC混合量子-经典架构，将参数化量子电路通过乘加耦合融入PI-DeepONets，并给出通用逼近性、梯度下界（避免贫瘠高原）、传感器数量下界与信息正则化的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用算子学习求解参数化PDE是AI与物理建模交叉领域的核心挑战。PI-DeepONets虽引入控制方程，但在高维下优化困难；量子扩展虽增强表达能力，却易受贫瘠高原（barren plateaus）限制可训练性。因此需要一种兼具表达力与可训练性、可用于参数化PDE求解的量子增强算子学习框架。

Method: 提出QPI-DeepONet-MAC混合量子-经典架构：将参数化量子电路（PQC）通过经典表示与量子期望值的乘加耦合（MAC）集成进PI-DeepONets。理论分析方面：证明该架构保持通用算子逼近能力；推导量子参数梯度上/下界，给出避免贫瘠高原的显式条件及可训练性缩放准则；推导达到目标精度所需传感器数量的下界；并提出基于量子相干性与态保真度动态的信息正则化方案，以抑制相干性损失、保持训练中的量子结构。

Result: 理论上证明了架构的通用算子逼近能力；建立了量子参数梯度界及避免贫瘠高原的显式条件与可训练性缩放准则；给出传感器数量下界，将其与输入函数正则性、域维度和PDE动力学增长直接关联；所提相干性/保真度正则化可防止相干性损失并保持量子结构。

Conclusion: 这些结果共同为量子增强的物理信息算子学习奠定理论基础，使QPI-DeepONet-MAC成为求解参数化PDE的可扩展且稳定的框架。

Abstract: General operator learning for parametric partial differential equations (PDEs) is a fundamental challenge at the intersection of artificial intelligence and physics-based modeling. Physics-informed Deep Operator Networks (PI-DeepONets) incorporate governing equations into learning, but face optimization difficulties in high dimensions. Quantum extensions offer additional representational capacity, yet their trainability can be hindered by barren plateaus. Here, we introduce QPI-DeepONet-MAC, a hybrid quantum-classical architecture that integrates parameterized quantum circuits into PI-DeepONets through a multiplicative-and-additive coupling (MAC) of classical representations and quantum expectation values. We prove analytically that the proposed architecture retains universal operator approximation capabilities. Furthermore, we derive bounds on quantum parameter gradients, establishing explicit conditions to avoid barren plateaus alongside a scaling criterion for trainability. Additionally, we derive a lower bound on the number of sensors required for a target accuracy, directly linking sampling requirements to input function regularity, domain dimension, and PDE dynamical growth. Finally, we propose an informational regularization scheme based on quantum coherence and state fidelity dynamics to prevent coherence loss and preserve quantum structure during training. Together, these results establish a theoretical foundation for quantum-enhanced physics-informed operator learning and position QPI-DeepONet-MAC as a scalable, stable framework for solving parametric PDEs.

</details>


### [105] [Experimental realization of Wheeler's delayed-choice experiment with dual selections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01832)
*Xiaowan Yang,Xinglei Yu,Liangsheng Li,Xinzhi Zhao,Tianle Zheng,Chengjie Zhang,Chuan-Feng Li,Guang-Can Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于纠缠光子实现双选择延迟选择实验，同时控制马赫-曾德尔干涉仪中的两个分束器，揭示了单光子的波粒行为，并讨论了分束器处于存在与不存在的量子叠加时，光子处于波粒量子叠加态的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 惠勒延迟选择实验利用马赫-曾德尔干涉仪的不同构型展示粒子的波粒二象性，量子版本中通过控制第二个分束器的有无来观测光子的波粒行为。已有工作通常只控制单个分束器，本文希望将控制扩展到两个分束器，实现"双选择"，以更全面地研究波粒行为及其量子叠加。

Method: 采用基于纠缠光子的延迟选择实验方案，通过路径编码对马赫-曾德尔干涉仪中的两个分束器同时进行控制，从而独立操控第二个分束器的存在与不存在。

Result: 实验揭示了单光子在两个分束器不同构型下的波粒行为。

Conclusion: 进一步讨论了两个分束器同时处于存在与不存在的量子叠加的情形，此时光子将处于波粒量子叠加态。

Abstract: Wheeler's delayed-choice experiment demonstrates wave-particle duality of particles using different experimental configurations of a Mach-Zehnder interferometer. In a quantum version of this experiment, the wave-particle behavior of photons can be observed by controlling presence or absence of the second beam splitter. Here, we implement a delayed-choice experiment with dual selections based on entangled photons, experimentally controlling both of two beam splitters in the Mach-Zehnder interferometer, where the presence and absence of the second beam splitter are simultaneously controlled through path encoding. Our experiment reveals the wave-particle behavior of single photons under different configurations of two beam splitters. We further discuss the scenario when two beam splitters are in a quantum superposition of being present and absent, photons will be in a wave-particle quantum superposition state.

</details>


### [106] [Toward Optimal Circuit Depth for Geometrically Local Hamiltonian Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01839)
*Zhenyu Shen,Yusen Wu,Penghui Yao,Xiao Yuan,Yukun Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文构造了模拟晶格上有限程哈密顿量动力学的近邻量子线路，深度为 $O(T\log(nT/\epsilon))$，将以往的多对数开销降为单对数，并证明均匀 XXZ 模型线路深度下界为 $\Omega(\log n/\log\log n)$，达到近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相互作用量子系统动力学的模拟是量子模拟的核心目标。几何局域性表明数字模拟应保留底层动力学的并行性，但已有算法的线路深度存在较大的多对数开销。作者希望降低这一开销，并厘清局域性与精度在决定线路复杂度中的作用。

Method: 基于 Lieb-Robinson 分解的对数尺度分块，利用空间局域性同时控制高精度代价：在每个块内，用修正项补偿常数阶乘积公式的误差，从而获得指数级精度；修正项的实现深度与块直径成正比，不引入超出空间局域性所需的额外渐近深度。进而对均匀 XXZ 哈密顿量证明无条件线路深度下界。

Result: 构造出近邻量子线路，在固定维晶格、归一化局域相互作用强度、时间 $T\ge1$ 下模拟 $n$ 比特含时无关有限程哈密顿量，精度 $\epsilon$，深度 $O(T\log(nT/\epsilon))$，门数 $O(nT\log(nT/\epsilon))$，将 Haah 等的多对数开销降为单对数。同时证明均匀 XXZ 模型在固定演化时间、足够小固定误差下，任何近邻线路的深度下界为 $\Omega(\log n/\log\log n)$。

Conclusion: 上界 $O(\log n)$ 与下界 $\Omega(\log n/\log\log n)$ 仅相差双对数因子，表明该区域晶格哈密顿量模拟的线路深度近最优。结果阐明了局域性与精度在决定量子动力学线路复杂度中的作用，并提示了空间受限量子计算的更广泛原则。

Abstract: Understanding the dynamics of interacting quantum systems is a central goal of quantum simulation. For geometrically local Hamiltonians, locality suggests that digital simulation should retain much of the parallelism of the underlying dynamics. For a target accuracy $ε$, we construct a nearest-neighbor quantum circuit that simulates the dynamics of an $n$-qubit, time-independent, finite-range Hamiltonian with normalized local interaction strength on a fixed-dimensional lattice for time $T\ge1$, with circuit depth $O(T\log(nT/ε))$ and gate count $O(nT\log(nT/ε))$. This reduces the polylogarithmic overhead of Haah, Hastings, Kothari, and Low to a single logarithm. On logarithmic-scale blocks from the Lieb--Robinson decomposition, a shallow but highly accurate implementation is essential for reducing the circuit depth. Our key observation is that spatial localization also controls the cost of high precision. In each block, the error of a constant-order product formula is compensated for by a correction, yielding exponential accuracy. The correction can be implemented with depth proportional to the block diameter, introducing no additional asymptotic depth scale beyond that required by spatial localization.
  Furthermore, we prove an unconditional circuit-depth lower bound for uniform XXZ Hamiltonians: at fixed evolution time $T$ in the stated regime and sufficiently small fixed error $ε$, any nearest-neighbor quantum circuit simulating these dynamics requires depth $Ω(\log n/\log\log n)$. This matches our $O(\log n)$ upper bound up to a doubly logarithmic factor, establishing near-optimal circuit depth for lattice Hamiltonian simulation in this regime. Together, these results clarify the role of locality and precision in determining the circuit complexity of quantum dynamics and suggest broader principles for spatially constrained quantum computation.

</details>


### [107] [Trapdoored Clifford Operators and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01848)
*Minki Hhan,Hojune Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"陷门Clifford算子分布",其样本与均匀随机Clifford计算不可区分,但借助陷门可在近线性时间内采样与实现,并由此加速随机Clifford协议及给出矩阵/Clifford问题的最坏到平均归约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀随机n量子比特Clifford算子的采样与实现因Clifford群规模而接近二次复杂度,制约了随机化基准测试、经典影子、量子认证等应用。作者希望在不牺牲"看似均匀随机"这一性质的前提下突破该复杂度瓶颈。

Method: 在LPN问题的一个变体假设下构造陷门Clifford算子分布:样本与均匀随机Clifford计算不可区分,但持有陷门时可近线性时间采样与实现;构造支持对Pauli标签快速tableau作用以用于经典模拟,并可优化为多对数深度实现。同时构造有限域上的陷门矩阵,支持矩阵及其逆的高效乘法,解决Vaikuntanathan与Zamir [SODA'26]遗留的开放问题。

Result: 获得基于随机Clifford的更快协议;给出矩阵与Clifford问题(含迭代矩阵乘法、Clifford电路综合)的最坏到平均归约;特别证明Clifford电路批处理的困难性——将同一Clifford作用于多个寄存器的综合至少与最坏情况矩阵乘法同样困难,即使综合仅需在随机Clifford的小常数比例上成功,该结论也扩展到通用量子电路的近似实现。

Conclusion: 陷门化随机Clifford分布在保持计算不可区分性的同时显著降低实现复杂度,既加速了随机Clifford相关协议,也揭示了Clifford电路综合与矩阵乘法之间的深层困难性联系。

Abstract: Random Clifford operators have numerous applications in quantum computing, including randomized benchmarking, classical shadows, and quantum authentication. However, sampling and implementing uniformly random $n$-qubit Clifford incur near-quadratic complexity due to the size of Clifford group.
  We introduce a cryptographic way to overcome these barriers: trapdoored Clifford operator distributions whose samples are computationally indistinguishable from uniformly random Cliffords, yet implementing them can be much faster given the trapdoor. We construct a distribution of trapdoored Clifford operators whose elements can be sampled and implemented in near-linear time under a variant of the learning parity with noise assumption. Our constructions allow fast tableau action on Pauli labels for classical simulation, and also can be optimized to admit polylogarithmic-depth implementation. Along the way, we construct trapdoored matrices over finite fields that support efficient multiplication by both a matrix and its inverse, resolving an open question left by Vaikuntanathan and Zamir [SODA'26].
  We use these constructions to obtain faster protocols based on random Cliffords. We also explore their applications to the worst-case to average-case reductions for matrix and Clifford problems including the iterated matrix multiplication and Clifford circuit synthesis. In particular, we show the hardness of batching Clifford circuits: synthesizing circuits that apply the same Clifford to multiple registers is at least as hard as worst-case matrix multiplication, even when synthesis succeeds on a small constant fraction of random Cliffords. This extends to approximate implementations by general quantum circuits.

</details>


### [108] [Quantum Optimal Transport Barycenters: Existence, Duality, and Gaussian Rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01855)
*Augusto Gerolin,Zhiyi Lin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了量子最优传输（QOT）重心框架，给出存在性与对偶性结果，并针对高斯输入态证明了2-量子Wasserstein重心的全局刚性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典Wasserstein重心的理论推广到量子态情形，统一处理量子态与量子信道两种表述下的2-量子Wasserstein重心，并探究高斯输入是否强制重心为高斯且唯一。

Method: 在可分Hilbert空间上对可能无界的传输代价建立QOT重心框架的存在性与对偶性；对2-QW情形特化到Caglioti–Golse–Mouhot–Paul及De Palma–Trevisan的二次代价算子；将重心问题化简为协方差矩阵上的有限维凸优化，并通过协方差互补松弛下的状态重构原理，将最优协方差的唯一性提升为全量子态的唯一性。

Result: 证明重心问题存在高斯极小元并等价于协方差矩阵的有限维凸优化；在至少一个高斯输入为忠实（faithful）时，重心在所有量子态中唯一且必为高斯，即全局刚性定理成立。忠实性为充分而非必要条件：纯态输入族仍确定唯一重心，而部分纯的非忠实高斯输入可能出现多重重心。

Conclusion: 该框架统一并推广了2-量子Wasserstein重心理论，所证刚性定理刻画了高斯输入下重心的唯一性与高斯性，并明确了忠实性条件的边界。

Abstract: We develop a Quantum Optimal Transport (QOT) barycenter framework for quantum states, as an analog of Wasserstein barycenters~\cite{AguCar}, and establish existence and duality results for a broad class of possibly unbounded transport costs on separable Hilbert spaces. Our framework provides, in particular, a unified treatment of $2$-quantum Wasserstein (QW) barycenters in both the quantum-state and quantum-channel formulations by specializing to the canonical quadratic cost operators associated with the $2$-quantum Wasserstein distances of Caglioti--Golse--Mouhot--Paul~\cite{caglioti2021towards,Golse-Mouhot-Paul:2016} and De Palma--Trevisan~\cite{DPaTre19}.
  The central results concern Gaussian input states and, in particular, Gaussian rigidity: whether Gaussian input states force the 2-QW barycenter itself to be Gaussian and uniquely determined when the minimization is taken over all quantum states. We first show that the barycenter problem admits a Gaussian minimizer and reduces to a finite-dimensional convex optimization problem over covariance matrices.
  The main difficulty is that uniqueness of the optimal covariance does not, in general, imply uniqueness of the underlying quantum state. We bridge this gap through a state-reconstruction principle under covariance complementary slackness that upgrades uniqueness of the optimal covariance to uniqueness of the full quantum state, and thereby prove a global rigidity theorem: if at least one Gaussian input is faithful, then the barycenter is unique among all quantum states and is necessarily Gaussian. Faithfulness is sufficient but not necessary: families of pure inputs still determine a unique barycenter, whereas partially pure nonfaithful Gaussian inputs may admit multiple barycenters.

</details>


### [109] [Approximation theorems for fermionic Gaussian states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01860)
*Amir-Reza Negari,Farzin Salek,Zoltán Zimborás,Aram Harrow,Patrick Hayden,Jens Eisert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在Majorana协方差矩阵层面建立费米子高斯态的定量平均场原理：可容性条件蕴含两点关联的单调性约束，由此推导出高次图上的基态/自由能乘积态逼近、有限费米子高斯de Finetti定理，以及基于互信息指数聚类的条件互信息与可恢复性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体物理中一个反复出现的原理是：足够分布式的相互作用或关联会导致有效的平均场描述。作者希望在费米子高斯态这一可精确处理的框架下，把该原理从定性直觉提升为定量结论，并直接在协方差矩阵层面给出统一证明。

Method: 核心方法基于Majorana协方差矩阵的可容性（admissibility）条件，证明其对两点关联施加了定量的monogamy约束——固定区域在众多不相交区域间分配协方差的总预算有限。以该约束为工具，作者分别处理三个方向：高次有限简单图上的二次费米子Hamiltonian；在分次费米子拷贝置换下n-可交换的k拷贝高斯态；以及互信息指数聚类的空间费米子高斯态。

Result: 第一，对高次有限简单图上的二次费米子Hamiltonian，得到基态能量与有限温度自由能的乘积态近似，D-正则图上误差为O(D^{-1/2})。第二，证明有限费米子高斯de Finetti定理：在分次拷贝置换下n-可交换的k拷贝高斯态，在迹范数下与其单拷贝边缘的乘积相差O(k/n)；特别地，高斯类内的无限可交换性蕴含精确乘积结构。第三，对互信息指数聚类的空间费米子高斯态，证明跨分离缓冲区的条件互信息以O(r^{-1})衰减，进而由可恢复性得到O(r^{-1/2})迹范数近似。

Conclusion: 费米子高斯系统的乘积态近似、de Finetti近似与可恢复性估计均可直接由协方差层面的结构导出，表明协方差矩阵的单调性约束是统一且有效的分析工具。

Abstract: A recurring principle in many-body physics is that sufficiently distributed interactions or correlations lead to an effectively mean-field description. For fermionic Gaussian states, we establish a quantitative version of this principle directly at the level of Majorana covariance matrices. The main observation is that the admissibility condition for fermionic covariance matrices imposes a quantitative monogamy constraint on two-point correlations: a fixed region has only a bounded covariance budget to distribute among many disjoint regions. We use this principle in three directions. First, for quadratic fermionic Hamiltonians on finite simple graphs of high degree, we obtain product-state approximations to both the ground-state energy and the finite-temperature free energy, with error of order \(D^{-1/2}\) for \(D\)-regular graphs. Second, we prove a finite fermionic Gaussian de Finetti theorem: a \(k\)-copy Gaussian state that is \(n\)-exchangeable under graded fermionic copy permutations is \(O(k/n)\)-close in trace norm to the product of its one-copy marginal. In particular, infinite exchangeability within the Gaussian class implies exact product structure. Third, for spatial fermionic Gaussian states with exponentially clustering mutual information, we prove that the conditional mutual information across a separating buffer decays as \(O(r^{-1})\), and hence, by recoverability, the state admits an \(O(r^{-1/2})\) trace-norm approximation by a recovered state. These results show that product and de Finetti approximations and recoverability estimates for fermionic Gaussian systems can all be derived directly from covariance-level structure.

</details>


### [110] [Fourier Symmetrization for Geometric Quantum Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01874)
*Letao Wang,Abdel Lisser,Sreejith Sreekumar,Zeno Toffano*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过量子傅里叶模型（QFM）的傅里叶表示研究对称性如何影响表达能力，提出对称化系数的方差恒等式与近似界，并引入随机化编码实现无辅助比特的不变模型，在二维 PDE 基准上验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何量子机器学习虽将对称性纳入量子模型，但对称性如何塑造模型表达能力、又如何指导有效模型设计仍缺乏充分理解，本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 采用量子傅里叶模型（QFM）的傅里叶表示，将频谱按对称性组织为轨道并求和得到对称化系数；在可训练层构成独立精确 2-design 时推导系数方差恒等式，对 ε-近似 2-design 单层 QFM 给出偏离界；以超八面体群分析轨道增长对表达能力消失的缓解作用；对初等阿贝尔 2-群对称化得到纯多元 Chebyshev 多项式基；提出无需辅助比特与量子 twirling 额外线路深度的随机化编码。

Result: 在可训练层形成独立精确 2-design 时，每个对称化系数的方差等于其轨道内各系数方差之和；对 ε-近似 2-design 单层 QFM 给出偏离上界，其单项傅里叶系数界可较现有界指数级更紧；超八面体群的轨道增长可缓解对称化系数表达能力消失；在二维屏蔽泊松方程与定常粘性 Hamilton-Jacobi 方程上，采用硬边界约束的 QPINN 中，精确对称化与随机化编码分别取得最低平均误差。

Conclusion: 对称性通过轨道结构系统性地塑造 QFM 的频谱与表达能力，所提方差恒等式、近似界与随机化编码为设计高效对称量子模型提供了理论工具，并在量子物理信息神经网络基准上得到验证。

Abstract: Geometric quantum machine learning incorporates symmetry into quantum models, but how symmetry shapes their expressivity and guides effective model design remains insufficiently understood. We address this question through the Fourier representation of quantum Fourier models (QFMs). Symmetry organizes the frequency spectrum into orbits and sums the Fourier coefficients within each orbit into a symmetrized coefficient. When QFM trainable layers form independent exact 2-designs, the variance of each symmetrized coefficient equals the sum of the coefficient variances in its orbit. For single-layer QFMs with $\varepsilon$-approximate 2-design trainable layers, we bound the deviation from this identity. The resulting bound for individual Fourier coefficients can be exponentially tighter than an existing bound. The hyperoctahedral group provides an example of orbit growth that can mitigate vanishing expressivity of the symmetrized coefficients. Symmetrization of QFMs over an elementary abelian 2-group also yields pure multivariate Chebyshev polynomial basis functions. We introduce randomized encoding, which implements invariant models without ancilla qubits or the additional circuit depth for quantum twirling. We evaluate the models as quantum physics-informed neural networks (QPINNs) on two-dimensional screened Poisson and stationary viscous Hamilton-Jacobi equations. Under hard boundary constraints, QPINNs using exact symmetrization and randomized encoding achieve the lowest mean errors in the two benchmarks, respectively.

</details>


### [111] [Floquet-Universal Hamiltonian Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01878)
*Emilio Onorati,Harriet Apel,Michael M. Wolf,Toby Cubitt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了Floquet模拟理论，用周期性驱动的哈密顿量来合成时间无关哈密顿量，给出了Floquet通用哈密顿量的完整构造性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类比模拟与数字模拟是量子系统模拟的两条不同路径，分别对应时间无关哈密顿量与完全可控的时间依赖量子电路两种动力学机制。时间周期哈密顿量代表二者之间的中间机制，但此前缺乏系统的Floquet模拟理论。

Method: 利用周期驱动哈密顿量合成时间无关哈密顿量。证明了相互作用集合S可Floquet模拟Lie(S)李代数中的每个哈密顿量，从而给出Floquet通用哈密顿量的完整构造性刻画。构造仅使用O(1)量级的局域相互作用强度及其比例。

Result: 构造避免了类比模拟中常见的多尺度局域相互作用强度需求；对于k-局域晶格哈密顿量等重要类别，所需驱动频率范围随系统规模多项式增长。由此得到Floquet物理中自然物理性质与量的多项BQP完全性与QMA困难性结果。

Conclusion: 周期驱动哈密顿量可作为连接类比与数字模拟的中间机制，实现实用的Floquet通用模拟，并为Floquet物理的复杂性理论提供新的刻画。

Abstract: Analogue and digital Hamiltonian simulation represent two distinct approaches to making one quantum system replicate the physical properties of another, with consequences ranging from practical applications to complexity theory and quantum gravity. They are rooted in two distinct regimes of quantum dynamics: time-independent Hamiltonians for the former; fully controllable time-dependent quantum circuits in the latter. Time-periodic Hamiltonians represent an intermediate regime. In this work, we establish a theory of Floquet simulation where we use periodically driven Hamiltonians to synthesise time-independent ones.
  We show that a set of interactions $S$ can Floquet-simulate every Hamiltonian in the Lie algebra $\mathrm{Lie}(S)$, and consequently we give a complete and constructive characterisation of Floquet-universal Hamiltonians that are able to produce any target Hamiltonian. Notably, our construction uses only $O(1)$ local interaction strengths and ratios thereof, avoiding the impractical multi-scale local interaction strengths often required in analogue simulation via time-independent Hamiltonians. Instead, the construction requires a range of driving frequencies which scales polynomially with system size for important classes such as $k$-local lattice Hamiltonians. This has immediate complexity-theoretic implications, including numerous BQP-completeness and QMA-hardness results for natural physical properties and quantities studied in Floquet physics.

</details>


### [112] [Exponential quantum advantages for decoded quantum interferometry in the streaming setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01902)
*Kewen Wu,Guangxu Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究解码量子干涉测量(DQI)在流式设定下是否具有可证明的内存量子优势，并给出肯定答案。通过Hermite插值与Hasse导数推广最优多项式交集(OPI)问题，证明存在量子算法以单遍、多对数空间满足93%约束，而任何经典算法即使无限时间、多项式遍数，要达到76%也需多项式空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DQI（Jordan等，Nature 2025）在OPI问题上于经典算法需指数时间的区间达到了近似保证。然而除时间外，空间也是核心资源：大规模输入的存储与操作极具挑战，尤其当逻辑量子比特带有高额容错实现开销时。因此作者提出：DQI是否带来内存层面的量子优势，且能否无条件证明？

Method: 在流式设定下考虑OPI的自然推广：使用Hermite插值与Hasse导数，要求寻找一个低次多项式，尽可能多地满足关于其取值与各阶导数的约束。基于此设计DQI算法的流式改编版本。

Result: [量子效率] DQI算法的改编版本能产生满足93%约束的多项式，且仅单遍读取输入流，使用多对数空间，每个流条目的计算时间为多对数。[经典困难性] 任何经典算法若要产生满足仅76%约束的答案，即使允许多项式遍数读取输入流且时间不受限，仍需多项式空间。

Conclusion: 结果给出了可调参数的完整权衡曲线，表明DQI在流式设定下具有可证明的量子内存优势，并由此推出DQI对原始OPI问题同样具有可证明的量子优势。

Abstract: Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) is a polynomial-time quantum algorithm introduced by Jordan et al. (Nature 2025). For a natural optimization problem, known as optimal polynomial intersection (OPI), it achieves approximation guarantees in regimes where all known classical algorithms require exponential time.
  Besides time, space is another central resource: storing and manipulating a massive input can be very challenging, especially when logical qubits carry substantial fault-tolerant implementation overhead. This motivates the following question: does DQI yield quantum advantages in memory, and can we prove it unconditionally?
  We give an affirmative answer to this question in the streaming setting. In particular, we consider a natural generalization of OPI using Hermite interpolation and Hasse derivatives, which asks for a low-degree polynomial satisfying as many constraints on its values and derivatives as possible. As a concrete example, we show
  [Quantum efficiency.] An adaptation of the DQI algorithm produces a polynomial satisfying $93\%$ of the constraints; moreover, it only reads the input stream in one pass, uses polylogarithmic space, and has polylogarithmic computation time per stream entry.
  [Classical hardness.] Any classical algorithm that produces an answer satisfying just $76\%$ of the constraints requires polynomial space, even if it can read the input stream with polynomially many passes and can use unlimited time.
  Our result provides a complete tradeoff curve for the tunable parameters, and implies that DQI has provable quantum advantages for the original OPI problem.

</details>


### [113] [Loss-tolerant distributed lattice surgery using fusion networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01923)
*Felix Burt,Richard Meister,Sheng-Ku Lin,Kuan-Cheng Chen,Michael Hanks,Roberto Bondesan,M. S. Kim,Kin K. Leung*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用ZX演算构造混合症状提取协议，用于分布式旋转表面码晶格手术，在无本地噪声时达到50%界面擦除阈值，并通过提升界面距离改善低本地噪声下的亚阈值性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过光子链路连接基于物质的量子处理单元（QPU）是实现可扩展容错量子计算机的重要途径，但光子损耗和概率性线性光学操作使链路噪声显著强于本地QPU。测量基与融合基量子计算对损耗和概率性光子操作具有鲁棒性，有望在网络化QPU接口处补充电路基纠错。

Method: 采用ZX演算变换构造混合症状提取协议，并将其应用于分布式旋转表面码晶格手术。将擦除阈值转换为光子损耗阈值，并利用融合增强（fusion boosting）考察亚阈值性能。

Result: 在本地噪声缺失时，多种协议（包括使用融合贝尔对的电路基晶格手术和使用线性簇态的混合协议）均达到50%界面擦除阈值。相比直线贝尔对界面几何，混合协议将合并可观测量界面距离从d+1提升至2d+1，并将垂直可观测量界面距离从⌊(d+1)/2⌋恢复至d。计算了截断资源态下的阈值下降，并刻画了资源态错误、本地电路噪声和融合擦除下的可纠正区域。在电路与资源态错误率为10^-3时，本地错误基本掩盖了界面距离优势；当本地噪声降至10^-4及以下时，线性链协议随擦除降低改善更快。

Conclusion: 界面距离增强在低本地噪声区间才更有效地转化为性能优势；在较高本地噪声下其收益被本地错误掩盖。

Abstract: Networking matter-based quantum processing units (QPUs) offers a promising route to scaling fault-tolerant quantum computers. This requires distributed logical operations to be performed across photonic links, where noise is characteristically different from and stronger than in local QPUs owing to photon loss and probabilistic linear-optical operations. Measurement- and fusion-based quantum computing are designed to be robust against loss and probabilistic photonic operations, suggesting they could complement circuit-based error correction at the interface between networked QPUs. We use ZX calculus transformations to construct hybrid syndrome extraction protocols and apply them to distributed rotated surface code lattice surgery, demonstrating that several protocols attain a $50\%$ interface-erasure threshold when local noise is absent, including circuit-based lattice surgery using fused Bell pairs and hybrid protocols using linear cluster states. Relative to a straight Bell-pair interface geometry, the hybrid protocols increase the merge-observable interface distance from $d+1$ to $2d+1$ and restore the perpendicular-observable interface distance from $\lfloor(d+1)/2\rfloor$ to $d$. We calculate how the threshold decreases when using truncated resource states and map correctable regions under resource-state errors, local circuit noise, and fusion erasure. We then convert these erasure thresholds into photon-loss thresholds and probe subthreshold performance using fusion boosting. At circuit and resource-state error rates of $10^{-3}$, local errors largely mask the interface-distance advantage. As local noise is reduced to $10^{-4}$ and below, the linear-chain protocols improve more rapidly with decreasing erasure, suggesting that interface distance enhancements are effective in low local noise regimes.

</details>


### [114] [Avoiding Exponentially Large Groups with Open Quantum System Technology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01932)
*Jihong Cai,Advith Govindarajan,Marius Junge*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于开放量子系统的态制备新方法：在(n+3)量子比特上识别出一个李代数维数为poly(n)的小李群G，通过重复使用部分迹、环境态制备与群操作，可从任意固定输入态逼近任意态；并进一步用单一"开放系统魔"相互作用哈密顿量实现密度矩阵空间上的传递性，以及通过更大环境逼近任意量子信道，实现信道普适性，进而得到"廉价/昂贵"态、密度与信道的新图景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统态制备方法通常依赖n量子比特上的完整酉群，控制开销大。作者希望借助开放量子系统（部分迹、环境辅助、耗散等）的全部能力，用更小、更廉价的群结构与有限的相互作用来实现普适的态、密度矩阵和信道制备。

Method: 1) 在(n+3)量子比特空间中识别一个李群G，其李代数维数为poly(n)，该群派生自局域Pauli串的李代数；2) 对任意固定输入态，反复施加部分迹（partial trace）、环境中的态制备以及G的群操作来逼近目标态；3) 识别单一"开放系统魔"相互作用哈密顿量，将其酉群与上述开放系统技术结合，使作用在所有密度矩阵空间上具有传递性；4) 通过引入更大环境，用类似手段逼近n量子比特上的任意信道，实现信道普适性。

Result: 1) 证明了仅用poly(n)维李代数的小群配合部分迹与环境态制备，即可从任意固定输入态逼近任意量子态；2) 单一开放系统魔相互作用哈密顿量使群作用在密度矩阵空间上传递；3) 借助更大环境可逼近n量子比特上的所有信道，达成信道普适性；4) 由局域Pauli串李代数导出的这些小群，划分出"廉价"与"昂贵"的态、密度及信道的新分类图景。

Conclusion: 开放量子系统为态制备提供了超越完整酉群控制的新范式：通过维数仅为poly(n)的小李群、部分迹与环境辅助操作，以及单一开放系统魔哈密顿量，可实现态、密度矩阵与信道的普适逼近，并由此得到一个关于制备成本（廉价/昂贵）的全新分类体系。

Abstract: We propose a novel approach to state preparation by embracing the full power of open quantum systems. Instead of working with the whole unitary group in $n$ qubits, we identify a small Lie group $G$ in $(n+3)$ qubits. The dimension of its Lie algebra is $\text{poly}(n)$. Every state can be approximated starting from any fixed input state by repeatedly using partial trace and state preparation in the environment alongside group operations. Moreover, we also identify a single (open system magic) interaction Hamiltonian whose unitary group can be combined with open system quantum technology mentioned above to achieve transitivity on the space of all densities. Similarly, we show that all channels on $n$ qubits can be approximated by using a larger environment, achieving channel universality. The interesting small groups we identify are derived from Lie algebras of local Pauli strings and lead to a new landscape of cheap and expensive states, densities, and channels.

</details>


### [115] [Optimal query complexity for fractional quantum evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01940)
*Anthony Yuezhang Liu,Adam Wesołowski,Jayne Thompson,Mile Gu,Lirandë Pira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了分数次查询问题的紧确查询复杂度下界，表明 QSVT 的构造在渐近意义下最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数次查询问题要求在给定未知酉算符 U=e^{iH} 的 oracle 访问下，实现非整数次幂 U^t=e^{itH}。QSVT 已给出 O(1/δ log(1/ε)) 的上界，但该复杂度是否最优、是否存在匹配下界尚不清楚。

Method: 将任意 N 次查询电路归约为用次数不超过 O(N) 的三角多项式逼近 e^{itθ}，并结合 Remez 不等式导出下界；同时给出基于构造线性泛函湮灭逼近空间的替代证明。

Result: 证明了任意查询算法的下界 Ω_τ(1/δ log(1/ε))，从而最优查询复杂度为 Θ_τ(1/δ log(1/ε))；替代方法给出对 δ 一致（uniform）的 Ω_τ(log(1/ε)) 下界。

Conclusion: 分数次查询问题的最优查询复杂度确为 Θ_τ(1/δ log(1/ε))，说明已知的 QSVT 构造在渐近意义下是最优的；两种下界证明均确认了这一紧确刻画。

Abstract: Given oracle access to an unknown unitary $U=e^{iH}$ , the fractional query problem asks how many queries are required to implement a noninteger power $U^t=e^{itH}$, $0<t<1$, when the spectrum is separated from the branch cut by a gap $δ$. Quantum singular value transformation gives an upper bound of $O\!\left(\frac{1}δ\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)$ queries for approximation error $\varepsilon$. We prove a matching lower bound for arbitrary query algorithms. Our argument reduces any $N$-query circuit to the approximation of $e^{itθ}$ by a trigonometric polynomial with degree bounded by $O(N)$, together with Remez inequality. This allows us to establish the lower bound of $Ω_τ\!\left(\frac{1}δ\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)$. Consequently, the optimal query complexity for fractional query problem is $Θ_τ\!\left(\frac{1}δ\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)$, showing that the known QSVT construction is asymptotically optimal. We also give an alternative lower bound proof based on constructing a linear functional that annihilates the approximant space, yielding a $Ω_τ\!\left(\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)$ bound uniform to $δ$.

</details>


### [116] [Universality Sacrifices Reliability in Classical-Quantum Channel Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01941)
*Kaito Watanabe,Masahito Hayashi,Takaya Matsuura,Hao-Chung Cheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明经典-量子信道中通用编码无法普遍达到信道已知（channel-aware）场景下的最优可靠性，并完整刻画了通用编码的最优可靠性指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典信道中通用码可同时达到容量与最优高码率可靠性。作者希望考察这一"兼容性"是否在经典-量子信道中依然成立，特别是输出系统的酉旋转未知对可靠性获取的影响。

Method: 构造一簇经典-量子信道，证明不存在固定编码方案能达到信道已知的最优可靠性；对酉不变解码器（通用编码的自然假设）推导可靠性逆界；构造与信道无关的编码器-解码器对并给出通用可达界。

Result: 酉不变解码器的逆界可严格小于信道已知的最优误差指数；所构造的通用可达界在高码率区间与该逆界匹配，从而刻画了最优通用可靠性。信道已知指数由Petz Rényi散度支配，通用指数由sandwiched Rényi散度支配；二者在输出可交换时重合、不可交换时分离。

Conclusion: 通用性在经典情形下保持最优可靠性，但在量子情形下会产生根本性的可靠性代价；该代价源于输出酉旋转未知导致的两种Rényi散度差异。

Abstract: Universal channel coding enables communication without a complete description of the channel. For classical channels, universal codes can attain both capacity and the optimal high-rate reliability. We show that this compatibility fails for classical-quantum channels in general; that is, the optimal reliability in the channel-aware scenario is not always achievable with universal coding due to the ignorance of the unitary rotation of the output system. We exhibit a family of classical-quantum channels for which one cannot achieve the channel-aware optimal reliability by a fixed coding scheme. We further derive a converse bound on the reliability for unitary-invariant decoders, a natural assumption for the universal coding scheme, that can be strictly smaller than the optimal channel-aware error exponent. Conversely, we construct a channel-independent encoder-decoder pair and establish a universally achievable bound on the reliability that matches this converse bound in the high-rate regime, thereby characterizing the optimal universal reliability. Specifically, the channel-aware and universal exponents are governed by the Petz and sandwiched Rényi divergences, respectively. These divergences coincide for commuting outputs but differ for noncommuting ones, explaining why universality preserves optimal reliability classically but can reduce it quantumly. Our results showcase the fundamental reliability cost of performing the classical-quantum channel coding task universally.

</details>


### [117] [Hardware-Efficient Ground-State Preparation using Variational Imaginary-Time Majorana Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01954)
*Federico Santona,Manuel G. Algaba,Aeishah Ameera Anuar,Anna M. Wernbacher,Prachi Sharma,Fedor Šimkovic*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 VIME 经典预训练算法与 c-tUPS 压缩拟设，用于分子基态制备，在强关联体系上以显著更少的 CNOT 和双比特深度达到或优于 DMRG 参考精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期容错量子化学需要紧凑的基态制备电路。现有基于 ADAPT-VQE 的变分 Majorana 传播方法（ADAPT-VMPE）在强关联分子上能量精度不足且量子资源开销大，因此需要更高效的经典预训练与压缩拟设方案。

Method: 提出变分虚时 Majorana 演化（VIME），将算子投影变分量子虚时演化（OVQITE）扩展到分子电子结构，基于单个编译的 Majorana 传播（MP）代理图进行经典预训练；同时开发压缩分块酉积态（c-tUPS）拟设，以降低经典模拟与量子态制备成本。

Result: 在强关联钌配合物 TLD-1411 中，最高 52 量子比特的活性空间计算达到化学精度（对标 DMRG 参考能量），能量误差比 ADAPT-VMPE 小约 400 倍；在至七并苯（60 量子比特）的强多参考并苯系列中，VIME 匹配或超越 ADAPT-VMPE 的 MP 报道精度，同时 CNOT 数最多减少 26 倍、双比特深度最多降低 167 倍。

Conclusion: 压缩变分拟设的虚时经典预训练是一种资源高效的分子基态制备途径，VIME 结合 c-tUPS 在强关联体系上展现出精度与量子资源上的双重优势。

Abstract: Compact ground-state preparation circuits are essential for early fault-tolerant quantum chemistry. We introduce Variational Imaginary-time Majorana Evolution (VIME), a classical pre-training algorithm that extends operator-projected variational quantum imaginary-time evolution (OVQITE) to molecular electronic structure using a single compiled Majorana-propagation (MP) surrogate graph. We pair VIME with a compressed tiled Unitary Product State (c-tUPS) ansatz that we develop to reduce both classical simulation and quantum state-preparation costs. In the strongly correlated ruthenium complex TLD-1411, active-space calculations requiring up to 52 qubits achieve chemical precision against DMRG reference energies, with energy errors approximately $400\times$ smaller than those from ADAPT-VQE-based variational Majorana-propagation (ADAPT-VMPE). Across the strongly multireference acene series up to heptacene (60 qubits), VIME matches or improves on the MP-reported energy accuracy of the ADAPT-VMPE comparison points with up to $26\times$ fewer CNOTs and $167\times$ lower two-qubit depth. These results establish imaginary-time classical pre-training of compressed variational ansätze as a promising route to resource-efficient molecular ground-state preparation.

</details>


### [118] [Fully tolerant product state testing and closest product state learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01979)
*Zongbo Bao,Jonas Helsen,Tuyen Nguyen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子多体态的乘积态测试与学习问题，提出基于随机着色和分块谱投影的算法，实现与n无关副本数的容忍测试器，并改进最近乘积态学习算法的副本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判断未知量子态是否为乘积态或远离乘积态是量子多体物理中的基本问题，现有方法在容忍测试和最近乘积态学习上副本复杂度较高，需要更高效且与n无关的算法。

Method: 提出随机着色论证：任意未知量子态都存在将[n]分成q部分的划分，使得与该划分下最近乘积态重叠平方仅增加O(1/q)。将问题归约为q方容忍测试；结合分块谱投影技术证明Harrow & Montanaro乘积态测试的k副本推广是高效容忍测试器。学习方面结合qudit版Bakshi等高保真乘积态学习算法和基于Werner最优克隆信道的采样技术。

Result: 给出时间高效、与n无关副本数的容忍测试算法；学习算法需Õ((nd)^2) 2^{Õ(1/ε^8)}个副本，可产生ε近似最优乘积态。

Conclusion: 随机着色与分块谱投影是统一而有力的技术工具，可同时改进乘积态测试与最近乘积态学习，显著降低副本复杂度并实现与系统规模n无关的测试。

Abstract: We address the problem of testing whether an unknown $n$-qudit state $ρ$ is $a$-close to a product state or $b$-far away from any product state, as measured in terms of state overlap. We provide a time-efficient algorithm to solve this problem that requires an $n$-independent number of copies of the unknown state. Our random coloring argument shows that for any unknown state there always exists a partition of $[n]$ into $q$ parts such that the square of the overlap with the closest product state with respect to this partition is only an additive factor $O(1/q)$ larger. This reduces the problem to tolerant testing of $q$ parties, with potentially growing local dimensions. Combining this insight with blockwise spectral projection arguments we can show that the natural $k$-copy generalization of Harrow & Montanaro's product state test provides an efficient tolerant tester.
  We use the same random coloring and blockwise spectral projection techniques to obtain a substantially improved algorithm for closest product state learning. In particular we give an algorithm that takes in $\widetilde{O}\big((nd)^2\big)\, 2^{\widetilde{O}(1/\varepsilon^8)}$ copies of the unknown state and produces an $\varepsilon$-approximately optimal product state. The key technical components of this learner are a qudit variant of the Bakshi et al. high-fidelity product state learning algorithm and a sampling technique based on Werner's optimal cloning channel.

</details>


### [119] [On the Metastability of the Mean-Field Interchange Model for Local Dimension $\geq 3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01987)
*Sergio Escobar,Lin Lin,Michael Ragone,Kevin D. Stubbs*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明平均场交换模型在低维局域下Davies动力学的热化行为存在非单调性：在静态一阶相变区间内谱隙随系统尺寸指数消失（亚稳态），但在更低和更高温度下谱隙恢复为多项式下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统在低温下常因亚稳态而缓慢热化，但作者希望探究是否存在进一步降温反而恢复多项式时间热化的情形，并刻画其动力学相图。

Method: 研究局域维度d≥3、具有单格点广义Pauli耦合的平均场交换模型的Davies动力学；利用Schur-Weyl对偶将Davies生成元分解为Young图上的经典Markov生成元，并分析其余对称扇区（谱隙为Ω(n^{-1})）。

Result: 得到由β_min(d)与β=d两个逆温度界定的动力学相图，静态一阶相变位于二者之间；在区间(β_min(d), d)内谱隙随n指数消失，而区间外（更低和更高温度）谱隙有系统尺寸的逆多项式下界；指数慢化完全来自Young图链。

Conclusion: 进一步冷却可恢复多项式时间热化，热化的非单调温度依赖由Young图链的经典动力学主导，Schur-Weyl对偶是分析的关键工具。

Abstract: Many quantum systems are believed to thermalize slowly at low temperatures due to the existence of metastable phases. However, there are cases where further cooling can restore polynomial-time thermalization: we demonstrate that the Davies dynamics of the mean-field interchange model with local dimension $d\geq 3$ and single-site generalized Pauli couplings exhibits this behavior. The dynamical phase diagram is beholden to two distinguished inverse temperatures, $β_{\mathrm{min}}(d)$ and $β=d$, with the static first-order phase transition lying between them. At low and high temperatures surrounding the interval $(β_{\mathrm{min}}(d),d)$, the spectral gap of the Davies generator is bounded below by an inverse polynomial in system size $n$. Meanwhile, in this interval, the gap vanishes exponentially in $n$, indicating metastability. The proof uses Schur-Weyl duality to decompose the Davies generator into a classical Markov generator on Young diagrams, and the restriction to the remaining symmetry sectors whose gap is $Ω(n^{-1})$. Thus, exponential slowdown arises entirely from the Young diagram chain.

</details>


### [120] [Geometry of Knill-Laflamme Coefficients for Pauli Error Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01992)
*Baisong Sun,Ningping Cao,Yiu Tung Poon,Bei Zeng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文刻画了Knill-Laflamme系数向量构成的几何结构，证明表示重数足以将主动期望数据提升为精确标量压缩，并由此给出系数范围凸性、连通性及签名谱为闭区间的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Knill-Laflamme系数通过误差算子到码空间的标量压缩来刻画精确量子纠错。对于给定Pauli可观测量和固定码维数，可达系数向量构成联合高阶数值域，其欧氏范数像即签名谱。作者希望理解算子表示、码维数与检测约束如何决定这一几何结构，并为连通系数范围与区间签名谱寻找更清晰的判据。

Method: 引入联合高阶数值域与签名谱框架，研究算子表示、码维数和检测约束对系数几何的影响。核心手段是分析表示重数对主动期望态的标量压缩可实现性：证明只要每个被占据块的重数至少等于码维数乘以相应主动态的秩，就存在提升。随后在此充分条件下推导系数范围等于完整主动期望体。通过交换Pauli族、子系统稳定子码、母Hamiltonian构造及三量子比特范例进行验证。

Result: 主要定理表明，表示重数足以将主动期望数据提升为精确标量压缩。当重数足以实现所有主动态时，系数范围为完整主动期望体，因而是凸且连通的，其签名谱为闭区间。该框架统一了交换Pauli族（足够共同本征空间简并时产生多胞体）与子系统稳定子码（受保护逻辑子系统为非交换规范可观测量提供重数空间）。显式例子显示：增大码维数可使范围收缩、塌缩或变空，而增大重数可将Bloch球变为实心球。子系统情形中，母Hamiltonian构造将精确检测码实现为简并基态空间。三量子比特例子中，额外非交换检测约束把四面体范围约化为其重心与四个顶点，导致签名谱不连通。

Conclusion: 本文为Knill-Laflamme系数几何提供了结构性框架，揭示了重数、码维数与检测约束之间的张力，并为进一步刻画结构化误差模型下连通系数范围与区间签名谱的更精细判据提供了动机。

Abstract: Knill-Laflamme coefficients characterize exact quantum error detection through scalar compressions of error operators to the code space. For prescribed Pauli observables and a fixed code dimension, attainable coefficient vectors form a joint higher-rank numerical range, whose Euclidean norm image is the signature spectrum. We study how operator representation, code dimension, and detection constraints govern this geometry. Our main theorem identifies representation multiplicity as sufficient to lift active expectation data to exact scalar compressions: a lift exists whenever each occupied block has multiplicity at least the code dimension times the rank of the corresponding active state. When multiplicity suffices to realize all active states, the coefficient range is the full active expectation body and is therefore convex and connected, with a closed interval as its signature spectrum. This framework unifies commuting Pauli families, where sufficient common-eigenspace degeneracy yields polytopes, and subsystem stabilizer codes, where protected logical subsystems supply multiplicity spaces for noncommuting gauge observables. Explicit examples show how increasing the code dimension can make a range shrink, collapse, or become empty, while increasing multiplicity can turn a Bloch sphere into a filled ball. In the subsystem setting, parent-Hamiltonian constructions realize exact detecting codes as degenerate ground spaces, selecting distinguished vectors and continuous paths within these ranges. A final three-qubit example shows additional noncommuting detection constraints reduce a tetrahedral range to its barycenter and four vertices, producing a disconnected signature spectrum. The results provide a structural framework for Knill-Laflamme coefficient geometry and motivate sharper criteria for connected coefficient ranges and interval signature spectra under structured error models.

</details>


### [121] [Operational transformation rates of quantum states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02025)
*Giulia Mazzola,Renato Renner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文放松量子态iid假设，用de Finetti型论证表明"操作上不可区分于张量幂"的态集渐近可表示为近iid态，并引入纠缠成本与蒸馏等渐近变换率的操作类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常用张量幂描述量子态的多份拷贝，但从操作角度，实验者无法排除未知位置缺陷或拷贝间关联，故iid假设先验不成立。

Method: 将iid假设放松，考虑一组"在实验者看来与张量幂态根本上不可区分"的资源态；借助de Finetti型论证刻画该操作态集的渐近结构。

Result: 证明该操作态集渐近可由近iid态表示；据此引入渐近变换率（如纠缠成本、纠缠蒸馏）的操作类比。

Conclusion: 操作类比变换率是否与iid对应量一致，成为有待回答的问题。

Abstract: Typically, multiple copies of a quantum state are described by a tensor power of that state. However, from an operational point of view, an experimentalist cannot, in general, rule out imperfections at unknown positions or correlations between copies, so assuming independent and identically distributed (iid) copies is not justified a priori. In the following, we relax the iid assumption by considering a whole set of resource states that are fundamentally indistinguishable from a tensor power state, and in that sense look like many copies of the quantum state to the experimentalist. Using a de Finetti-type argument, we show that this operational set of states can asymptotically be represented by almost-iid states. As an application, we introduce operational analogues of asymptotic transformation rates, such as entanglement cost and distillation. This raises the question of whether these rates coincide with their iid counterparts.

</details>


### [122] [(A Variant of) Clifford Circuit Synthesis is NP-Complete](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02029)
*Luna Lima Keller,Richard Kueng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究最优Clifford电路合成问题，证明其一个变体是NP-hard的，从而与已有的启发式算法（如归约到3-SAT）形成匹配的硬度结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优电路合成是量子和经典硬件设计中的核心问题。经典版本在启发式和严格硬度方面已有充分理解，但最优量子电路合成的复杂度认识不足，尤其是Clifford电路合成缺乏严格的下界结果。

Method: 证明分两部分：(i) 将3-正则图上的3边着色问题归约到一个仅涉及CZ门的电路合成问题；(ii) 证明额外的初等Clifford门（尤其是Hadamard、相位门和CNOT）的存在不会带来最优电路深度的进一步改进。

Result: 最优Clifford合成的一个变体是NP-hard的，由此建立了与现有启发式方法相匹配的硬度结论；并进一步刻画了Clifford电路的复杂度图景：模拟与等价性检验属于P，而判定Clifford酉变换能否在给定深度内实现是NP-complete的。

Conclusion: 该工作加深了对Clifford电路复杂性的理论理解，明确了合成问题在复杂度层级中的位置，与可高效求解的模拟和等价性检验形成鲜明对比。

Abstract: Optimal circuit synthesis is the problem of finding the shortest-depth circuit representation of a given functionality with respect to a pre-specified elementary gate set. This is a central problem in both quantum and classical hardware design. While the classical version is very well understood -- both in terms of heuristics and rigorous hardness assertions -- much less is known about optimal quantum circuit synthesis.
  We focus on optimal Clifford circuit synthesis, for which various heuristics are known, e.g. via reduction to 3-SAT. Our main result supplies a matching hardness result: a variant of optimal Clifford synthesis is NP-hard. The proof proceeds in two parts: (i) reduce 3-edge colorability on 3-regular graphs to a circuit synthesis problem that only involves CZ gates, (ii) prove that the availability of additional elementary Clifford gates -- most notably: Hadamard, phase and CNOT -- cannot lead to further improvements of the optimal circuit depth. Our work sharpens the complexity-theoretic understanding of Clifford circuits: simulation and equivalence checking are in P, whereas deciding whether a Clifford unitary admits an implementation within a prescribed depth is NP-complete.

</details>


### [123] [Sparse Hamiltonian simulation with optimal dependence on the maximum column Euclidean norm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02030)
*Zecheng Li,Chunhao Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种模拟d-稀疏厄米哈密顿量的量子算法，查询复杂度为O(tΛ√d + √d log(2/ε))，去除了Low算法中的次多项式开销，并在特定参数范围达到最坏情形下界，同时应用于稀疏量子线性系统求解与黑盒酉算子实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Low [STOC 2019] 的稀疏哈密顿量模拟算法存在次多项式开销，未达到已知最坏情形下界；此外Berry和Childs [QIC 2012] 提出的黑盒酉算子实现查询复杂度问题尚未解决。

Method: 针对d-稀疏厄米哈密顿量H，假设已知其最大列欧氏范数||H||_{1→2}的上界Λ，设计量子模拟算法，使用稀疏oracle查询，并将精度项改为可加的对数形式；同时将该方法推广到稀疏量子线性系统求解及黑盒酉算子的门高效实现。

Result: 对tΛ≥1/2，算符范数误差ε的模拟使用O(tΛ√d + √d log(2/ε))次稀疏oracle查询；当d>1且tΛ≥log(2/ε)时匹配最坏情形下界；门数量与查询规模线性相关。应用于d-稀疏量子线性系统得到O(κ√d polylog(κ/ε))查询；对每行每列至多d个非零元的黑盒酉算子以O(√d log(2/ε))查询实现，常数误差下达到最优，任意N×N酉算子为Θ(√N)。

Conclusion: 该工作消除了稀疏哈密顿量模拟中的次多项式开销并达到下界，同时解决了黑盒酉算子实现的开放问题，为稀疏量子线性系统求解提供了高效算法。

Abstract: We give a quantum algorithm for simulating a $d$-sparse Hermitian Hamiltonian $H$, assuming a known upper bound $Λ$ on its maximum column Euclidean norm $\|H\|_{1\to2}$. For $tΛ\ge1/2$, simulation with operator-norm error $ε$ uses \[
  O\!\left(tΛ\sqrt d+\sqrt d\log(2/ε)\right) \] sparse-oracle queries. This removes the subpolynomial overhead in Low's algorithm [STOC 2019], replacing it with an additive logarithmic precision term. For $d>1$ and $tΛ\ge\log(2/ε)$, the bound matches the worst-case lower bound. A known spectral-norm upper bound may also be used in place of $Λ$. The number of 1- and 2-qubit gates is linear in the query scale, up to oracle costs and polynomial overhead in the input bit lengths and logarithmic precision parameters.
  As applications, we obtain $O(κ\sqrt d\,\mathrm{polylog}(κ/ε))$ queries for solving $d$-sparse quantum linear systems with $\|A\|\le1$ and $\|A^{-1}\|\leκ$, under standard sparse and state-preparation access. We also give a gate-efficient implementation of black-box unitaries with at most $d$ nonzero entries per row and column using $O(\sqrt d\log(2/ε))$ queries, given sparse access to the unitary and its adjoint. At constant error, the query bound is optimal and yields $Θ(\sqrt N)$ queries for arbitrary $N\times N$ unitaries, resolving the open question on black-box unitary implementation posed by Berry and Childs [QIC 2012].

</details>


### [124] [Adaptivity is all you need: Optimal stabilizer learning using just single-copy measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02031)
*L. Bittel,J. Eisert,W. Gong,A. A. Mele,L. Schatzki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明自适应单副本Clifford测量可完全弥合稳定子态学习中的样本复杂度差距：以Θ(n)次单副本测量完成学习，达到Bell采样的最优样本复杂度，并将结果推广到稳定子零化度受限的态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定子态在量子纠错、基准测试与经典模拟中至关重要，但其可学习性存在显著差距：两副本Bell测量仅需Θ(n)个副本，而非自适应单副本测量需Ω(n²)个副本。作者旨在探究自适应性能否弥合该差距。

Method: 提出一种多项式时间自适应算法，仅使用Θ(n)次单副本Clifford测量学习任意n量子比特稳定子态；相同思想用于构造样本最优的单副本容忍检验器，并在k量子比特量子内存下给出最优检验权衡Θ(n−k+1/ε)；进一步将该自适应机制推广到稳定子零化度至多为r的态。

Result: 自适应单副本Clifford测量以Θ(n)次测量实现稳定子态学习，匹配Bell采样的最优样本复杂度且无需多副本测量；给出样本最优单副本容忍检验器及带k量子比特内存时的最优权衡Θ(n−k+1/ε)（不忠度ε）；对稳定子零化度≤r的态（含受限T门数量Clifford电路制备的态）可用O(n2^r)次单副本测量学习。

Conclusion: 自适应性完全弥合了稳定子态学习的单副本与多副本样本复杂度差距，并统一了学习、检验及含有限非Clifford资源的扩展场景，表明单副本自适应测量在容错量子信息处理中具有最优效率。

Abstract: Stabilizer states are central to quantum computing, underlying quantum error correction, benchmarking, and efficient classical simulation. Yet their learnability exhibits a striking gap: an $n$-qubit stabilizer state can be learned from $Θ(n)$ copies using two-copy Bell measurements, whereas non-adaptive single-copy measurements require $Ω(n^2)$ copies. Here we show that adaptivity completely closes this gap. We give a polynomial-time adaptive algorithm that learns an arbitrary $n$-qubit stabilizer state from $Θ(n)$ single-copy Clifford measurements, matching the optimal sample complexity of Bell sampling without any multi-copy measurements. The same ideas yield a sample-optimal single-copy tolerant tester and, with $k$ qubits of quantum memory, the optimal testing tradeoff $Θ(n-k+1/\varepsilon)$ at infidelity $\varepsilon$. Finally, we show that this adaptive mechanism extends beyond exact stabilizer states: states of stabilizer nullity at most $r$, including states prepared by Clifford circuits with a bounded number of $T$ gates, can be learned using $O(n2^{r})$ single-copy measurements.

</details>


### [125] [Nonlocality without entanglement for multipartite quantum measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02032)
*Satyaki Manna,Debashis Saha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将“无纠缠的非局域性”从量子态推广到量子测量：给出联合可完美区分、但LOCC下不可完美区分的两组分积测量对，并揭示自适应优于非自适应、方向依赖及跨任意划分不可区分的层级结构，最后给出基础光学实现的数据隐藏方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态层面的“无纠缠的非局域性”已有研究，但对应的测量（量子过程）层面是否存在类似现象、其结构是否更简单、以及能否产生新的信息处理任务尚不清楚。作者希望建立测量的非局域性类比并刻画其LOCC区分能力。

Method: 构造两量子比特积测量对，比较联合访问与LOCC下的完美区分性；设计六组双分体qutrit积测量以对比自适应与非自适应LOCC协议；分析协议发起方对LOCC区分性的方向依赖；推广构造n量子比特积测量对，使其全局可完美区分但在任意双划分（即使允许两侧任意联合量子操作）下均不可完美区分；并用基础光学元件实现基于量子过程无纠缠非局域性的量子数据隐藏。

Result: 发现两量子比特积测量对在联合访问可完美区分而LOCC不可，构成测量版无纠缠非局域性；揭示LOCC区分策略层级，自适应可严格优于受限非自适应（六组双分体qutrit积测量示例）；LOCC可区分性本质上是方向性的，取决于哪一方发起协议；构造出全局可完美区分但任意双划分（含任意联合量子操作）均不可完美区分的n量子比特积测量对；实现基于量子过程非局域性的数据隐藏的光学方案。

Conclusion: 测量（量子过程）层面的无纠缠非局域性可在极简单设置下出现，并伴随自适应优势、方向依赖与强非局域性等层级特征，为量子数据隐藏等任务提供了可实验实现的新途径。

Abstract: We show that a pair of two-qubit product measurements can be perfectly distinguished when accessed jointly, yet cannot be perfectly distinguished when their local components are distributed between distant parties restricted to local quantum operations and classical communication (LOCC). This establishes a measurement analogue of nonlocality without entanglement that, unlike its state-based counterpart, emerges in a remarkably simple setting. We further uncover a hierarchy of LOCC discrimination strategies: adaptive protocols, in which the probe used at one site may depend on classical information obtained from another, can strictly outperform restricted non-adaptive protocols, as demonstrated by a set of six bipartite qutrit product measurements. We also show that LOCC distinguishability of bipartite measurements can be intrinsically directional, depending on which party initiates the protocol. More generally, we construct pairs of $n$-qubit product measurements that are globally perfectly distinguishable but cannot be perfectly distinguished across any bipartition, even when all parties on either side of the bipartition are allowed arbitrary joint quantum operations. Finally, we show how quantum data hiding based on quantum-process nonlocality without entanglement can be realized using elementary optical components.

</details>


### [126] [Complexity and Applications of Nearest Stabilizer Product State Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02037)
*Daniel Grier,Hakop Pashayan,Luke Schaeffer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究最邻近稳定子乘积态问题，给出其完整复杂性分类：在Clifford群对称性下共有9类单量子比特稳定子态集合S，除最简单的两类外均为NP完全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定子乘积态优化问题在量子计算多个领域具有应用价值，包括经典模拟算法的运行时间界、纠缠度量以及低秩矩阵补全复杂性，但此前缺乏对该问题系统性的复杂性刻画。

Method: 定义在n量子比特稳定子态|ψ⟩与单量子比特稳定子态集合S上的最大化问题：最大化|⟨ψ|φ_1,…,φ_n⟩|²，其中|φ_i⟩∈S；通过改变集合S并考虑Clifford群对称性进行复杂性分类分析。

Result: 在Clifford群对称性下共有9种不同的可能集合S；除最简单的两类外，其余均为NP完全。

Conclusion: 该问题可应用于经典模拟算法的更紧运行时间界、纠缠度量及低秩矩阵补全复杂性；并完成了最邻近稳定子乘积态问题的完整复杂性分类。

Abstract: Consider the following optimization problem over stabilizer product states: given an $n$-qubit stabilizer state $\left|ψ\right\rangle$ and a set of single-qubit stabilizer states $S$, maximize $\left|\langle ψ| φ_1, \ldots, φ_n \rangle\right|^2$ over single-qubit stabilizer states $\left|φ_i\right\rangle \in S$. By varying the set $S$, we show that solutions to this problem can be useful in a variety of settings: tighter runtime bounds for certain classical simulation algorithms; measures of entanglement; and the complexity of low-rank matrix completion. Moreover, we give a complete complexity classification of this nearest stabilizer product state problem. After accounting for the symmetries in the Clifford group, there are $9$ distinct possible sets $S$, and we show that all but the two simplest of these are $\NP$-complete.

</details>


### [127] [Fermionic Gaussianity can be tested with mode-independent sample-complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02050)
*Maxwell West,Martin Larocca*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的技术，用于对一般量子态族的2副本测试接受概率进行上界估计，并将其应用于费米子高斯态与Slater行列式态的判定问题，证明Bell采样是样本复杂度最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判定未知量子态是否属于某给定态族（或远离该族）是量子信息论中的自然问题。在这类问题中，通常存在一个自然的2副本测试，当态确实属于目标族时总是接受；然而，对远离该族的态的接受概率给出上界往往困难得多。本文旨在为一般态族发展一种简单的上界技术，从而上界判定问题本身的样本复杂度。

Method: 作者提出一种简单的通用新技术，用于对一般态族的2副本测试接受概率进行上界估计，进而给出判定问题样本复杂度的上界。具体应用于费米子高斯态判定问题：判定未知纯n模态是费米子高斯态，还是与所有高斯态在迹距离上至少ε远，所用的测试即著名的Bell采样过程。此外还将该框架应用于Slater行列式态族的类似判定问题。

Result: 作为框架的突出示例，作者证明判定未知纯n模态是否为费米子高斯态（或至少ε远离所有高斯态）可以用最优的Θ(ε^{-2})份副本完成。值得注意的是，分析表明著名的Bell采样过程用于检验费米子高斯性是样本复杂度最优的，即使与允许任意集体和自适应测量的协议相比也是如此。作为第二个例子，Slater行列式态族的类似判定问题也可以以与模数无关的样本复杂度求解。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个通用框架来上界一般态族2副本测试的接受概率，并由此上界判定问题的样本复杂度。该框架不仅证明了Bell采样在检验费米子高斯性方面达到最优，还表明Slater行列式态判定问题同样可用与模数无关的样本复杂度解决。

Abstract: Deciding whether an unknown quantum state either belongs to, or is far from, a given family of states is a natural question of quantum information theory. In many such cases, there is a natural 2-copy test which always accepts whenever the state indeed belongs to the target family; it is often much more difficult, however, to bound the probability of acceptance for states that are far from the family. Here we develop a simple new technique for upper bounding this probability for general families, and therefore upper bounding the sample-complexity of the decision problem itself. As a particularly striking example of this framework, we show that deciding whether an unknown pure $n$-mode state is fermionic Gaussian, or at least $\varepsilon$-far in trace distance from all Gaussian states, can be accomplished using the optimal number $Θ(\varepsilon^{-2})$ of copies. Remarkably, our analysis applies to the well-known Bell sampling procedure for testing fermionic Gaussianity, which we therefore show to be optimal, even compared to protocols making arbitrary collective and adaptive measurements. As a second example, we show that the analogous decision problem for the family of Slater determinant states can also be solved with mode-independent sample-complexity.

</details>


### [128] [Sequential Capacity of Quantum Processes with Finite Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02068)
*Yibin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出"序贯响应容量"以量化量子器件在固定内部记忆下随运行时长增长的响应复杂度，证明该容量随每轮时间步数K以K log K增长，且单量子比特含时相位旋转即可达到，而固定基测量的经典随机过程仅线性增长，并给出噪声下对数增益的容量上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定内部记忆的量子器件在运行更久时响应能有多复杂，尚缺乏可量化的容量刻画；同时需明确量子相对经典的优势来源及其在噪声下的存续条件。

Method: 定义序贯响应容量：统计能以规定响应概率间隔区分不同过程的自适应测试阶段数（每阶段一次全新运行）。对固定系统与记忆规模，建立容量与运行长度、概率分辨率之间的紧致关系；构造使用单个可见量子比特上的含时相位旋转、无额外内部记忆的方案，使测试响应概率恰为0或1；并对存储经典标签所选的相位序列，分析已知独立Pauli噪声对对数增强的影响，在理想控制与校正后弱残差相位噪声下证明匹配的容量界。

Result: 固定分辨率下容量随K按K log K量级增长，构造达到该增长；经典固定基逐次测量的随机过程在同测试下固定规模与分辨率仅线性容量；噪声分析表明以残差相位翻转概率的倒数为相干时间尺度，限制额外对数增长，并给出固定小概率间隔下的匹配上下界。

Conclusion: 序贯响应容量揭示了长时运行下量子响应复杂度的对数增强，可由极简量子资源实现，但该增强受残差相位噪声设定的相干时间尺度制约。

Abstract: How complex can the responses of a quantum device become as it runs longer with a fixed internal memory? We quantify this complexity through sequential response capacity: how many adaptive testing stages, each using a fresh run, can continue to separate possible processes by a prescribed gap in response probabilities. For fixed system and memory sizes, we establish a tight law relating this capacity to run length and probability resolution. At fixed resolution, the capacity grows on the order of $K\log K$, where $K$ is the number of time steps in each run. Our construction attains this growth using time-dependent phase rotations on a single visible qubit with no additional internal memory; its tests give response probabilities exactly zero or one. Under the same tests, classical stochastic processes that measure in a fixed basis at every step have only linear capacity at fixed sizes and resolution. For phase sequences selected by a stored classical label, we then quantify how known independent Pauli noise changes this logarithmic enhancement. With ideal controls and weak residual phase noise after correction, we prove matching capacity bounds at a fixed small probability gap. These bounds identify the inverse residual phase-flip probability as the coherence timescale that limits the extra logarithmic growth.

</details>


### [129] [Universal Bound and Phase Transition in Many-Body Fermionic Non-Gaussianity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02075)
*Masahiro Hoshino,Ryota Matsuda,Yuto Ashida*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 以magic Rényi熵(MRE)度量费米子非高斯性，推导其普适上界与Haar均值，证明典型Haar随机态的MRE密度趋近每个Majorana $\ln(4/3)$的上限，并揭示非高斯性增长中的一阶相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米子非高斯性是通用量子计算的资源，可由量子多体相互作用生成。但缺乏对MRE上界及其在典型态中行为的刻画，也不清楚非高斯性如何向最大密度增长，因此需要系统研究其度量、极限与动力学演化。

Method: 采用magic Rényi熵(MRE)作为非高斯性度量，推导其普适上界和Haar均值；比较具有相同协方差矩阵但MRE不同的态；在Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev哈密顿量下，以虚时和实时演化一个高斯态，并改变两类时间时长；MRE通过四个副本的置换对称性进行评价。

Result: 证明典型Haar随机态在大系统极限下达到最大MRE密度 $\ln(4/3)$ per Majorana；展示MRE可在协方差矩阵相同的态之间出现广泛差异；在SYK演化中发现由MRE密度拐点和四副本置换对称性自发破缺标记的一阶相变。

Conclusion: 该一阶相变代表态的非高斯性的定性变化，而这种变化并未反映在热力学自由能中，说明非高斯性作为量子计算资源具有独立于热力学性质的行为。

Abstract: Fermionic non-Gaussianity is a resource for universal quantum computation that can be generated by interactions in quantum many-body systems. Using the magic Rényi entropy (MRE) as a measure of non-Gaussianity, we derive its universal upper bound and Haar mean, proving that typical Haar-random states attain the maximal MRE density of $\ln(4/3)$ per Majorana in the large-system limit. We also show that the MRE can differ extensively between states with identical covariance matrices. To investigate how non-Gaussianity grows toward this maximal density, we evolve a Gaussian state in imaginary and real time under the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev Hamiltonian. By varying the imaginary- and real-time durations, we identify a first-order transition marked by a kink in the MRE density and spontaneous breaking of the permutation symmetry among the four copies used to evaluate the MRE. This transition represents a qualitative change in the non-Gaussianity of the state that is not reflected in the thermal free energy.

</details>


### [130] [A computational phase diagram for the transverse field Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02079)
*Thuy-Duong Vuong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究横场Ising模型的配分函数与Gibbs态观测量的经典算法可逼近性，给出以Δ(J)·tanh(βη)/η≤1为界的精确相变式复杂度刻画：该条件下存在多项式时间随机经典算法，越过该界则逼近问题NP难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体系统的配分函数与Gibbs态期望值一般难以经典计算，横场Ising模型是量子相变与量子计算复杂性的典型模型。作者希望确定在何种参数区间内配分函数可被高效经典逼近，以及何时逼近本质困难，从而刻画经典与量子优势的分界。

Method: 针对哈密顿量H=½∑J_ij Z_iZ_j+∑h_i^z Z_i+η∑X_i，以Δ(J)=λ_max(J)−λ_min(J)刻画相互作用谱宽。当Δ(J)·tanh(βη)/η≤1时，构造随机化经典算法逼近配分函数Z(β)=Tr(e^{−βH})至相对误差ε，复杂度为n、β、模型参数与ε^{−1}的多项式；并进一步给出逼近Gibbs态Pauli串观测量期望的随机经典算法，达到任意小加性误差，对X基对角观测量P∈{I,X}^{⊗n}可达任意小相对误差。当Δ(J)·tanh(βη)/η>1时，通过复杂性归约证明以exp(o(n))乘法因子逼近Z(β)为NP难。

Result: (1) Δ(J)·tanh(βη)/η≤1时，存在多项式时间随机经典算法以相对误差ε逼近配分函数；(2) Δ(J)·tanh(βη)/η>1时，exp(o(n))因子逼近为NP难，故在标准复杂性假设下不太可能存在高效经典或量子算法；(3) 可逼近条件下，Gibbs态Pauli串观测量可达任意小加性误差，X基对角观测量可达任意小相对误差。

Conclusion: 论文给出横场Ising模型经典可逼近性的阈值判据Δ(J)·tanh(βη)/η=1：该阈值以下配分函数与Gibbs态观测量存在高效随机经典算法，以上则逼近问题NP难，从而在算法与复杂性两侧建立了紧致对应，揭示了相互作用强度、温度与横场强度之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: We study the transverse field Ising model, defined by the Hamiltonian $H =\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i, j\in [n]} J_{ij} Z_i Z_j +\sum_{i=1}^n h_i^z Z_i + η\sum_{i} X_i$ where $J $ is the symmetric interaction matrix, and $η$ is the transverse field strength. Let $Δ(J)=λ_{\max}(J)-λ_{\min}(J)$ be the spectral width of $J.$ When the inverse temperature $β\geq0$ satisfies $Δ(J)\cdot\frac{\tanh(βη)}η\leq1$, we give a randomized classical algorithm that approximates the partition function $Z(β)=\operatorname{Tr}(e^{-βH})$ to a given relative error $ε\in(0,1)$ in time polynomial in $n$, $β$, the model parameters, and $ε^{-1}$. When $ Δ(J) \cdot \frac{\tanh(βη)}η > 1 ,$ we show that approximating $ Z(β)$ within an $\exp(o(n))$-multiplicative factor is $\textbf{NP}$-hard, and thus unlikely to admit an efficient classical or quantum algorithms under standard complexity theoretic assumptions. Furthermore, in the regime $Δ(J)\cdot \frac{\tanh(βη)}η\leq 1,$ we provide an efficient randomized classical algorithm that approximates Pauli string observables of the Gibbs state $ ρ_β= \frac{e^{-βH}}{\operatorname{Tr}(e^{-βH})}$ within an arbitrarily small additive error. In the special case when the observable is also diagonal in the $X$-basis, i.e. $P \in \{I, X\}^{\otimes n}$, the algorithm further achieves arbitrarily small relative error.

</details>


### [131] [Strong Simulation of 1D Quantum Circuits via Reduced Transition Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02082)
*Matilde Grassi,Stefano Carignano,Luca Tagliacozzo,Jacopo De Nardis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 Sweeping RTM 算法，用横向张量网络收缩近似强模拟一维混沌砖墙电路指定输出概率，数值发现所需键维随时间的增长呈亚指数而非指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯态直接演化受空间纠缠线性增长（体积律）严重限制，模拟混沌幺正电路的完整概率分布代价高昂；因此转向更聚焦的任务：以有限相对精度近似强模拟某个指定输出概率。

Method: 基于约化转移矩阵（RTM）的横向张量网络收缩算法——Sweeping RTM。给定输入、输出比特串 y 与 x，计算 p(x|y)=|⟨x|U(T)|y⟩|²，联合压缩左右两个时间边界态，并沿空间切割与键维扫描寻求收敛的输出概率。

Result: 对一维混沌砖墙电路，在固定相对目标精度下，数值证据显示获得稳定估计所需的键维数在可及时间窗口内随时间呈亚指数增长，而非指数增长。

Conclusion: 该工作为混沌量子电路的经典概率查询开辟了直接途径，潜在应用于基准测试与学习类任务。

Abstract: Tensor networks are powerful tools for simulating quantum many-body systems, but the growth of spatial entanglement severely limits the direct evolution of pure states under chaotic unitary circuits. Here we consider a more targeted task: the approximate strong simulation of a specified output probability of a 1D chaotic brick-wall circuit at finite relative precision. Given input and output bit strings $\by$ and $\bx$, we evaluate $p(\bx|\by)=|\bra{\bx}U(T)\ket{\by}|^2$ using the Sweeping RTM algorithm, a transverse tensor-network contraction based on reduced transition matrices (RTMs). The algorithm compresses the left and right temporal boundary states jointly, seeking an output probability that converges across spatial cuts and as the bond dimension is increased. For chaotic one-dimensional brick-wall circuits at a fixed relative target precision, we find numerical evidence that the bond dimension required to obtain stable estimates grows subexponentially over the accessible time window. Our findings open a direct route to classical probability queries for chaotic quantum circuits, with potential applications to benchmarking and learning tasks.

</details>


### [132] [Stationarity as a One-Mode Constraint on Quantum Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02090)
*Itai Panas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文论证定态时间演化的"单模性":在正规化Hartree-Fock框架下,通过单体排除公理,即使响应谱复杂,终端标量能量仍可被单模充分描述,氦原子给出最小非平凡验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定态量子态无论内部响应跨越多大Hilbert空间,只有一个动力学相位因子和一个标量能量。作者关注的不是绝对相位的数值或可观测性,而是定态时间演化的"单模性"(one-ness)。需要厘清微观谱复杂性与一维能量充分性之间的关系。

Method: 在正规化Hartree-Fock(reg-HF)中,先于任何单模约简建立与定态平均场的联系:由高斯HF表示诱导的原始积相对坐标高斯测度固定一个协方差,在该协方差处该分布同时是最小Fisher信息与最大Shannon微分熵分布,并取Stam界的等号情形。库仑算符允许兼容的高斯尺度分解,virial完备化将正规化对位移转换为定态兼容响应。定态性导出对终端标量关联响应的单模约束,其决定性假设为"单模排除公理":与保留对模正交的响应分量所携带的协方差被排除在终端标量能量性质之外,但不主张无限制响应是一维的。氦原子作为最小非平凡检验。

Result: 无参数保留库仑载体 -δ/e 与有限Z插入 8δ/9 紧密重现了独立的类氦系数与能量;显式Bethe-Goldstone计算显示宽广的非单分母回谱(return spectrum),但其标量能量恰好是矩可压缩的(moment-compressible)。

Conclusion: 结果将微观谱复杂性与单模能量充分性区分开来:定态能量的单模充分性可以在响应谱高度复杂的情况下成立,单模排除公理为此提供了明确的框架边界。

Abstract: A stationary quantum state has one dynamical phase factor and one scalar energy even when its internal response spans a large Hilbert space. Our argument concerns not the value or observability of an absolute phase, but the one-ness of stationary time evolution. In regularized Hartree--Fock (reg-HF), the connection to the stationary mean field is established before any one-mode reduction: the primitive-product relative-coordinate Gaussian measure induced by the Gaussian HF representation fixes a covariance and, at that covariance, is simultaneously the minimum-Fisher-information and maximum-Shannon-differential-entropy distribution and the equality case of the Stam bound. The Coulomb operator admits a compatible Gaussian scale resolution, and virial completion converts the resulting regularized pair displacement into a stationary-compatible response. Stationarity motivates a one-mode constraint on the terminal scalar correlation response. The decisive reg-HF hypothesis is a one-mode exclusion axiom: covariance carried by response components orthogonal to the retained pair mode is excluded from the terminal scalar energy property, without asserting that the unrestricted response is one dimensional. Helium provides the minimal nontrivial test. The parameter-free retained Coulomb carrier $-δ/e$ and finite-$Z$ insertion $8δ/9$ closely reproduce independent He-like coefficients and energies, while an explicit Bethe--Goldstone calculation displays a broad non-single-denominator return spectrum even though its scalar energy is exactly moment-compressible. The result separates microscopic spectral complexity from one-mode energetic sufficiency.

</details>


### [133] [One-Way Quantum Symmetric Private Information Retrieval Protocol From A Single Database Server Using NISQ Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02093)
*Xiang Zou,H. F. Chau*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于NISQ设备（无长期量子存储、到达即测量）的对称私有信息检索（SPIR）协议，在信息论安全性下实现单服务器SPIR，仅需BB84设备加一个被动线性光学测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单服务器对称私有信息检索（SPIR）在无限制量子能力下不可能实现。已有的有界/噪声量子存储模型限制了窃听者的存储，而本文希望在窃听者全能力的前提下，仅限制诚实服务器与用户为无长期量子存储的NISQ设备，从而实现更严格的存储限制下的可行SPIR。

Method: 服务器单向发送BB84态，用户进行无歧义态区分（USD），从而获知随机子集的比特。经联合随机置换分块后，用户公布其私下选定块的确定性位置，服务器对每块中这些位置进行哈希并取随机奇偶校验；置换使同一位置集合匹配两个块的概率指数级小。除BB84设备外仅增加一个带辅助模式的被动线性光学测量。安全性证明包括：对窃听者的保密性、通过公布集合的显式规模规则实现的数据库隐私、以及在服务器按规定制备BB84态并如实公布条件下抗服务器攻击的用户隐私，具体在单光子情形成立，容噪信道下仅差一个小型多光子项。

Result: 在1e4比特数据库、数据库隐私1e-6下，容噪信道中对执行规定测量的用户，约一千量子比特的块大小即足够。认证任意无记忆用户及承诺置换需要更大的辅助传输，文中用有限密钥进行了量化。数据库隐私可扩展到诱骗态弱相干脉冲，仿真显示该界近乎紧致。

Conclusion: 在NISQ设备（无长期量子存储、到达即测量）与全能力窃听者的设定下，单服务器SPIR可在信息论安全意义下实现，仅需在BB84基础上增加一个被动线性光学测量，并给出了具体的参数规模与安全性量化。

Abstract: Symmetric private information retrieval (SPIR) lets an inquirer obtain one specific bit only, without leaking any information about its index to the database owner. With a single server, this is impossible for parties with unrestricted quantum power. We show that it becomes possible, with information-theoretic security, when the server and the user hold only noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) devices without long-term quantum memory and measure each qubit on arrival, while the eavesdropper remains all-powerful; this is a stricter form of the memory restriction of the bounded- and noisy-quantum-storage models. The server sends BB84 states one way; the user discriminates them unambiguously and so knows a random subset of the bits. After a joint random permutation into blocks, the user announces the conclusive locations of one privately chosen block, and the server hashes and takes a random parity of these locations in every block; the permutation makes it exponentially unlikely that one set of locations fits two blocks. We prove secrecy against the eavesdropper, database privacy through an explicit size rule for the announced set, and user privacy against a server who prepares the prescribed BB84 states and announces truthfully, exactly for single photons and up to a small multi-photon term over a tolerated noise channel. The only addition to BB84 equipment is a passive linear-optical measurement with one ancillary mode. For a $10^{4}$-bit database and database privacy $10^{-6}$, blocks of about a thousand qubits suffice over a tolerated noise channel for a user who applies the prescribed measurement; certifying an arbitrary memoryless user and committing to the permutation need larger auxiliary transmissions, which we quantify with finite keys. Database privacy extends to decoy-state weak coherent pulses, and simulations show that the bound is nearly tight.

</details>


### [134] [Quantum state preparation for weighted d-DNNF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02094)
*Steef Hegeman,Joon Hyung Lee,Alfons Laarman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于由带权 d-DNNF（确定性、可分解伪布尔电路）描述的量子态，可在常数级复杂度算术意义下的线性时间内构造出制备该量子态的量子线路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态制备问题的目标是在给定量子态描述的情况下，高效生成计算该态的量子线路。已有方法对状态描述的利用效率有限，作者希望借助经典知识编译中的 d-DNNF 结构来提升制备效率。

Method: 将量子态表示为由带权 d-DNNF 电路描述的对象，利用 d-DNNF 的确定性与可分解性，构造相应的量子线路来制备该态，分析其时间复杂度（不计复数算术开销，即 up to complex arithmetic）。

Result: 对于带权 d-DNNF 描述的量子态，能够在关于描述规模的线性时间内得到制备该态的量子线路（在复数算术运算代价之外）。

Conclusion: 带权 d-DNNF 是一类可支持线性时间量子态制备的高效经典表示，将知识编译技术与量子态制备相连接。

Abstract: The quantum state preparation problem is to, given a description of a quantum state, efficiently generate a quantum circuit computing the state. We show that for quantum states described by weighted d-DNNF (deterministic, decomposable pseudo-Boolean circuits) a quantum circuit computing the state can be obtained in linear time up to complex arithmetic.

</details>


### [135] [A No-Go Theorem for Order-Two Clifford Electric-Magnetic Duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02097)
*Shunta Takahashi,Zhi Li,Beni Yoshida*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究环面码中电磁对偶的微观点实现：对 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 环面码证明任意保局域 Clifford 实现不可能具有二阶，对 $\mathbb{Z}_N$ 环面码则发现奇数 $N$ 存在二阶实现而偶数 $N$ 不可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁对偶在任意子类型层面是二阶的（Z2），并在编码量子信息上表现为 Hadamard 型逻辑变换。作者希望探究这一代数性质能否在微观层面被忠实地提升为二阶 Clifford 操作。此问题关乎涌现对称性的群律是否能在微观实现中保持。

Method: 对 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 环面码，通过一个完全一般的证明，不依赖平移对称性、不限制系统尺寸族、也不假定顶点与面稳定子之间的固定配对，论证电磁交换的任何保局域 Clifford 实现都不可能为二阶。几何上，证明通过局部模拟电弦与磁弦之间不可避免的交叉，类似于不可定向流形中的 cross-cap。随后将问题推广到 $\mathbb{Z}_N$ 环面码并进行相应分析。

Result: 对 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 环面码：任何保局域 Clifford 的电磁交换实现均非二阶（证明具有一般性）。对 $\mathbb{Z}_N$ 环面码：二阶 Clifford 实现当 $N$ 为奇数时存在，当 $N$ 为偶数时不可能。

Conclusion: 涌现电磁对偶的群律（在任意子层面为二阶）未必能忠实地提升到其微观 Clifford 实现。该结果揭示了涌现对称性的代数结构与其微观实现之间的不平凡差异，并给出了奇数与偶数 $N$ 情形下的完整判据。

Abstract: Electromagnetic duality in the toric code has order two at the level of anyon types and acts as a Hadamard-type logical transformation on the encoded quantum information. Here, we ask whether it can likewise be realized microscopically as an order-two Clifford operation. For the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ toric code, we prove that any locality-preserving Clifford realization of electric-magnetic exchange cannot have order two. Our proof is fully general and requires neither translation symmetry with restricted families of system sizes nor a fixed pairing between vertex and plaquette stabilizers. Geometrically, the proof locally emulates the unavoidable crossing between electric and magnetic strings, similar to a cross-cap in a non-orientable manifold. Extending the problem to the $\mathbb{Z}_N$ toric code, we find that an order-two Clifford realization exists for odd $N$, whereas it is impossible for even $N$. Our results show that the group law of an emergent electromagnetic duality need not lift faithfully to its microscopic Clifford realization.

</details>


### [136] [An exponential separation between entanglement-assisted and unassisted one-way quantum communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02099)
*Ryan Anselm,Srijita Kundu,Olivier Lalonde,Ashwin Nayak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在单向通信模型下，存在全布尔函数族在纠缠辅助时只需 O(log n) 比特经典通信，而无纠缠时需 Ω(n^{1/3}) 量子比特，从而首次给出全布尔函数的指数分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子通信复杂性中长期存在的问题是：是否存在某些任务在纠缠辅助下仅需极少通信，而在无纠缠时需大量量子通信。此前这类分离仅对关系型问题及同时消息传递模型下的部分函数已知，对全布尔函数是否成立尚未解决。
