Method: 构造一族全布尔函数 f_n:{0,1}^n×{0,1}^n→{0,1}，其基于子群成员问题（Aaronson、Le Gall、Russell 和 Tani 首次在通信场景中研究的问题）的特例。在单向通信设定下，分析纠缠辅助经典通信与无纠缠量子通信所需资源的下界与上界。

Result: 证明该函数族在带预共享纠缠时可用 O(log n) 比特单向经典通信计算，而在无纠缠时需 Ω(n^{1/3}) 量子比特单向量子通信，实现了指数级分离。

Conclusion: 首次对全布尔函数给出纠缠可大幅降低通信需求的指数分离，正面回答了该长期开放问题。

Abstract: A longstanding question in quantum communication complexity is whether some task can be accomplished with a small amount of communication in the presence of entanglement, yet require much more quantum communication in the absence of entanglement. Separations of this nature were previously known for relational problems and, in the simultaneous message passing model, for partial functions. But it has remained unresolved whether any such separation exists for a total Boolean function. We resolve this question with an exponential separation in the one-way setting: we exhibit a family of total Boolean functions $f_n\colon \{0,1\}^n \times \{0,1\}^n \to \{0,1\}$ that can be computed with $O(\log n)$ bits of one-way classical communication given prior entanglement, but that require $Ω(n^{1/3})$ qubits of one-way quantum communication without entanglement. Our function is a special case of the subgroup membership problem, first studied in the communication setting by Aaronson, Le Gall, Russell, and Tani.

</details>


### [137] [On the pseudorandomness of simple quantum processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02100)
*Jesko Dujmovic,Jonas Haferkamp,Alexander Poremba*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Gowers关于"局部随机可逆操作反复复合能否生成伪随机置换"猜想的幺正量子类比，证明统计矩匹配（unitary design）一般不能推出伪随机性，并给出两种分离结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gowers (1996) 猜想反复复合局部随机可逆操作可得到与随机置换不可区分的全局置换，该猜想长期未解。作者试图通过研究其幺正量子类比来为该猜想取得新进展，同时澄清unitary design与pseudorandom unitary之间的关系。

Method: 构造可高效采样的分布族：对任意固定t，给出一、二量子比特门上的分布{ν_n}，经T=O_t(n²log²n)步独立复合后得到n量子比特系综，其近似为误差exp(-Ω(log²n))的unitary t-design，但存在高效量子算法仅用O_t(log²n)次查询即可将其与随机区分。第二个结果给出在多项式有界矩层面unitary design与pseudorandom unitary之间更强的分离，但反例需高度结构化的系综。

Result: 第一，证伪了Hoory–Magen–Myers–Rackoff猜想（ICALP '04）的幺正类比；对每个固定t，构造出近似unitary t-design却被高效算法以O_t(log²n)查询区分的分布族。第二，在多项式有界矩上得到unitary design与pseudorandom unitary之间更强的分离，但依赖高度结构化系综。

Conclusion: 统计矩匹配（unitary design）一般不足以推出伪随机性；这提示用unitary design建模黑洞物理中的信息扰乱需谨慎，因为即使最大程度扰乱也可能存在高效实验可及的结构。作者据此提出在简单量子过程（如随机量子电路）中伪随机性如何产生的新猜想。

Abstract: Can simple processes appear highly complex? Gowers (Comb. Prob. Comp. '96) conjectured that repeatedly composing local random reversible operations can yield global permutations that are indistinguishable from random. In this work, we study the unitary quantum analog of this question, in an attempt to make new progress on this longstanding conjecture.
  Our first result shows that statistical moment matching in the form of unitary designs does not generically lead to pseudorandomness---even for the simplest quantum processes: for every fixed $t$, we give an efficiently samplable family $\{ν_n\}_n$ of distributions on one- and two-qubit gates such that, after $T=O_t(n^2\log^2 n)$ independent steps, the resulting $n$-qubit ensemble is an approximate unitary $t$-design with negligible error $\exp(-Ω(\log^2 n))$, yet an efficient quantum algorithm distinguishes it from random using only $O_t(\log^2 n)$ queries. This refutes the unitary analog of the Hoory--Magen--Myers--Rackoff conjecture (ICALP '04) for permutations.
  Our second result is a stronger separation between unitary designs and pseudorandom unitaries at polynomially bounded moments; our counterexample, however, requires highly structured ensembles, in contrast with the simple local walks from before. This suggests caution when using unitary designs to model information scrambling in black-hole physics, as even maximally scrambled systems can exhibit structure which is accessible to efficient experiments. Motivated by these findings, we then propose new conjectures for how pseudorandomness can plausibly emerge within simple quantum processes, such as random quantum circuits.

</details>


### [138] [Time-space lower bounds for breaking quantum cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02101)
*Fangqi Dong,Alex Lombardi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在随机预言机模型下证明了破解量子密码学的近最优时间-空间下界，通过将最优预处理攻击表示为随机矩阵的算子范数并结合压缩预言论，得到量子密码学相较经典密码学的新优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后量子单向函数的最佳下界为 O((T^2+ST)/N)，且在 S=N 时存在平凡攻击，无法体现量子密码学在抗预处理攻击上的优势；本文旨在建立量子情形下更强的时间-空间下界，揭示量子通信在安全性上的新优势。

Method: 将最优预处理攻击刻画为随机矩阵的算子范数，利用迹矩方法（trace-moment method）在随机预言机上对其期望进行上界估计，并借助压缩预言机（compressed oracles，Zhandry, Crypto 2019）为迹矩提供自然解释；该方法可视为对 Liu (Eurocrypt 2023) 方法的简化与推广。

Result: 证明 T 次查询、S 量子比特非均匀建议的敌手从 n 量子比特二元相位态 |ψ_k⟩ 恢复随机密钥 k 的概率至多为 O((T^2+√(ST))/N)；同时（1）收紧 Liu 对 QROM 中后量子 PRG 的分析，区分优势上界为 O(T^2/N + √(ST/N))；（2）将 LMW24 的单查询下界扩展至可额外进行一次任意函数查询及多项式次自适应随机预言机查询的敌手；（3）证明随机二元相位态对空间 S 区分者的伪随机性紧界 O(√S/N)。

Conclusion: n 量子比特通信即可在空间高达 N^2（而非 N）的预处理攻击下保持安全性，展示了量子密码学相对于经典密码学的新优势；所提迹矩与压缩预言机框架统一并强化了多项已有时间-空间权衡结果。

Abstract: We prove near-optimal time-space lower bounds for breaking quantum cryptography in the random oracle model. Specifically, we show that a $T$-query adversary with $S$ qubits of non-uniform advice can recover a random key $k$ from the $n$-qubit binary phase state $|ψ_k\rangle \propto \sum_{x} R(k,x) |x\rangle$ with probability at most $O(\frac{T^2 + \sqrt{ST}}{N})$ for $N=2^n$. In contrast, the best known bound for post-quantum one-way functions is $O(\frac{T^2 + ST}{N})$, with a trivial attack at $S = N$. This demonstrates a new advantage of quantum cryptography over classical cryptography: $n$ qubits of communication suffice for security against preprocessing attacks with space up to $N^2$ rather than $N$. Our methodology is simple: express the optimal preprocessing attack as the operator norm of a random matrix, and bound this value in expectation over the random oracle via the trace-moment method. These trace moments have a natural interpretation using compressed oracles [Zhandry, Crypto 2019], which we then analyze. This can be viewed as a simplification and generalization of the approach of Liu [Eurocrypt 2023] for proving time-space tradeoffs for breaking post-quantum cryptography. We also prove the following results: (1) We tighten Liu's analysis of post-quantum PRGs in QROM, achieving a distinguishing advantage bound of $O(\frac{T^2}N + \sqrt{\frac{ST}N})$. (2) For unitary synthesis, we extend the one-query lower bound of Lombardi-Ma-Wright [STOC 2024] to hold against adversaries that can make one arbitrary function query along with polynomially many (adaptive) queries to the random oracle, either before or after the function query. This also interprets the original LMW24 result in terms of compressed oracles. (3) Finally, we prove a tight $O(\frac{\sqrt{S}}N)$ bound for the pseudorandomness of random binary phase states against space $S$ distinguishers.

</details>


### [139] [Unitary Schur Sampling of Qudits via Random SWAP Tests: Hunt for Antisymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02103)
*Shrigyan Brahmachari,David Jakab,Henry D. Pfister,Iman Marvian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明任意置换不变混合态的酉Schur采样可仅用两量子比特SWAP测试（对称/反对称子空间投影）实现，误差ε的diamond距离下需O(n·min{n,d}³·log²(n/ε))次随机SWAP测试，核心思想是反复“提取最大反对称子系统”，等价于在Young图上随机游走；并应用于量子纯度放大，仅保留单个指定输出qudit即可获得最优纯化态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schur采样是从多体量子系统中提取置换不变信息的基本原语，在量子信息科学中应用广泛。然而现有基于电路的实现通常依赖相干表示论操作，如Clebsch-Gordan变换、广义相位估计或对称群上的量子Fourier变换，这些操作实现复杂、资源开销大。本文旨在寻找一种更简单、仅需两体投影测量的实现方式，以降低酉Schur采样的实现门槛。

Method: 提出仅使用成对SWAP测试的协议：对n个d维qudit上的任意置换不变混合态，通过对两qudit进行对称与反对称子空间的投影测量来模拟酉Schur采样。核心思想是反复识别并提取当前系统中最大的反对称子系统，将酉Schur采样转化为对“反对称性”的搜索，并赋予算法在Young图上随机遍历的自然解释。该协议在保持SU(d)不可约表示态的同时，在重数子系统中制备一个规范纯态。相比传统方法，避免了Clebsch-Gordan变换、广义相位估计与对称群量子Fourier变换等相干操作。

Result: 协议以O(n·min{n,d}³·log²(n/ε))次随机SWAP测试达到误差ε的diamond距离。作为应用，研究了量子纯度放大（QPA）：将多个含噪纯态副本组合以产生更高纯度的态，证明最优纯化态可通过仅保留单个指定输出qudit、舍弃其余qudit获得。

Conclusion: 酉Schur采样无需复杂的表示论相干操作，仅凭两体SWAP测试即可高效实现，且复杂度对n和d有良好标度；其“反对称搜索+Young图遍历”的视角提供了新的算法解释。该结果不仅简化了Schur采样的物理实现，还给出量子纯度放大中“单qudit保留即最优”的简洁结论，对量子信息处理中置换不变信息提取与纯化任务具有实用价值。

Abstract: Schur sampling is a fundamental primitive for extracting permutation-invariant information from many-body quantum systems and has broad applications in quantum information science. Circuit-based implementations typically rely on coherent representation-theoretic operations such as Clebsch-Gordan transforms, generalized phase estimation, or quantum Fourier transforms over the symmetric group. We show that unitary Schur sampling of arbitrary permutation-invariant mixed states on $n$ $d$-dimensional qudits can instead be implemented using only pairwise SWAP tests, namely, two-qudit projective measurements onto the symmetric and antisymmetric subspaces. Our protocol achieves error $ε$ in diamond distance using $O(n\min\{n,d\}^{3}\log^2(n/ε))$ random SWAP tests. The central idea is to repeatedly identify and extract the largest antisymmetric subsystem. This turns unitary Schur sampling into a search for antisymmetry and gives the algorithm a natural interpretation as a stochastic traversal of a Young diagram. The protocol preserves the $\mathrm{SU}(d)$-irrep state while preparing a canonical pure state in the multiplicity subsystem. As an application, we consider quantum purity amplification (QPA), in which multiple noisy copies of a pure quantum state are combined to produce a state of higher purity. We show that the optimal purified state can be obtained by retaining a single designated output qudit and discarding the rest.

</details>


### [140] [Quantum Advantage for Two-Party Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02113)
*Daniel Alabi,Emil T. Khabiboulline*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出信息论安全的量子双方汉明距离协议，在Klauck诚实、非抢占、消息保持模型下达到O(1)误差与O(n)通信，突破经典信息论下Ω(√n)或Ω(√n/log n)的误差下界，媲美经典计算安全精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典情况下，双方汉明距离估计在信息论安全下误差至少为Ω(√n)（纯差分隐私）或Ω(√n/log n)（强近似差分隐私），而O(1)误差仅在计算假设下可达。作者希望利用量子通信实现信息论层面的O(1)误差，并探究量子消息保持作为隐私资源的作用。

Method: 在Klauck诚实、非抢占、消息保持模型下构造O(n)通信的量子协议：核心为带守卫的相干往返（guarded coherent round trip）与等Gram刚性原则（equal-Gram rigidity），防止诚实方保留输入相关的互补信息；纯ε量子差分隐私(QDP)下期望误差≤2/sinh ε+γ；近似(ε,δ)QDP下用精确曲棍球棒散度计算获得更小误差。并与更弱的指定信道隐私及完全保留鲁棒安全进行区分。

Result: 纯ε QDP协议实现O(1)信息论误差，δ=o(1/n)时保持O(1)与Ω(√n/log n)的分离；近似(ε,δ)QDP误差严格更小；指定信道隐私已有精确经典实现，而完全保留鲁棒安全下仍可能遭受测量并中止攻击。

Conclusion: 量子通信可实现O(1)信息论误差，达到经典仅靠计算假设才能获得的精度；非正交量子消息的保持被识别为一种隐私资源。

Abstract: We introduce information-theoretically private quantum protocols for two-party Hamming distance when both parties must output the same estimate. Classically, for input length $n$, information-theoretic protocols require $Ω(\sqrt{n})$ error under pure differential privacy and $Ω(\sqrt{n}/\log n)$ error under strong approximate differential privacy, whereas computational security permits $O(1)$ error. In Klauck's honest, nonpreemptive, message-preserving model, we give an $O(n)$-communication quantum protocol with pure $\varepsilon$ quantum differential privacy (QDP) and expected error at most $\frac{2}{\sinh \varepsilon}+γ$, for every $γ>0$. For approximate $(\varepsilon, δ)$ QDP, an exact hockey-stick divergence calculation yields strictly smaller error, while preserving the $O(1)$-versus-$Ω(\sqrt{n}/\log n)$ separation for $δ=o(1/n)$. Thus, quantum communication achieves $O(1)$ information-theoretic error, matching the accuracy available classically only under computational assumptions.
  The main construction uses a guarded coherent round trip and an equal-Gram rigidity principle that prevents an honest player from retaining input-dependent complementary information. We separate this model from weaker prescribed-channel privacy, which already admits an exact classical realization, and from fully retention-robust security, against which measurement-and-abort attacks remain possible. Therefore, we identify preservation of non-orthogonal quantum messages as a resource for privacy.

</details>


### [141] [Local random quantum circuits converge to the Porter-Thomas distribution in polynomial depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02125)
*Aniruddha Sen,Nicholas Hunter-Jones*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明多项式深度 brickwork 随机电路的输出分布以逆多项式速率在全变差距离下收敛到 Porter-Thomas 分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机量子态及混沌量子多体系统的输出分布呈现 Porter-Thomas 统计特征。在随机电路采样和量子优势实验中，低深度随机电路输出被认为近似服从 Porter-Thomas，但此前缺乏严格收敛性证明。

Method: 针对由最近邻 Haar 随机门构成的局部随机量子电路，固定比特串输出概率分布，利用近似设计的矩界、特征函数的解析估计，以及逆矩的局部反集中性质进行证明。

Result: 对任意 m ≥ 0，深度为 O(n^{2m+1} log n) 的电路，其输出分布与 Porter-Thomas 分布的全变差距离至多为 O(1/n^m)，即收敛速率关于深度为逆多项式。

Conclusion: 结果首次严格建立了多项式深度 brickwork 随机电路输出分布在总变差距离下向 Porter-Thomas 分布的逆多项式收敛，为随机电路采样的相关假设提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Porter-Thomas statistics are a characteristic feature of the output distribution of random quantum states and, more broadly, chaotic quantum many-body systems. Convergence to Porter-Thomas plays a central role in random circuit sampling and experimental demonstrations of quantum advantage, where the output statistics of low-depth random quantum circuits are expected to be approximately Porter-Thomas, despite the absence of a rigorous proof of convergence. We show that the output distribution of polynomial-depth brickwork random circuits converges inverse-polynomially in total variation distance to the Porter-Thomas distribution. Specifically, consider the output probability distribution over a fixed bitstring of a local random quantum circuit, constructed from nearest-neighbor Haar random gates. Then, for any $m \geq 0$, the distribution corresponding to circuits of depth $O(n^{2m+1}\log(n))$ is at most $O(1/n^m)$ far in total variation distance from the Porter-Thomas distribution. Our proof uses moment bounds from approximate designs, analytic estimates for characteristic functions, and a local anticoncentration property for inverse moments.

</details>


### [142] [Spiking neural networks for streaming qubit readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02129)
*Barry M. Dillon,Aqib Javed,Jim Harkin,Patryk Dabkowski,Benjamin Lienhard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将脉冲神经网络（SNN）判别器用于超导量子比特读出，通过按时间分块流式处理测量窗口，在精度上超越匹配滤波并接近全轨迹人工神经网络，同时实现FPGA上的低延迟实时推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子处理器的反馈、校准和纠错需要快速且准确的量子比特态判定。在超导平台中，频率复用读出本质上是多变量问题：测量轨迹会编码串扰、弛豫事件及其他瞬态非理想因素，而传统匹配滤波无法充分刻画这些特征。因此需要一种能利用时间结构、低延迟且实时的判别方法。

Method: 引入SNN判别器处理超导量子比特读出。将测量窗口划分为连续时间块，网络随数据到达逐步更新分类分数，无需等待读出窗口结束，从而利用时序结构进行流式判别。此外采用量化感知训练与hls4ml综合，将模型部署到FPGA上。

Result: 脉冲网络的性能优于匹配滤波判别，并接近全轨迹人工神经网络的精度。SNN的关键优势在于提供随读出信号采集而演化的流式、时间分辨的量子态估计。经量化感知训练与hls4ml综合后，每次FPGA推理更新可在下一个读出时间块到达前完成。

Conclusion: 结果表明SNN是实现FPGA硬件上低延迟、实时量子比特读出的一条有前景的路径，并对时间关键的量子控制与科学推断应用具有更广泛的意义。

Abstract: Fast and accurate qubit-state assignment is essential for feedback, calibration, and error correction in quantum processors. In superconducting platforms, frequency-multiplexed readout makes this task intrinsically multivariate as measured traces can encode crosstalk, qubit-state relaxation events, and other transient nonidealities that are not fully captured by conventional matched filtering. Here, we introduce spiking neural network (SNN) discriminators for superconducting qubit readout. By processing the measurement window in successive time chunks, the networks exploit temporal structure and update classification scores as data arrive, rather than waiting until the end of the readout window. The spiking networks outperform matched-filter discrimination and approach the accuracy of a full-trace artificial neural network. Beyond reaching the performance of artificial neural networks, the key advantage of SNNs is that they provide a streaming, time-resolved estimate of the qubit state that evolves as the readout signal is acquired. Using quantisation-aware training and hls4ml synthesis, we further demonstrate that each FPGA inference update can be completed before the next readout chunk arrives. These results establish spiking neural networks as a promising route to low-latency, real-time qubit readout on FPGA hardware, with broader implications for time-critical quantum-control and scientific-inference applications.

</details>


### [143] [Quantum impurity models: easy at equilibrium, universal in motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02130)
*Srinivasan Arunachalam,Sergey Bravyi,Anirban Chowdhury,Arkopal Dutt,Alexandru Gheorghiu,Zhi Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子杂质模型的经典与量子计算复杂度，证明平衡态性质可经典高效近似，而时间演化是BQP完全的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子杂质模型描述小规模相互作用子系统嵌入自由费米子大浴中的物理系统，理解其基态能量、热平衡与动力学性质的计算复杂度，对凝聚态物理与量子多体理论具有重要意义。

Method: 利用按能量尺度和Krylov深度组织的基底下，多粒子浴激发的指数抑制性质，设计经典算法；并构造一个平稳量子处理器，其程序以自由传播费米子流的形式到达，以实现通用量子计算。

Result: (1) 基态能量可在poly(n,1/ε)时间内经典逼近至加性误差ε，改进了先前准多项式算法；(2) 逆温度β下，亥姆霍兹自由能与热场双态经典描述可在poly(n,β,1/ε)时间内计算；(3) 对时间无关且杂质尺寸恒定的H，模拟时间演化e^{-iHt}是BQP完全的。

Conclusion: 量子杂质模型的平衡态性质可被经典高效近似，而其时间演化却能实现通用量子计算，揭示了平衡与非平衡计算复杂度之间的鲜明对比。

Abstract: A quantum impurity model describes a small interacting subsystem embedded into a large bath of free fermions. Here we study the computational complexity of calculating the ground energy, thermal equilibrium, and dynamical properties of these models. Our work reveals a sharp contrast: equilibrium properties can be efficiently approximated by classical means, whereas time evolution can implement a universal quantum computation. More precisely, let $H$ be the Hamiltonian of an impurity model with $n$ fermionic modes and a constant-size impurity. We show that: (1) the ground energy of $H$ can be approximated to additive error $\varepsilon$ by a classical algorithm with runtime $\textsf{poly}(n,1/\varepsilon)$, improving on the quasi-polynomial runtime of the best previously known algorithm; (2) at inverse temperature $β$, the Helmholtz free energy and a classical description of the thermofield double state can be computed to precision $\varepsilon$ in time $\textsf{poly}(n,β,1/\varepsilon)$; (3) simulating the time evolution $e^{-iHt}$ is $\textsf{BQP}$-complete, for $H$ that is time-independent and has a fixed, constant impurity size. Our algorithms exploit exponential suppression of multi-particle bath excitations in a basis organized by energy scale and Krylov depth. Our universality construction realizes a stationary quantum processor whose program arrives in a stream of freely propagating fermions.

</details>


### [144] [Optimal transducers using symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02133)
*Benoît Dubus,Julien Ladeuze,Jérémie Roland*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用状态转换问题的对称群，简化了量子换能器（transducer）的构造：证明最优催化剂可取为协变形式，换能器可在同分异构分解下取块对角形式，从而系统性地导出若干量子算法原语的最优换能器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 换能器框架虽在量子查询复杂度中很强大，且其最优换能复杂度等于对手界（即 Las Vegas 复杂度），但构造高效乃至最优换能器仍需解对手 SDP 并显式构造酉算子，难度很大。作者希望借助对称性来简化这两步。

Method: 首先通过对称化论证证明：最优催化剂总可以选为在对称群表示下协变。其次证明换能器纠缠（intertwine）两个不同的群表示，因而可在 Hilbert 空间的同分异构分解（isotypic decomposition）中选为块对角。随后将这些表示论方法用于推导具体算法原语的最优换能器。

Result: 推导出多种广泛使用的量子算法原语的最优换能器，且常数最优，包括无结构搜索、振幅放大与振幅估计。该工作将以往用表示论计算对手下界的方法扩展到了最优算法的系统性构造。

Conclusion: 对称群方法同时简化了对手 SDP 的求解与酉算子的构造，为系统性地获得最优量子算法提供了新途径，是表示论在量子算法设计中的进一步应用。

Abstract: Transducers (Belovs, Jeffery and Yolcu, 2024) are a quantum computing framework describing a quantum algorithm as a unitary converting an input state into a target state using a catalyst, an auxiliary vector that is left unchanged. They are a powerful tool in quantum algorithm design, especially in the context of quantum query complexity: feasible points of the (dual) adversary semidefinite program directly translate into transducers and the optimal transduction complexity is equal to the adversary bound, i.e. the Las Vegas complexity, which is known to characterize bounded-error quantum query complexity. Moreover, contrary to bounded-error algorithms, transducers compose exactly, which limits overheads due to controlling errors in algorithms constructed by composition. Constructing efficient, let alone optimal, transducers in terms of quantum query complexity nevertheless remains a hard task since it still requires solving the adversary SDP and constructing the unitary to obtain an explicit algorithm. In this paper, we show how using the symmetry group of state-conversion problems simplifies both steps. First, using a symmetrization argument, we prove an optimal catalyst can always be chosen covariant under a representation of the symmetry group. Second, we prove that the transducer intertwines two different representations of the group and can thus be chosen block diagonal in the isotypic decomposition of the Hilbert space. Using those methods, we then derive optimal transducers, with optimal constants, for different widely used quantum algorithmic primitives, such as unstructured search, amplitude amplification and amplitude estimation. Our approach extends previous work on the use of representation theory to compute adversary lower bounds (Høyer, Lee, and {\v S}palek, 2007; Ambainis, Magnin, Roetteler and Roland, 2011) to the systematic construction of optimal algorithms.

</details>


### [145] [A Generalized quantum Stein lemma on von Neumann algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02134)
*Li Gao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在任意von Neumann代数上，针对凸且张量稳定的备择态族，证明了一个适用于i.i.d.正态态的广义量子Stein引理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典/量子Stein引理推广到更一般的算子系统（任意von Neumann代数）与更一般的备择假设族（凸、张量稳定），并覆盖正态态这一重要情形。

Method: 结合相对熵的hockey-stick散度积分表示、模检验（modular testing）界以及凸极小极大（convex minimax）论证。

Result: 在存在相对零态有限相对熵的备择态的前提下，对任意type-I错误容限ε∈(0,1)，最坏情形下的type-II错误指数由正则化相对熵达到，并具强逆（strong converse）。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了量子假设检验中Stein型渐近最优性的广泛刻画，将Stein引理拓展至von Neumann代数上的正态态与凸张量稳定族。

Abstract: We prove a generalized quantum Stein lemma for i.i.d. normal states against convex, tensor-stable families on arbitrary von Neumann algebras. Assuming the existence of an alternative state with finite relative entropy from the null state, we show that, at every type-I error tolerance $\varepsilon\in (0,1)$, the worst case type-II error exponent is achieved with the regularized relative entropy with a strong converse. The proof combines the integral representation of relative entropy by hockey-stick divergences, a modular testing bound, and convex minimax argument.

</details>


### [146] [Random Quantum Circuits Beyond Moment Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02135)
*Shih-Han Hung*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为强 $\varepsilon$-近似酉 $k$-设计在单个输出概率分布上的逼近精度给出定量保证：一般情况下每个输出概率分布与 Porter-Thomas 分布的 Kolmogorov 距离上界为 $O(\sqrt{2^n/(k(k+2^n))}+\varepsilon)$ 且最优至常数因子；在局部不变性下可指数改进至 $2^{-\Omega(k)}+O(\varepsilon)$，并给出总变差距离保证及其对输出香农熵的推论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机量子电路旨在高效复现理想随机量子演化的统计性质，构造近似酉设计是主要途径。已有工作多关注矩匹配本身，而设计在多大程度上能复现单个输出概率的完整分布尚缺乏定量刻画，本文正是要填补这一空白，并辨别矩匹配所保证的精度与需要何种额外结构才能显著提升。

Method: 对任意位于 $n$ 量子比特上的强 $\varepsilon$-近似酉 $k$-设计，用 Kolmogorov 距离度量每个输出概率的分布与有限维 Porter-Thomas 分布（参数为 $(1,2^n-1)$ 的 beta 分布）之间的偏差，得到上界 $O(\sqrt{2^n/(k(k+2^n))}+\varepsilon)$，并证明其在常数因子意义下最优；进一步引入在 $\log k+O(1)$ 个量子比特上酉平移作用下的不变性（局部不变性），在 Kolmogorov 与总变差两种度量下重新界定误差；最后将分布逼近结果用于输出分布香农熵的偏差估计。

Result: 一般情形下误差上界为 $O(\sqrt{2^n/(k(k+2^n))}+\varepsilon)$ 且最优（至常数因子），说明仅靠矩匹配难以再改进一致界；在局部不变性下误差改进至 $2^{-\Omega(k)}+O(\varepsilon)$（Kolmogorov 距离）与 $2^{-\Omega(k)}+O(\varepsilon\log(2/\varepsilon))$（总变差距离），在 $k$ 的依赖上获得指数级提升；应用方面，若强近似设计的每个输出概率边缘分布与 Haar 对应物的 Kolmogorov 距离不超过 $\eta$，则输出分布期望香农熵与 Haar 值的偏差为 $O(\sqrt{\eta})$。

Conclusion: 矩匹配（近似酉设计）对单个输出概率分布的刻画存在一项与 $k$ 相关的固有限制，且该限制在常数因子意义下不可改进；要获得指数级更优的分布逼近，需要额外的局部不变性结构。所得分布保证还可定量转移到输出分布期望香农熵的偏差上，为随机量子电路的统计分析提供理论依据。

Abstract: Random quantum circuits aim to efficiently reproduce the statistical properties of ideal random quantum evolution. One approach is to construct approximate unitary designs, which match the moments of Haar-random unitaries up to a prescribed order with controlled error. In this work, we establish quantitative guarantees for how accurately these designs reproduce the full distributions of individual output probabilities. We show that, for every strong $\varepsilon$-approximate unitary $k$-design on $n$ qubits, the distribution of each individual output probability is within $O\left(\sqrt{\frac{2^n}{k(k+2^n)}}+\varepsilon\right)$ in Kolmogorov distance of the finite-dimensional Porter-Thomas distribution, a beta distribution with parameters $1$ and $2^n-1$. This bound is optimal up to constant factors, implying that a substantially better uniform bound requires additional structure. We further show that local invariance yields an exponential improvement in the dependence on $k$, with guarantees also in the stronger metric of total variation: every strong $\varepsilon$-approximate unitary $k$-design invariant under unitary translations acting on $\log k+O(1)$ qubits achieves error $2^{-Ω(k)}+O(\varepsilon)$ in Kolmogorov distance and $2^{-Ω(k)}+O(\varepsilon\log(2/\varepsilon))$ in total variation distance. These results identify both the distributional accuracy guaranteed by moment matching and the additional structure that substantially improves it. As an application, we show that if the marginal distribution of each output probability of a strong approximate design is within Kolmogorov distance $η$ of its Haar counterpart, then the expected Shannon entropy of the output distribution differs from its Haar value by $O(\sqrtη)$.

</details>


### [147] [One-Shot any Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02137)
*Andrew C. Yuan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明任意单次测量码均为自纠错码，并给出将任意[[n,k]] CSS QLDPC码变换为具有单次量子纠错与高效局部解码器（O(log m)并行时间）的[[nm,k]] CSS QLDPC码的构造方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子低密度奇偶校验码虽具良好参数，但通常需要多轮测量才能纠错；作者希望构造兼具单次纠错能力、高效局部解码、阈值与误差抑制可控，并能提升已有单次解码码误差抑制的量子码。

Method: 首先给出证明：任意单次码均为自纠错码。随后构造一种变换，把任意[[n,k]] CSS QLDPC码扩展为[[nm,k]] CSS QLDPC码，该码具备单次量子纠错能力，并配有运行时间为O(log m)并行时间的局部解码器。

Result: 在联合局部随机物理噪声与测量噪声下，存在常数阈值p_RG>0；T轮纠错后的逻辑失败概率为O(Tn)exp[-Ω(m^α)]，其中α>0为常数。即阈值为p_RG，误差抑制为Ω(m^α)。若原码带阈值为p_c>0、误差抑制为Ω(n^β)的单次解码器，则输出码将误差抑制增强为Ω(m^α n^β)，并保持与解码器无关的阈值p_RG；当p_c<p_RG时称其为增强。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了单次码与自纠错码的联系，并提供了一种通用变换，使任意CSS QLDPC码获得单次纠错、高效局部解码、常数阈值及可控（可增强）误差抑制，可为构造实用量子纠错码提供框架。

Abstract: We first provide a simple proof showing that any single-shot code is self-correcting. We then show that any $[[n,k]]$ CSS quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) code can be transformed into an $[[nm,k]]$ CSS QLDPC code with single-shot (SS) quantum error correction and an efficient local decoder which runs in $O(\log m)$ parallel time. Below a constant threshold $p_{\rm RG}>0$ for joint local stochastic physical and measurement noise, the logical failure probability over $T$ correction rounds is bounded by \begin{equation*}
  O(T n)\exp[-Ω(m^α)] \end{equation*} for a constant $α>0$; we say that the \textit{threshold} is $p_{\rm RG}$ and \textit{error suppression} is $Ω(m^α)$. Moreover, if the input code is equipped with a single-shot decoder with threshold $p_c>0$ and error suppression $Ω(n^β)$, then the output code \textit{enhances} the error suppression to $Ω(m^α n^β)$, while maintaining the decoder-independent threshold $p_{\rm RG}$, and thus \textit{enhanced} if $p_c < p_{\rm RG}$.

</details>


### [148] [A provable quantum advantage for approximate optimization via decoded quantum interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02145)
*Maximilian J. Kramer,Elies Gil-Fuster,Benjamin D. M. Jones,Jens Eisert,Franz J. Schreiber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在oracle模型下证明了Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI)相对所有多项式时间经典算法的严格优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DQI是一种具有强性能保证、利用优化与编码理论对偶性的量子近似优化新范式，但其能否可证明地超越所有多项式时间经典算法尚不明确。

Method: 定义folded optimal polynomial intersection (folded OPI)优化任务，接受集随机选取并通过成员oracle访问；基于Jordan等人的DQI框架，将Yamakawa与Zhandry精确搜索oracle分离所用的经典下界方法推广到近似情形，并结合Sun与Wootters、Horinaga与Yamakawa、Jo的最新工作构造改进版DQI算法。

Result: 建立了任意多项式时间经典算法可达到的近似比与DQI算法近似比之间的严格鸿沟；改进版算法在folded OPI上取得更大差距。以码率0.3为例，DQI与改进算法的期望得分分别约为0.85和0.95，而经典算法要以常数概率超过0.65阈值需超多项式多次成员查询。

Conclusion: DQI在oracle设定下可证明优于所有多项式时间经典算法，改进版DQI进一步给出更强的量子分离。

Abstract: Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) is a novel paradigm for tackling approximate optimization problems on quantum computers. This framework comes with strong performance guarantees and exploits a well-established duality between optimization and coding theory. A central question, however, is whether DQI can actually provably outperform all polynomial-time classical algorithms. In this work, we establish such an advantage in an oracle setting: we consider an optimization task called folded optimal polynomial intersection (folded OPI), where the acceptance sets are chosen randomly and accessed through membership oracles. We establish a strict gap between the approximation ratio achievable by any polynomial-time classical algorithm and the approximation ratio achieved by the DQI algorithm. Our proof builds on Jordan et al.'s DQI framework for approximate optimization and extends the classical lower-bound method underlying Yamakawa and Zhandry's exact-search oracle separation to approximation. Building on recent developments by Sun and Wootters, Horinaga and Yamakawa, and Jo, we further show that a modified version of the DQI algorithm achieves a strictly larger gap on the folded OPI problem, yielding an even stronger quantum separation. As a concrete example, for code rate $0.3$, DQI and the modified algorithm achieve expected scores of approximately $0.85$ and $0.95$, respectively. In contrast, exceeding the classical threshold of $0.65$ by any fixed amount with constant probability on sampled instances requires super-polynomially many classical membership queries.

</details>


### [149] [Polynomial-time additive-error estimation of output probabilities for shallow quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02146)
*Matthew Coudron,Michael J. Gullans,Jon Nelson,Joel Rajakumar,Shi Jie Samuel Tan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个确定性经典算法，可在 poly(n,1/ε) 时间内将常数深度量子电路输出概率 |⟨x|U|0^n⟩|^2 估计到加性误差 ε，显著优于此前最优算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对常数深度量子电路输出概率的经典估计需要 n^{O(log n)} 时间，几何局部情形为 n^{O(log log n)}，二维几何局部电路为 n^{O(1)}。降低该任务的经典模拟复杂度是理解量子电路经典可模拟性的核心问题。

Method: 提出确定性经典算法，针对由有界扇入门构成、任意连接方式的常数深度量子电路 U，估计任意 n 比特输出串 x 的概率幅平方 |⟨x|U|0^n⟩|^2。

Result: 算法在 poly(n,1/ε) 时间内达到加性误差 ε，将任意连接常数深度电路的复杂度从 n^{O(log n)} 降至多项式级，同时改进几何局部电路的 n^{O(log log n)} 与二维局部电路的 n^{O(1)} 结果。

Conclusion: 常数深度、有界扇入、任意连接的量子电路其输出概率估计可被经典多项式时间算法高效完成，表明该类电路在加性误差意义下不具备经典计算优势。

Abstract: We give a deterministic classical algorithm that estimates $|\langle x|U|0^n\rangle|^2$ to additive error $\varepsilon$ in $\mathrm{poly}(n, 1/\varepsilon)$ time, where $U$ is a constant-depth quantum circuit comprised of gates with bounded fan-in and arbitrary connectivity, and $x$ is an arbitrary $n$-bit output string. This improves over prior state-of-the-art algorithms that takes $n^{O(log(n))}$ time for the same task, $n^{O(log(log(n))}$ when $U$ is geometrically local, and $n^{O(1)}$ for 2D geometrically-local circuits.

</details>


### [150] [The Robustness of QAC0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02154)
*Daniel Grier,Jackson Morris,Kewen Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究 QAC^0 在容错性与门集限制下的鲁棒性：证明其固有误差可被完全消除（可实现精确模拟），并证明 QAC^0 对单量子比特门限制具有鲁棒性（仅需广义 Toffoli、S 与 Hadamard 门即可近似实现）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QAC^0 通常允许非零误差，且默认可使用任意单量子比特门。作者希望检验这两个设定是否本质必要：误差是否是计算能力所必需的，任意单量子比特门的自由度是否是 QAC^0 计算能力的来源。

Method: 利用在多副本情形下对精确振幅放大（exact amplitude amplification）的新颖应用，消除并行 W-test 中固有的误差；并通过门集受限的电路构造，将任意 QAC^0 电路近似转换为仅含广义 Toffoli、S、Hadamard 门的 QAC^0 电路。

Result: 精确 QAC^0 能以多项式多个经典输入副本精确模拟 TC^0；对每个固定素数 p，精确 QAC^0、EQAC^0 可计算 AC^0[p] 之外的全布尔函数。此外，任意 QAC^0 电路均可由仅含广义 Toffoli、S、Hadamard 的 QAC^0 电路近似实现，且该近似电路可由原电路的经典描述高效构造。

Conclusion: QAC^0 的非零误差并非必要，且其计算能力不依赖于任意单量子比特门的使用；QAC^0 在误差容限与门集限制两方面均表现出鲁棒性。

Abstract: In this work we study the robustness of $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ with respect to error tolerance and modifications to its gate-set. First, we investigate whether the non-zero error typically allowed for $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ circuits computing Boolean functions is truly necessary. We show that the error inherent in the parallel $W$-test of \cite{grier_morris_wu} can be eliminated entirely via a novel application of exact amplitude amplification in the many-copies context. Consequently, we find that $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ can \textit{exactly} simulate $\mathsf{TC}^0$ with polynomially many copies of the classical input and that for every fixed prime $p$ exact $\mathsf{QAC}^0$, $\mathsf{EQAC}^0$, can compute total Boolean functions outside of $\mathsf{AC}^0[p]$.
  Second, we ask to what extent the computational power of $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ follows from the fact that arbitrary single-qubit gates may be used at any point in the circuit. We find that $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ is in fact robust to restrictions on which single-qubit gates are permitted: every $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ circuit can be approximately implemented by a $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ circuit consisting of just generalized Toffoli, $S$, and Hadamard gates. Moreover, this approximating circuit can be constructed efficiently from a classical description of the original circuit.

</details>


### [151] [Learning Many-Body Hamiltonians Using a Local Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02157)
*Suying Liu,Zitai Xu,Alexey V. Gorshkov,Xiaodi Wu,Yu-Xin Wang,Zhi-Yuan Wei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作证明：仅用单个可测量子比特，结合快速单比特控制与连通的参考骨架，即可在Heisenberg极限下学习N量子比特有界度两体哈密顿量的全部O(N)个独立参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有哈密顿量学习协议通常假设可以直接测量整个系统，这一要求在大规模量子设备中难以满足。因此需要研究在仅有局部测量接口时，能否高效、高精度地学习多体哈密顿量的众多参数。

Method: 利用快速单比特控制构建连通的参考骨架；通过量子信号处理合成的鲁棒SWAP门实现相干态转移，将远处哈密顿量参数下演化的量子态传送到唯一可测量子比特上，且无需对中间链路的哈密顿量参数进行预校准；并采用并行学习架构。

Result: 对N量子比特链，总查询时间为Õ(N)，同时保持Heisenberg极限的精度标度；这些标度在对数因子内达到基本精度与信息传播下界。框架可推广至任意有界度相互作用图。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仅通过局部测量接口即可学习大量多体哈密顿量参数，为可扩展的哈密顿量学习提供了新途径。

Abstract: Hamiltonian learning provides a systematic framework for reconstructing unknown quantum dynamics. However, existing protocols typically assume direct measurement access to the entire system. With fast single-qubit control and a connected reference backbone, we show that a single measurable qubit suffices to learn all $O(N)$ independent parameters of a bounded-degree two-body Hamiltonian on $N$ qubits at the Heisenberg limit. Crucially, our protocol uses robust SWAP gates synthesized by quantum signal processing, enabling coherent transfer of states evolving under distant Hamiltonian parameters to the measurable qubit. This transfer requires no prior calibration of the Hamiltonian parameters of the intermediate links. A parallel learning architecture achieves total query time $\widetilde{O}(N)$ for an $N$-qubit chain, while retaining Heisenberg-limited precision scaling. On the chain, these scalings match the fundamental precision and information-propagation lower bounds up to logarithmic factors. The framework further extends to arbitrary bounded-degree interaction graphs. Our results establish a scalable route to learning an extensive number of many-body Hamiltonian parameters through only a local measurement interface.

</details>


### [152] [Beyond Light Cones: State Preparation Complexity in Quantum Spin Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02166)
*Omar Al-Ghattas,David Gamarnik,Bobak T Kiani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文引入"有效轮廓复杂度"（基于Pauli轮廓的度量熵）来刻画稠密量子p-spin哈密顿量的态制备复杂度，突破了仅依赖光锥的界，并据此证明近基态能量需Ω(n²/log n)个门、给出深度-宽度权衡及魔术层级障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有态制备复杂度界多基于电路光锥，对稠密p-spin哈密顿量不够紧；深层/受限度电路（如Clifford可扩散可观测量的情形）无法直接用小光锥界处理，需要新的复杂度度量。

Method: 以Pauli轮廓（恰好支撑在p个量子比特上的Pauli算符期望）的度量熵定义"有效轮廓复杂度"；结合Berta等人的非对称量子de Finetti定理与高斯过程熵界，将轮廓复杂度与基态能量分离度、基准类比较联系起来。

Result: 一致有界二次有效轮廓复杂度的类与基态能量相差正比于√n；次二次复杂度类无法在领头阶超越积态基准。达到近基态能量需Ω(n²/log n)个单/双量子比特门（允许可丢弃辅助比特）；并得到深度-宽度权衡、纠缠深度与矩阵积态键维下界、以及魔术层级各固定层级的双向障碍（总宽度O(n)）；Clifford+T电路若有o(n)个T门则对积稳定子态无领头阶能量优势。

Conclusion: 有效轮廓复杂度是研究稠密哈密顿量态制备复杂度的有效工具，能统一导出门数下界、深度-宽度权衡、纠缠与张量网络下界及魔术层级障碍，并涵盖允许任意辅助比特和Clifford扩展的场景。

Abstract: We introduce a method for studying state preparation complexity in dense quantum $p$-spin Hamiltonians on $n$ qubits, going beyond bounds based only on circuit lightcones. The key input is the class's effective profile complexity, which is derived from the metric entropy of its Pauli profiles. These profiles record expectations of all Pauli operators supported on exactly $p$ qubits. Classes with uniformly bounded quadratic effective profile complexity remain separated from the ground-state energy by a positive multiple of $\sqrt n$ for sufficiently large fixed $p$. At subquadratic effective profile complexity, the class cannot outperform a suitable benchmark class at leading order, with product states providing a universal benchmark. The proof combines an adaptation of a nonsymmetric quantum de Finetti theorem of Berta et al. (arXiv:1810.12197) with Gaussian process entropy bounds.
  Applying this framework, we show that attaining near-ground-state energy requires $Ω(n^2/\log n)$ one- and two-qubit gates, even with arbitrary discardable ancillas. We also obtain depth-width tradeoffs, entanglement-depth and matrix product state bond-dimension lower bounds, and obstructions for both orientations at every fixed level of Parham's magic hierarchy (arXiv:2504.19966), with total circuit width $O(n)$. In first-level reverse magic, a shallow circuit is followed by an unrestricted Clifford circuit. The latter can spread local observables across the system, preventing a direct application of small-lightcone bounds. For this first-level class, our bounds also allow arbitrarily many clean ancillas at fixed shallow-circuit depth. A sharper benchmark shows that Clifford+$T$ circuits with $o(n)$ $T$-gates have no leading-order energy advantage over product stabilizer states, even with unrestricted Clifford operations and arbitrary discardable ancillas.

</details>


### [153] [Polynomial-time classical and quantum simulation of quantum impurity models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02167)
*Jiaqing Jiang,Nathan Ju,Ojas Parekh,Chaithanya Rayudu,Andrew Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究量子杂质模型的计算复杂性，证明其静态性质可在经典计算机上多项式时间高效模拟，而其动力学性质（如非平衡格林函数）在经典上困难、在量子计算机上容易，从而刻画了该问题的经典与量子可处理性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子杂质模型既是相互作用量子物质的重要范例，也是现代电子结构方法的核心计算原语，描述与无相互作用大热库耦合的小型相互作用费米子子系统。明确其经典与量子可处理性的边界，对于理解何时需要量子优势、何时经典算法已足够具有重要意义。

Method: 作者对量子杂质模型模拟的计算复杂性进行系统性研究：一方面设计经典算法，用于估算基态能量与配分函数；另一方面通过复杂性论证，分析动力学性质（如非平衡格林函数）的经典与量子计算难度。

Result: (1) 经典算法可在 poly(n, δ^{-1}) 时间内以加性精度 δ 估算基态能量；(2) 可在 poly(n, β, δ^{-1}) 时间内以相对精度 δ 估算逆温度 β 下的配分函数（n 为系统规模）。这将基态能量估计的最优复杂度从拟多项式改进为多项式，并首次为热平衡模拟提供严格多项式时间保证。相反，计算非平衡格林函数即使在有限温度下也具备完整量子计算能力，经典困难而量子容易。

Conclusion: 静态性质的模拟不存在超多项式量子加速，而平衡外（非平衡）杂质物理的模拟则为量子优势提供了途径。

Abstract: Quantum impurity models are paradigmatic models of interacting quantum matter, as well as key computational primitives for modern electronic-structure methods. They describe a small subsystem of interacting fermions coupled to a large, noninteracting bath. We perform a comprehensive study of the computational complexity of simulating impurity models, delineating the boundary between classical and quantum tractability for this class of problems. Our main finding is that static properties of quantum impurity models can be calculated efficiently on a classical computer. Specifically, we give classical algorithms that (1) estimate the ground-state energy to additive precision $δ$ in time $\mathrm{poly}(n,δ^{-1})$, and (2) estimate the partition function at inverse temperature $β$ to relative precision $δ$ in time $\mathrm{poly}(n,β,δ^{-1})$, where $n$ is the system size. These results improve the previous best-known complexity for ground-state energy estimation from quasipolynomial to polynomial time, while establishing for the first time rigorous polynomial-time guarantees for simulating impurity models in thermal equilibrium. On the other hand, we find that simulating dynamical properties of impurity models is hard for classical computers but easy on a quantum computer. As a canonical example, we show that computing their nonequilibrium Green's functions captures the full power of quantum computation, even at finite temperature. Taken together, our results rule out superpolynomial quantum speedups for computing static properties, but provide an avenue for quantum advantage in simulating impurity physics out of equilibrium.

</details>


### [154] [Robust exponential lower bounds for fermionic and bosonic Gaussian ranks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02172)
*Fuchuan Wei,Kong-Wing Wu,Zhengwei Liu,Zi-Wen Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在玻色与费米系统中，任何纯非高斯态的近似边界高斯秩随张量幂次至少指数增长，从而表明非高斯性普遍导致指数级高斯分解复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典模拟能力与量子计算优势密切相关。基于将态相干分解为经典可处理"自由态"的模拟范式中，分解秩决定模拟复杂度；但对该秩证明强下界极其困难，典型如量子比特稳定子秩问题。本文旨在研究玻色与费米系统的高斯版本，建立高斯秩的稳健指数下界。

Method: 证明结合两类技术：对费米子，采用约化到四模的方法并配合Majorana谱界；对玻色子，采用高斯后选择与基于熵的秩界。均针对有限模上的纯非高斯态（费米情形要求确定宇称）。

Result: 证明对任意有限模上的纯非高斯态，其张量幂的近似边界高斯秩在任意固定且小于1的范数误差下至少指数增长。给出具体实例：四模费米GHZ态与玻色单光子态的显式指数下界；玻色结果无需对目标态能量作任何假设。

Conclusion: 非高斯性普遍意味着指数级高斯分解复杂度，为基于分解的玻色与费米系统经典模拟设定了根本性限制。

Abstract: The power and limitations of classical simulation are central to understanding quantum computational advantages. A leading simulation paradigm is based on coherent decomposition into classically tractable free states where the decomposition rank determines the simulation complexity. Proving strong lower bounds on this number is a notoriously difficult and mathematically rich problem, as exemplified by the qubit stabilizer rank problem. Here, we study the Gaussian version of this problem in both bosonic and fermionic systems and establish robust exponential lower bounds on Gaussian rank. In particular, we prove that for every pure non-Gaussian state on finitely many modes, with definite parity in the fermionic case, the approximate border Gaussian rank of its tensor powers grows at least exponentially at any fixed norm error below one. Our proofs combine reduction to four modes with Majorana spectral bounds for fermions, and Gaussian postselection with an entropy-based rank bound for bosons. As concrete examples, we derive explicit exponential lower bounds for the four-mode fermionic GHZ state and the bosonic single-photon state. The bosonic results require no assumption on the energy of the target state. Our results show that non-Gaussianity universally entails exponential Gaussian decomposition complexity, setting fundamental limitations on decomposition-based classical simulation of bosonic and fermionic systems.

</details>


### [155] [From Permutation Symmetry to Communication Bounds and Additivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02176)
*Zahra Baghali Khanian,Debbie Leung,Graeme Smith*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明置换不变性限制了信道关联带来的量子通信增益：优化相干信息每使用量收敛于单次使用最大值，并在有限块长处取到上确界，从而将渐近优化归结为单次信道使用；同时给出纯源纠缠生成的强逆定理，并在统一变分框架下揭示完全有界范数与sandwiched Rényi信道量的共同结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨信道使用的关联可提升量子通信速率，因此需在任意大块上做优化，这成为确定量子容量的核心困难。作者希望借助置换不变性刻画这一优势的上限，并统一若干已知的可乘性与可加性结果。

Method: 利用完全置换不变性与De Finetti约化、置换协变性，构造共享变分框架；分析优化后的每使用相干信息随块长的行为，并考察纯源纠缠生成中置换不变输入下的保真度；同时对完全有界范数与sandwiched Rényi信道量给出替代证明。

Result: 优化相干信息每使用量收敛到单次使用最大值，并在有限块长处达到其上确界，故有限块超可加性仍可能存在，而渐近优化退化为单次信道使用；对置换不变输入的纯源纠缠生成建立强逆定理：速率高于单次相干信息最大值时保真度趋于零；在统一变分框架下重新导出完全有界范数与sandwiched Rényi信道量的可乘性与可加性；置换不变输入下输出熵每使用量不能低于单次最小值，该熵极限对Rényi阶数大于1及von Neumann熵均成立。

Conclusion: 对称性（置换不变性）将通信界与输出熵控制统一起来：渐近最优量子通信可归结为单次信道使用，有限块超可加性仍可能；同时为纠缠生成提供强逆定理，并统一解释了相关范数与Rényi信道量的已知结果。

Abstract: Correlations across channel uses can improve quantum communication rates, making optimization over arbitrarily large blocks a central difficulty in determining quantum capacity. We show how full permutation invariance limits this advantage for every finite-dimensional memoryless channel. The optimized coherent information per use converges to the single-use maximum and consequently attains its supremum at a finite block length. Thus finite-block superadditivity remains possible, while the asymptotic optimization reduces to a single channel use. We also establish a strong converse for pure-source entanglement generation with permutation-invariant inputs: at any fixed rate above the single-use coherent-information maximum, the fidelity tends to zero. Beyond these communication bounds, we identify a common structure underlying completely bounded norms and the sandwiched Rényi channel quantities governing discrimination against a replacer and entanglement-assisted communication. De Finetti reduction and permutation covariance yield systematic alternative proofs of their known multiplicativity and additivity results within a shared variational framework. We further show that, for permutation-invariant inputs, correlations cannot sustain an asymptotic reduction in output entropy per use below the single-use minimum. This entropy limit holds for Rényi orders greater than one and for the von Neumann entropy, extending the role of symmetry from communication bounds to the control of output entropy.

</details>


### [156] [Learning SYK Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02178)
*Anurag Anshu,Srinivasan Arunachalam,Sitan Chen,Yeongwoo Hwang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究从Gibbs态副本中学习稠密SYK哈密顿量的问题，突破现有算法依赖几何局域性或有限相互作用度的限制，利用模型的随机平均场结构，实现在恒定温度下多项式样本量、逆多项式精度学习整个哈密顿量；并在逆温度足够小时给出准多项式时间学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有哈密顿量学习算法通常依赖几何局域性或相互作用度有界，而SYK模型中每个四体相互作用与Θ(n^3)个其他项重叠，故这些方法均不适用，因此需要新的方法克服该障碍。

Method: 利用SYK模型的随机平均场结构，而非几何局域性或有界相互作用度，来学习稠密哈密顿量；分别构造基于Gibbs态副本的样本高效算法，以及在逆温度为足够小常数时的准多项式时间学习算法。

Result: 在任意恒定温度下，以高概率（对SYK耦合随机性）可用多项式数量的样本将整个哈密顿量学习到逆多项式精度；当逆温度限制为足够小的常数时，构造出与样本高效算法性质不同的准多项式时间学习算法。

Conclusion: SYK哈密顿量的稠密性与高重叠障碍可通过随机平均场结构被克服，从而在恒定温度下实现多项式样本的高精度学习；在低温限制下还可获得准多项式时间算法，表明样本效率与计算效率之间存在不同的算法路径。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning the dense Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev (SYK) Hamiltonian from copies of its Gibbs state. Existing algorithms for Hamiltonian learning typically rely on geometric locality or bounded interaction degree and therefore do not apply to SYK, where each quartic interaction overlaps with $Θ(n^3)$ others. We show that this obstruction can be overcome by exploiting the random mean-field structure of the model. At any constant temperature, we prove that with high probability over the SYK couplings, the entire Hamiltonian can be learned to inverse-polynomial accuracy using polynomially many samples. Furthermore, when the inverse temperature is restricted to be a sufficiently small constant, we construct a quasipolynomial-time learning algorithm which is qualitatively different from the sample-efficient algorithm.

</details>


### [157] [Single-Particle Spectral Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02183)
*Adrian Chapman,Charles Derby,Steven T. Flammia,Yeongwoo Hwang,Joel Klassen,Calum McCartney*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究全局酉变换遮蔽下的哈密顿量学习，证明恢复非相互作用模式的权重是DQC1-hard，并提出SPICES算法高效恢复谱分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料建模中（如平均场理论、格林函数、密度泛函理论）常需获取哈密顿量的准粒子谱，但当非相互作用结构被未知全局酉U遮蔽时，如何在不重构U的情况下恢复权重E_i成为一个有实际意义的问题。

Method: 论文首先对该问题给出计算复杂度刻画，证明其为DQC1-hard；随后提出SPICES（single-particle inference via contour estimators），将Hadamard测试与新颖的经典后处理相结合，利用单粒子能量与配分函数零点在复逆温度下的傅里叶频率之间的对应关系。

Result: 证明了该学习问题在非相互作用结构存在的情况下仍为DQC1-hard；SPICES在若干自然假设下能以Wasserstein距离高效恢复谱分布。

Conclusion: 由于准粒子谱在后续材料计算中普遍出现，且SPICES 简单实用，有望成为材料模拟中量子应用的有前景路径。

Abstract: We study Hamiltonian learning in a novel setting, where we are promised a physical structure that is obfuscated by a global unitary. In particular, we consider the task of learning the weights $E_i$ of a Hamiltonian $H=U^{\dagger}(\sum_{i \in I} E_i n_i + H_O)U$ comprised of a set of non-interacting modes $I$ and an arbitrary ``spectator'' $H_O$ acting on a disjoint set of modes $O$. The non-interacting structure is hidden by an unknown global unitary $U$, and our goal is to recover the $E_i$ without reconstructing $U$. Such Hamiltonians are well-motivated in the setting of materials modeling, where they find application for classical methods such as mean-field theory, Green's functions, and density functional theory. We show that this problem is DQC1-hard, despite the apparent simplification given by the non-interacting structure. We then introduce SPICES (single-particle inference via contour estimators), which combines a Hadamard test with a novel classical postprocessing procedure to efficiently recover the spectral distribution in Wasserstein distance under certain natural assumptions. The method exploits the correspondence between single-particle energies of the individual quasiparticles and the Fourier frequencies governing zeros of the partition function at complex inverse temperature. As quasiparticle spectra feature ubiquitously in downstream materials calculations, and with SPICES being simple and practical to execute, we expect that SPICES is a promising route to quantum applications in materials simulation.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Tunable flat bands and their signatures in electronic specific heat of an Aharonov-Bohm triangular quantum network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00273)
*Sanchita Nandi,Santanu K. Maiti*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了由三角环构成的紧束缚量子网络的热响应，推导出平带出现的解析条件，并证明电子比热可作为识别平带及其AB磁通调控的有效手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由环状结构组成的量子网络在电子输运方面已被广泛研究，但其热响应相对缺乏探索，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建由有限个三角plaquette通过单键连接、每个三角环贯穿Aharonov-Bohm磁通的紧束缚量子网络模型，分析非均匀配位、跳跃不对称性与量子干涉的相互作用，推导能量分支与电子动量无关的解析条件，并通过电子比热研究其热响应。

Result: 获得了平带出现的解析条件；发现AB磁通可直接调控平带位置；证明平带位置在电子比热中表现为明显特征，从而建立起平带形成与热响应之间的直接联系。

Conclusion: 电子比热可作为识别动量无关能级（平带）的替代途径，且AB磁通可选择性调控电子比热；该方法可推广至其他支持平带的简单与复杂量子网络。

Abstract: Quantum networks composed of loop-like structures provide a rich platform for exploring electronic transport phenomena and have been widely studied in various contexts. However, their thermal response remains relatively unexplored, motivating us to investigate it in the present work. We consider a tight-binding (TB) quantum network composed of a finite number of triangular plaquettes, where neighboring plaquettes are connected through single bonds and each triangular loop is threaded by an Aharonov-Bohm (AB) flux $φ$. The interplay of nonuniform site coordination, hopping asymmetry, and quantum interference gives rise to both dispersive and completely flat energy levels. We derive an analytical condition involving the TB parameters under which one of the energy branches becomes independent of the electronic momentum. We further show that the AB flux $φ$ provides a direct means of tuning the position of the flat band. The characteristic features of the energy spectrum associated with this quantum geometry are reflected in the thermal response, which we investigate through the electronic specific heat (ESH). In particular, we demonstrate that the position of the flat band manifests itself as a distinct feature in the ESH, establishing a direct connection between the flat-band formation and the thermal response of the system. Thus, the ESH provides an alternative route for identifying the presence of a flat band, consistent with the analytical condition and the corresponding energy dispersion. Moreover, the AB flux allows the ESH to be selectively regulated through the tuning of the flat-band position. The proposed approach provides a simple complementary means of identifying momentum-independent energy levels and may be extended to other simple and complex quantum networks supporting flat bands.

</details>


### [159] [Interior contacts in a narrow quantum Hall bar: a two-dimensional self-consistent screening calculation of the current distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00326)
*A. Siddiki*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文对带内部接触的六端GaAs/AlGaAs霍尔条进行全二维自洽计算，揭示ν=2平台下内部接触经历电连接、浮空、隔离三种区制及其转变机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Kendirlik等的内部接触实验，需要在屏蔽理论框架下完整理解内部接触在整数量子霍尔平台中的电学行为及其区制转变的物理起源。

Method: 在整数量子霍尔效应的屏蔽理论下，于整个器件平面（含探针臂与内部接触周围耗尽区）求解Thomas-Fermi-Poisson方程，得到电子密度、局域填充因子与电流分布；电流由填充因子相关的电导张量经局域欧姆定律给出，分别考虑源漏激发与内部接触激发。

Result: 在ν=2平台上，随磁场降低，内部接触依次经历三种区制：在可压缩体态中与电流路径电连接、在全不可压缩体态的霍尔势中浮空、以及被包围每个接触的闭合不可压缩环与体态和边条隔离。浮空与隔离区制下内部接触间两端电阻升高数个量级；隔离区间位于霍尔平台内且在其低场边缘前结束，其高场端由不可压缩体态消失锐利决定，低场端随温度和朗道能级展宽而收缩；内部接触与周边探针间电阻趋近于量子化霍尔电阻的一半。

Conclusion: 不可压缩区拓扑与隔离区间边界对局域电导正则化、网格及中等无序不敏感，而隔离电阻的量级则不然。

Abstract: We present fully two-dimensional self-consistent calculations of the electron density, local filling factor and current distribution in a six-terminal GaAs/AlGaAs Hall bar of width W = 3 micrometers with two additional square contacts, A and B, placed on the channel axis and connected to the outside by air bridges, following the interior-contact experiment of Kendirlik et al. [Nat. Commun. 8, 14082 (2017)]. Within the screening theory of the integer quantized Hall effect, the Thomas-Fermi-Poisson equations are solved on the full device plane, including the probe arms and the depletion regions around the interior contacts, and the current is obtained from a local Ohm's law with a filling-factor dependent conductivity tensor, for source-drain and interior-contact excitation separately. Across the nu = 2 plateau the interior contacts pass through three regimes as the field is lowered: galvanically connected to the current path in a compressible bulk, floating in the Hall potential of a fully incompressible bulk, and isolated from both the bulk and the edge strips by closed incompressible rings encircling each contact. In the floating and isolated regimes the two-terminal resistance between the interior contacts rises by orders of magnitude. The isolation interval lies within the Hall plateau and ends before its low-field edge; its high-field end is sharply set by the disappearance of the incompressible bulk, while its low-field end shrinks with temperature and Landau-level broadening. The resistance between an interior contact and a perimeter probe approaches one half of the quantized Hall resistance across the plateau. The topology of the incompressible regions and the boundaries of the isolation interval are insensitive to the regularization of the local conductivity, to the grid and to moderate disorder, whereas the magnitude of the isolation resistance is not.

</details>


### [160] [From Quantized Hall Plateaus to Topological Surfaces: Quantum Capacitance as a Unifying Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00336)
*A. Siddiki*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文指出量子霍尔效应、拓扑绝缘体与量子电容虽通常被分作不同课题，但共享同一主线：体拓扑决定受保护的边界态，而量子电容是探测态密度的少数静电手段之一，因此量子电容是观察拓扑所强加边界态密度最清晰的窗口，并揭示量子霍尔—拓扑绝缘体类比中"绝缘体体态"假设的局域性与场依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子霍尔效应、拓扑绝缘体与量子电容在文献中通常被当作互不相干的课题处理。作者希望表明三者存在共同线索：体拓扑决定受保护的边界态，而量子电容作为少数能够探测态密度的静电方法，可将这两条线联系起来。同时，自洽屏蔽理论对量子霍尔体态"均匀绝缘"的简单图像提出了挑战，值得系统梳理。

Method: 采用综述与概念分析的方法：回顾整数量子霍尔效应中由陈数给出的拓扑边界输运起源及其在零场下向Z2拓扑绝缘体（螺旋边缘态与狄拉克表面态）的推广；梳理量子电容从二维电子气研究发展而来的技术脉络，及其用于测绘量子霍尔效应的朗道能级态密度与拓扑表面态线性狄拉克态密度的应用；并引入自洽屏蔽理论与局域量子电容测量结果进行对照分析。

Result: 自洽屏蔽理论表明，量子霍尔体态会分裂为可压缩区与不可压缩区，且这些区域随磁场移动。局域量子电容测量证实，在量子化平台范围内体态并非均匀绝缘，尽管平台本身仍保持量子化。

Conclusion: 量子电容并非这些现象的次要特征，而是观察拓扑所强加边界态密度最清晰的窗口之一；它同时揭示了支撑量子霍尔效应与拓扑绝缘体类比的"绝缘体体态"假设具有局域、依赖磁场的性质。

Abstract: The quantum Hall effect, topological insulators and quantum capacitance are usually treated as distinct topics, yet they share a common thread. Bulk topology determines a protected boundary state, and quantum capacitance is one of the few electrostatic methods able to detect the density of states. This paper examines that connection. The integer quantum Hall effect showed that quantised boundary transport has a topological origin given by the Chern number, later extended to zero field in Z2 topological insulators with helical edge and Dirac surface states. Quantum capacitance, developed for two-dimensional electron gases, has since become a key technique for mapping the Landau level density of states underlying the quantum Hall effect and the linear Dirac density of states of topological surface states. This picture is complicated by self-consistent screening theory, which shows that the quantum Hall bulk splits into compressible and incompressible regions that shift with field. Local quantum capacitance measurements confirm that the bulk is not uniformly insulating across a quantised plateau, even though the plateau stays quantised. The paper argues that quantum capacitance is not a secondary feature of these phenomena but one of the clearest windows into the boundary density of states that topology imposes, and that it reveals the local, field-dependent nature of the insulating bulk assumption underlying the analogy between the quantum Hall effect and topological insulators.

</details>


### [161] [Strongly coupled ultracompressed self-cavity plasmon polaritons at millikelvin temperatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00473)
*Luojia Zhang,Andrew T. Pierce,Xuepeng Wang,Simon Reinhardt,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Kin Fai Mak,Jie Shan,Debanjan Chowdhury,Valla Fatemi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种超导自腔平台，实现极低温下亚太赫兹光谱测量，并观测到石墨烯腔等离激元极化激元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范德华异质结具有从亚太赫兹到太赫兹的自腔共振，可用于研究腔修饰物相，但现有方法难以在毫开尔文温度下实现高频率分辨率的谱学测量。

Method: 利用超导层构建自腔结构，实现约10^7量级的电磁体积压缩；集成石墨烯层以激发腔等离激元极化激元；并采用基于超导电路的毫开尔文兼容亚太赫兹光谱仪进行测量。

Result: 观测到频率低至0.04 THz的欠阻尼腔等离激元极化激元，并发现其与腔模之间的避免交叉现象。

Conclusion: 该平台为在毫开尔文温度下对腔-物质杂化系统进行光谱与输运同步测量提供了新路径。

Abstract: Van der Waals heterostructures exhibit self-cavity resonances from sub-terahertz to terahertz frequencies and thus offer an attractive route toward realizing cavity-modified phases of matter. Spectroscopy of such systems calls for high frequency resolution at millikelvin temperatures, which is extremely challenging with existing methods. Here we demonstrate a new platform for realizing and probing such systems. Superconducting layers define self-cavity structures with electromagnetic volume compression of order $10^7$. With an integrated graphene layer, we observe underdamped cavity plasmon polaritons at frequencies as low as 0.04 THz and which exhibit avoided crossings with cavity modes. To measure such spectra, we deploy a millikelvin-compatible sub-THz spectrometer based on a superconducting circuit. This work paves the way for simultaneous spectroscopy and transport measurements of cavity-matter hybrid systems at millikelvin temperatures.

</details>


### [162] [Algebraic Relaxation of Strong-to-Weak Symmetry-Breaking Order via Charge-Sector Broadening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00474)
*Zi-Xi Fang,Kai Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究强对称破缺Liouvillian动力学下强弱自发对称破缺（SW-SSB）序的衰减行为，揭示离散$\mathbb{Z}_2$与连续$U(1)$对称性在局域电荷交换下的不同鲁棒性，并给出两阶段动力学标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SW-SSB是混合量子态的独特现象，由与强对称相关的非线性长程序刻画。但现实开放量子系统中强对称宏观上脆弱，可被与环境局域电荷交换显式削弱为弱对称。作者旨在探究此强对称破缺动力学如何影响SW-SSB序的衰减，以区分不同对称类型下序的稳定性。

Method: 通过Liouvillian动力学分析局域对称破缺微扰对SW-SSB序的影响：对离散$\mathbb{Z}_2$对称研究Rényi-1长程关联的衰减；对连续$U(1)$对称分析电荷扇区分布展宽；建立连通Rényi-1长程关联的严格代数下界；并在热力学极限下推导两阶段动力学标度；最后用$z=2$和$z=4$的费米子链做数值验证。

Result: 离散$\mathbb{Z}_2$对称下，局域破缺微扰导致Rényi-1长程关联指数衰减，显示离散SW-SSB序的脆弱性。连续$U(1)$对称下，局域电荷交换展宽对称电荷扇区分布，产生参数上更慢的代数弛豫；建立了连通Rényi-1长程关联的严格代数下界，证明该慢弛豫的鲁棒性。热力学极限下发现两阶段动力学标度：早期$\tilde{R}_1\sim t^{-1}$，长时$\tilde{R}_1\sim t^{-1/z}$，其中$z$由底层流体动力学决定。费米子链数值结果（$z=2,4$）与理论预测高度一致。

Conclusion: 强对称破缺下SW-SSB序的衰减强烈依赖对称类型：离散对称序指数脆弱，连续对称序呈稳健的慢代数弛豫，并遵循由流体动力学指数$z$控制的两阶段标度律。

Abstract: Strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (SW-SSB) is a distinctive phenomenon of mixed quantum states, characterized by nonlinear long-range order associated with strong symmetry. In realistic open quantum systems, however, strong symmetry is macroscopically fragile and can be explicitly reduced to weak symmetry through local charge exchange with the environment. In this paper, we investigate how the SW-SSB order decays under such strong-symmetry-breaking Liouvillian dynamics. For discrete $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry, a local symmetry-breaking perturbation drives an exponential decay of the Rényi-1 long-range correlator, revealing the fragility of discrete SW-SSB order. By contrast, for continuous $U(1)$ symmetry, local charge exchange broadens the distribution over symmetry-charge sectors and leads to parametrically slower algebraic relaxation. We establish a rigorous algebraic lower bound on the connected Rényi-1 long-range correlation, demonstrating the robustness of this slow relaxation. In the thermodynamic limit, we further uncover a two-stage dynamical scaling: $\tilde{R}_1\sim t^{-1}$ at early times and $\tilde{R}_1\sim t^{-1/z}$ at long times, with $z$ set by the underlying hydrodynamic dynamics. As a numerical verification, we consider fermionic chains with $z=2$ and $z=4$, which are excellent agreement with the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [163] [How Superradiance Survives Molecular Noise inside Virus Capsids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00739)
*Tianran Li,Irina Tsvetkova,Matthew Brauser,Amala Raj,Philip Shushkov,Bogdan Dragnea*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 染料标记的二十面体病毒衣壳在室温下可自发同步发射超辐射光；实验证实衣壳存在低阻尼低频振动模，理论建模表明该运动可促进实现超辐射所需的集体极化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室温下分子体系中超辐射（SR）机制尚不清楚：尽管存在碰撞退相和inhomogeneous broadening，含激子的有机染料标记病毒衣壳仍能实现超辐射。电子-振动耦合被认为起核心作用，但缺乏对衣壳声子样模及其在从非关联到协同发射转变中的作用的认识。

Method: 实验上探测SR活性染料标记衣壳中是否存在低阻尼低频（声子样）模，并观测这些模是否调制染料激发态响应；理论上建立模型，分析衣壳运动如何促成超辐射发射所需的集体极化。

Result: SR活性染料标记衣壳表现出低阻尼低频振荡，该振荡可调制染料的激发态响应；模型显示这种衣壳运动能够促进集体极化，从而支持超辐射发射。

Conclusion: 病毒衣壳的相干低频振动模是室温超辐射的关键因素之一，为理解分子基低维体系中协同发射机制提供了实验与理论证据。

Abstract: Excited organic dyes chemically bound to an icosahedral virus shell can spontaneously synchronize and emit a superradiant burst of light. The mechanism that allows it to occur at room temperature, despite collisional dephasing and inhomogeneous broadening, is not understood. Few other known low-dimensional condensed matter systems exhibit room-temperature superradiance (SR). In the fluctuation-prone environment of molecular-based systems, electron--vibrational coupling has been proposed to play a central role. Here, we addressed experimentally the existence of underdamped virus capsid phonon-like modes and theoretically their involvement in the transition from uncorrelated to cooperative emission. We find that SR-active dye-labeled capsids exhibit underdamped low-frequency oscillations that modulate the dye excited-state response, and modeling shows how such capsid motion can promote the collective polarization required for superradiant emission.

</details>


### [164] [Tunable Electron Quantum Optics in Graphene Slit Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00768)
*Mohamed Amine Rhanbouri,Wojciech Julian Pasek,Abdelouahed El Fatimy*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 石墨烯狭缝结被证明是一种可实现定向电子束形成、相干干涉与对称性调控自旋过滤的紧凑几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找一种紧凑几何结构，在同一平台上同时实现电子光学（定向束流、干涉）与自旋相关输运，并探索单层与双层石墨烯中的低能实现。

Method: 原子级紧束缚计算结合非平衡格林函数形式，通过傅里叶分析与谐波拟合表征磁通依赖振荡；并采用自洽平均场Hubbard计算研究自旋输运，辅以四带连续模型进行谷分辨计算。

Result: 单层石墨烯中三角度扭曲（trigonal warping）在约2.7 eV附近产生强定向电子束；边界反射将束流重新导向公共漏极，透射率呈现符合h/e周期的Aharonov-Bohm磁通振荡，且可由局域静电栅调控干涉相位。原始反铁磁基态透射自旋简并，而左右对称性的结构或静电破缺可实现自旋过滤。双层石墨烯在50 meV处呈现类似的束流形成。

Conclusion: 狭缝结是一种兼具电子光学与自旋相关输运的通用几何结构，值得进一步研究低能双层石墨烯实现。

Abstract: A graphene slit junction is investigated as a compact geometry for directional electron-beam formation, coherent interference, and symmetry-controlled spin filtering. Atomistic tight-binding calculations combined with the nonequilibrium Green's function formalism show that trigonal warping in monolayer graphene produces strongly directional electron beams near E~2.7eV. Boundary reflections redirect these beams toward a common drain, where the transmission exhibits magnetic flux-dependent oscillations consistent with an h/e Aharonov-Bohm period, whose modulation is characterized by Fourier analysis and harmonic fitting. A local electrostatic gate provides additional control of the interference phase. In separate self-consistent mean-field Hubbard calculations, the pristine antiferromagnetic ground state is shown to have spin-degenerate transmission, whereas structural or electrostatic breaking of the left-right symmetry enables spin filtering. Complementary valley-resolved calculations using a four-band continuum model demonstrate analogous beam formation in bilayer graphene at E = 50meV. The results identify the slit junction as a common geometry for electron-optical and spin-dependent transport and motivate further investigation of low-energy bilayer implementations.

</details>


### [165] [Hydrogen plasma-assisted atomic layer epitaxy of superconducting titanium nitride](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00956)
*Yi-Hsun Chen,Yin-Chun Huang,Zachary Degnan,David Sommers,Kaijian Xing,Manjith Bose,Eduardo Solano,David Cortie,Michael Fuhrer,Julian A. Steele,Peter Jacobson,Miin-Jang Chen,Arkady Fedorov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文开发了氢等离子体辅助原子层沉积（ALD）方法，在c面蓝宝石上外延生长出结构有序的超导TiN薄膜，其超导转变温度为2.2 K、动力学电感为15 pH/sq、相干长度为14.6 nm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALD具有精确、共形的薄膜生长控制能力，是半导体制造的核心工艺，但其在超导量子电路中的应用受限于需在低温下同时控制结晶度、化学计量比和界面无序度。

Method: 采用氢等离子体辅助ALD外延生长超导TiN薄膜，利用氢等离子体步骤在每个沉积周期修饰表面化学以抑制前驱体引入的杂质；结合同步辐射X射线散射、X射线谱学和中子反射术表征结构、成分与界面。

Result: 同步辐射X射线散射显示TiN在c面蓝宝石上呈半共格外延，具有离散晶畴且长程结构无序极小；X射线谱学与中子反射术表明薄膜中氧相关无序可忽略、无显著氢掺入；薄膜超导转变温度2.2 K，动力学电感15 pH/sq，超导相干长度14.6 nm，与测得的晶体相干长度相当。

Conclusion: 氢等离子体辅助ALD可作为实现结构有序超导TiN薄膜外延的途径，表明ALD的纳米尺度控制能力可拓展至适用于低温量子技术的材料。

Abstract: Atomic layer deposition (ALD) offers precise, conformal control of thin-film growth and is a workhorse for semiconductor manufacturing, but its use in superconducting quantum circuits is constrained by the need to simultaneously control crystallinity, stoichiometry and interfacial disorder at cryogenic temperatures. Here we develop hydrogen plasma assisted ALD to epitaxially grow superconducting titanium nitride (TiN) thin films, using a hydrogen plasma step to modify the surface chemistry to suppress precursor-derived impurities during each deposition cycle. Synchrotron X-ray scattering reveals semi-coherent epitaxy of TiN on c-plane sapphire, with discrete crystallographic domains and minimal long-range structural disorder. Complementary X-ray spectroscopy and neutron reflectometry show negligible oxygen-related disorder throughout the film and no substantial hydrogen incorporation. The resulting TiN films exhibit a superconducting transition at 2.2 K, a kinetic inductance of 15 pH/sq and a superconducting coherence length of 14.6 nm, comparable to the measured crystal coherence length. These results establish hydrogen plasma assisted ALD as a route to structurally ordered superconducting TiN thin film epitaxy and demonstrate that the nanoscale control of ALD can be extended to materials suitable for cryogenic quantum technologies.

</details>


### [166] [Charge and spin dynamics in spintronic THz emitters from Time-Dependent Density Functional Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01089)
*Ali Kefayati*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 采用含时密度泛函理论实时、实空间解析Co/Pt与Co/W双层的电荷、自旋与磁化动力学，发现自旋流相似但层间电荷转移显著不同，其根源在于材料依赖的基态电子结构与自旋/轨道分辨的相空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁磁/重金属异质结自旋电子太赫兹发射器虽能提供高效宽带辐射，但超快自旋输运与电荷再分布之间的微观关系尚不清楚。

Method: 使用含时密度泛函理论（TDDFT），在真实时间和空间尺度上追踪Co/Pt与Co/W双层的电荷、自旋和磁化动力学，并进行自旋与轨道分辨的相空间分析。

Result: 两者层间自旋流动力学定性相似，但层间电荷转移差异显著：Co/Pt表现为较小且部分反转的Pt→Co转移，Co/W表现为较大且持久的Co→W转移；差异源于基态电子结构与光激发载流子可及相空间。自旋分辨动力学揭示自旋轨道介导的再分布与光致跨位点自旋转移共存，包括两种体系共有的、延迟的重金属→Co少子自旋转移。层间存在净电子自旋角动量转移，但无净电子轨道角动量转移。Co/W中电子密度由间隙态向原子中心态的持久再分布，为光致电子局域化提供了有力证据。

Conclusion: 超快自旋输运并不唯一决定自旋电子太赫兹发射器中的电荷或角动量再分布，后者关键取决于材料特异的电子相空间。

Abstract: Spintronic terahertz (THz) emitters based on ferromagnet/heavy-metal heterostructures provide efficient broadband sources of THz radiation, yet the microscopic relation between ultrafast spin transport and charge redistribution remains incompletely understood. Here, we employ time-dependent density functional theory to resolve the coupled charge, spin, and magnetization dynamics of Co/Pt and Co/W bilayers in real time and space. Despite qualitatively similar interlayer spin-current dynamics, the two heterostructures exhibit markedly different interlayer charge transfer: Co/Pt shows a relatively small and partially reversed transfer from Pt to Co, whereas Co/W exhibits a larger and persistent transfer from Co to W. We trace these differences to the material-dependent ground-state electronic structure and the spin- and orbital-resolved phase space accessible to photoexcited carriers. Spin-resolved dynamics further reveal coexisting spin-orbit-mediated redistribution and optically induced intersite spin transfer, including a delayed minority-spin transfer from the heavy metal to Co common to both systems. While net electronic spin angular momentum is transferred between the layers, we find no corresponding net transfer of electronic orbital angular momentum. In Co/W, the persistent redistribution of electronic density from interstitial to atom-centered states additionally provides strong evidence for light-induced electronic localization. These results demonstrate that ultrafast spin transport does not uniquely determine charge or angular-momentum redistribution in spintronic THz emitters, which instead depends critically on the material-specific electronic phase space.

</details>


### [167] [Gate Dispersive Charge Detection in Ge/SiGe Quantum Dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01100)
*M. R. Tanvir,E. Sajadi,M. Veldhorst,G. Scappucci,J. Salfi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文演示了Ge双量子点及单空穴盒-双量子点系统的紧凑型射频栅极色散电荷读出，通过单空穴盒将互点信号信噪比提升20倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ge异质结中的侧栅量子点是自旋量子比特的重要实现平台，但现有RF单空穴晶体管和集成超导谐振器读出方案尺寸较大，存在集成挑战，因此需要紧凑的读出方法。

Method: 采用紧凑的RF栅极色散电荷读出技术，针对Ge双量子点（DQD）以及单空穴盒（SHB）-DQD系统进行测量，分辨量子点加载与互点跃迁，并结合功率和温度依赖测量分析噪声来源。

Result: 在DQD耦合tc≈5 GHz时，SHB使540 MHz电路的互点信噪比提升20倍，在135微秒达到单位信噪比，而直接探测需2.7毫秒；功率和温度依赖测量表明信噪比受介电损耗限制，影响阻抗匹配。

Conclusion: 这些结果确立了基于栅极色散检测的紧凑读出方案，适用于Ge/SiGe异质结量子点。

Abstract: Spin qubits are an attractive platform for scalable quantum information processing, and recently, laterally gated quantum dots (QDs) in Ge heterostructures have emerged as a leading implementation of spin qubits. To date, RF single-hole transistors (SHT) and integrated superconducting resonators have been used for spin and charge readout, but their size can pose challenges. Here we demonstrate compact RF gate-dispersive charge readout of a Ge double QD (DQD), and a single hole box (SHB)-DQD system. We resolve QD loading and interdot transitions with both techniques. We find that the SHB enhances the interdot signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) of our 540 MHz circuit by a factor of 20 for a DQD coupling $t_c \approx 5$ GHz, providing a unity-SNR at 135 microseconds, c.f. for 2.7 miliseconds for the direct detection. Power- and temperature-dependent measurements verify that SNR is limited by dielectric loss which compromises impedance matching. These results establish a compact readout approach based on gate-dispersive detection for Ge/SiGe heterostructure QDs.

</details>


### [168] [Nutational Spin Pumping and Dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01164)
*Hans Gløckner Giil,Arne Brataas*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在太赫兹驱动频率下，磁化动力学进入惯性区，超越标准LLG理论；本文揭示包含体项和界面项的章动阻尼力矩，界面项导致与驱动频率平方成正比的直流自旋流（章动自旋泵浦），并通过散射方法关联微观参数，预测在超薄膜和低费米能导体中显著增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert (LLG)理论无法描述太赫兹驱动频率下磁化动力学所处的惯性区，需要超越LLG框架来理解该区域的自旋动力学与阻尼机制。

Method: 理论分析章动阻尼力矩，将其分解为体项和界面贡献；采用散射方法将界面项（描述向相邻金属的自旋发射）与微观材料参数关联。

Result: 界面项产生章动自旋泵浦：一种与驱动频率平方成正比的直流自旋流；预测该效应在超薄膜和低费米能导体中显著增强。

Conclusion: 章动自旋泵浦可通过逆自旋霍尔效应直接探测，或通过章动阻尼的厚度依赖重整化间接探测。

Abstract: At terahertz drive frequencies, magnetization dynamics enter an inertial regime beyond the scope of standard Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert (LLG) theory. We reveal a nutational damping torque comprising a bulk term and an interfacial contribution that describes spin emission into adjacent metals. The interfacial term generates nutational spin pumping: a dc spin current that scales quadratically with the drive frequency. Using a scattering approach, we relate this effect to microscopic material parameters and predict pronounced enhancements in ultrathin films and low-Fermi-energy conductors. Nutational spin pumping can be detected directly via the inverse spin Hall effect or indirectly via thickness-dependent renormalization of nutational damping.

</details>


### [169] [Synthesis of Metal-Semiconductor Heterojunctions in Single Graphene Nanoribbons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01194)
*Ziyi Wang,Kaitlin Slicker,Weichen Tang,Boyu Qie,Rafal Zuzak,Haowen Pang,Yudi Huang,Xinheng Li,Peter H. Jacobse,Steven G. Louie,Felix R. Fischer,Michael F. Crommie*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文利用顺序表面合成与STM诱导脱氢，在不改变宽度和碳骨架连接性的前提下，将金属性7-iGNR片段直接写入半导体H₂-7-iGNR中，实现原子级精确的金属-半导体GNR界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNR是高度可调的1D量子材料，带隙与带边对齐可控，是纳米电子学平台，但构建高质量电子界面仍是难点，尤其是金属-半导体接触。

Method: 采用顺序on-surface合成结合STM诱导脱氢，在半导体H₂-7-iGNR中直接写入金属性7-iGNR片段；用STS和第一性原理计算表征界面，并以扩展的Su-Schrieffer-Heeger锯齿梯子Hamiltonian建模金属态。

Result: 金属性片段在费米能级处呈现非零谱权重和空间扩展电子态，符合两条色散能带穿过EF；相邻半导体GNR价带边接近EF，STS显示界面两侧约1 nm内即恢复体相GNR性质，无可辨耗尽区。

Conclusion: 观测到的带边对齐对应较小的空穴注入势垒，为未来GNR电子器件提供原子级精确、低势垒p型接触的潜在路径。

Abstract: Graphene nanoribbons (GNRs) are a highly tunable class of one-dimensional (1D) quantum materials that can be fabricated through bottom-up synthesis. Precise control over GNR band gaps and band-edge alignments has established them as a promising nanoelectronics platform, but forming high-quality electronic interfaces remains challenging. Here we combine sequential on-surface synthesis and scanning tunneling microscopy (STM)-induced dehydrogenation to directly write metallic 7-iGNR segments into otherwise semiconducting H$_2$-7-iGNRs without changing the GNR width or carbon backbone connectivity. The resulting metallic segments exhibit nonzero spectral weight at the Fermi level ($E_\mathrm{F}$) and spatially extended electronic states, consistent with two dispersive bands crossing $E_\mathrm{F}$. The new metallic states are well described by an extended Su--Schrieffer--Heeger zigzag-ladder Hamiltonian. Scanning tunnelling spectroscopy (STS) and first-principles calculations show that the valence band edge of adjacent, connected semiconducting GNR segments lies close to $E_\mathrm{F}$ for the metallic segments. STS spectra recover bulk GNR properties within $\sim$1 nm on either side of the metal-semiconductor GNR interface, with no discernible depletion region. The observed GNR band edge alignment is consistent with a small energy barrier for hole injection, thus suggesting a possible route towards atomically precise, low-barrier p-type contacts for future GNR-based electronic devices.

</details>


### [170] [Phonon interference induced by defect pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01200)
*Ran-Bo Yang,Ming-Hao Li,Chun-Yu Cai,Yue-Wen Wang,Zhi-Qing Li,Li-Xia Zhao,Zi-Wu Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出缺陷对诱导声子干涉的理论模型，发现干涉条纹强度可由Huang-Rhys因子直接衡量，并通过缺陷对内参量调控干涉图样，预言Dirac型和节线型拓扑态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非平衡条件下电子-声子耦合的非绝热现象，需要理解晶格波的相干动力学，而缺陷对作为多自由度相干驱动源是理想研究平台。

Method: 构建由缺陷对诱导的声子干涉理论模型，利用强局域电子-声子耦合使各缺陷独立发射选择性模式晶格波，并以Huang-Rhys模型模拟色心激发后的超快光谱。

Result: 干涉条纹强度与可测量的Huang-Rhys因子直接对应；干涉图样几何受电荷态、空间距离、能级深度和局域强度调控；特定参量下出现Dirac型和节线型拓扑态；超快光谱强度与干涉条纹呈锁钥式匹配。

Conclusion: 缺陷对可作为多自由度相干动力学过程的驱动力，为非平衡与超快物理中基础量子现象的研究提供理想平台。

Abstract: The coherent dynamics of lattice waves is of significant importance for exploring the nonadiabatic phenomena of the electron-phonon coupling under nonequilibrium conditions. Here, we propose a theoretical model for phonon interference induced by a defect pair, in which lattice waves with the selective modes are launched independently by each of defects due to the strongly local electron-phonon coupling. We find that the intensities of phonon interference fringes could be directly evaluated by a measurable quantity in experiments--Huang-Rhys factor, and the geometry of interference patterns could be modified by the internal parameters of defect pair, including the charge states, spatial distance, energy-level depth and localization strength. In particular, several novel quantum states, such as the Dirac-like and nodal-line topological states are emerged for the appropriate internal parameters. Furthermore, using Huang-Rhys model, we simulate the ultrafast optical spectroscopy in the phonon interference duration when defect pairs are excited as the color centers, where interference fringes are perfectly matched by intensities of spectrum, similar to the $\textit{lock-key}$ pattern. These results show that defect pair, serving as the driven force for the coherent dynamical processes for multidegrees of freedom, offers an ideal platform to explore fundamental quantum phenomena in nonequilibrium and ultrafast physics.

</details>


### [171] [Composite fermions in ideal Chern bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01217)
*Songyang Pu,Liangtao Peng,Shaffique Adam*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文将复合费米子框架推广到分数量子陈绝缘体，在Aharonov-Casher带中构造Jain态及其准粒子波函数，揭示复合费米子能带结构超越传统等效朗道能级图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合费米子框架原用于将强关联分数量子霍尔态映射为约化有效磁场中的弱相互作用整数量子霍尔态，但分数量子陈绝缘体中均匀磁场被陈带的非均匀贝里曲率取代，该框架如何修正尚不明确。

Method: 以理想陈带模型Aharonov-Casher带为平台，构造Jain态及其准粒子、准空穴的复合费米子波函数，并与严格本征态进行重叠计算，进而分析涌现的复合费米子能带结构。

Result: 在宽泛的场调制范围内，所构造波函数与严格本征态高度重叠；复合费米子能带结构超越等效朗道能级图像，能正确描述准粒子色散；发现孤立准粒子带携带的总贝里通量不同于朗道能级。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了可分数量子陈绝缘体任意子的复合费米子描述，并提供了理解其稳定性的理论框架。

Abstract: The composite-fermion framework maps the strongly correlated fractional quantum Hall state onto a weakly interacting integer quantum Hall state in a reduced effective magnetic field. We ask how this framework is modified for fractional Chern insulators, where the role of a uniform magnetic field is replaced by the nonuniform Berry curvature of a Chern band. Specifically, we construct composite-fermion wave functions for Jain states and their quasiparticles and quasiholes in Aharonov-Casher bands, a widely used model for ideal Chern bands. We find that these states overlap closely with exact eigenstates over a broad range of field modulation. Using these wave functions, we demonstrate that the emergent composite-fermion band structure goes beyond the conventional picture of effective Landau levels, correctly capturing the quasiparticle dispersion. Remarkably, we find that an isolated quasiparticle band carries a total Berry flux that is different from a Landau level. These results establish a composite-fermion description for mobile fractional Chern insulator anyons and provide a framework to understand their stability.

</details>


### [172] [Inertial Dynamics of a Skymeron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01339)
*Mona Bhukta,Duc Minh Tran,Kilian Leutner,Takaaki Dohi,Nikolai S. Kiselev,Filipp N. Rybakov,Olle Eriksson,Fabian Kammerbauer,Sebastian Wintz,Markus Weigand,Hendrik Ohldag,Maria-Andromachi Syskaki,Robin Tietgen,Edoardo Mangini,Sabrina Kerber,Thibaud Denneulin,Joseph Vimal Vas,Rafal E. Dunin-Borkowski,Bastian Pfau,Oleg A. Tretiakov,Robert Frömter,Mathias Kläui*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过在合成反铁磁体中调控两层有效磁各向异性差，诱导自旋翻转转变，将反铁磁斯格明子同伦重构为由面外斯格明子与面内双麦纟元耦合的三维自旋织构"skymeron"，并揭示其极性依赖的超快动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内禀反铁磁体两亚晶格完全相同，缺乏额外调控自由度；而合成反铁磁体可通过独立调节两铁磁层性质，像铁磁体一样调整总磁矩，为设计新型拓扑自旋织构提供可能。作者希望利用层选择性各向异性工程，探索常规反铁磁体中不可能出现的复合自旋织构及其内部动力学自由度。

Method: 调控两层的有效磁各向异性差，驱动自旋翻转转变，使其中一层从面外重取向至面内，从而稳定正交构型；采用元素分辨X射线显微术逐层解析该自旋织构；利用时间分辨泵浦-探测X射线显微术观察电流脉冲下的动力学；并结合微磁学模拟分析其机理。

Result: 自旋翻转转变使反铁磁斯格明子发生同伦重构，形成由一层中的面外斯格明子与另一层中的面内双麦纟元耦合而成的三维自旋织构，命名为"skymeron"；元素分辨X射线显微术在两层中分别确认了该耦合结构。时间分辨实验发现极性依赖的独特动力学：电流脉冲后出现类惯性的短暂延续运动，随后缓慢回归被钉扎的初始态；微磁学模拟表明该惯性传播源于skymeron重取向的有限时间弛豫。

Conclusion: 层选择性各向异性工程可作为通往具有内部动力学自由度、全新复合自旋织构的途径，这类织构在亚晶格完全相同的常规反铁磁体中无法实现，为低功耗自旋电子学器件提供了新思路。

Abstract: Topological spin textures in antiferromagnets inherit the compensated magnetic order of the host material, resulting for skyrmions in the suppression of the skyrmion Hall effect and enabling ultrafast dynamics that make them attractive for low-power spintronic devices.
