In intrinsic antiferromagnets, the two sublattices are identical, but synthetic antiferromagnets offer additional control mechanisms by enabling independent tuning of the properties of two ferromagnetic layers. Analogous to a ferrimagnet, the total magnetic moment can be adjusted by compensating the two-layer moments. Here, we demonstrate that tuning the effective magnetic anisotropy difference between the two layers drives a spin-flop transition where one layer reorients and thus stabilizes an orthogonal configuration with one layer oriented along the out-of-plane direction and the other in-plane. In this transition, an antiferromagnetic skyrmion undergoes a homotopic reconfiguration into a complex spin texture comprising a skyrmion coupled to an in-plane bimeron. Element-specific X-ray microscopy resolves this texture layer by layer: one hosts an out-of-plane skyrmion coupled to an in-plane bimeron in the other. We refer to this previously unexplored three-dimensional spin texture as a skymeron. By using time-resolved pump-probe X-ray microscopy, we discover unique polarity ,dependent dynamics during current pulses: a short post-pulse inertia-like continuation of the motion, and a slower return towards the pinned initial state. Micromagnetic simulations reveal that the inertia-like propagation originates from the finite-time relaxation of reorientation of the skymeron. Our results establish layer-selective anisotropy engineering as a route to uncharted composite spin textures with internal dynamical degrees of freedom, not possible in conventional antiferromagnets with identical sublattices.

</details>


### [173] [Transport theory for a generic two-arm co-propagating Majorana interferometer with Majorana fermion and edge vortex tunneling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01402)
*Domenico Giuliano,Andrea Nava,Fabian Hassler,Reinhold Egger*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在近邻耦合量子反常霍尔系统中，共传播手性马约拉纳边缘模式被预言存在。本文通过引入复制扇区的玻色化方法，将研究推广至完全非对称构型，分析Mach-Zehnder干涉仪，发现直流电导无边缘涡旋干涉信号，而低频下干涉表现为有效电容，该电容对边缘涡旋的非阿贝尔任意子统计、拓扑自旋及共形维度敏感，并确定低温相关尺度为热长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共传播手性马约拉纳边缘模式结合了边缘涡旋作为飞行非阿贝尔Ising任意子的前景与通过任意子融合实现全电学探测的可能性。此前处理多限于对称或特定构型，需推广至更一般的完全非对称情形以刻画电输运干涉效应。

Method: 对模型引入额外复制扇区进行玻色化处理，将先前处理推广到完全非对称构型；分析Mach-Zehnder干涉仪，考察直流电导与低频响应。

Result: 直流电导不显示边缘涡旋干涉特征；在低频下，该干涉表现为有效电容，该量对边缘涡旋的非阿贝尔任意子统计敏感，并携带其拓扑自旋与非常规共形维度的信号。热长度l_T=ħv/(πk_B T)是低温下的相关尺度，类似常规量子霍尔边缘态中速度为v的准粒子输运；当干涉仪臂长在该尺度内匹配时，边缘涡旋非阿贝尔统计的输运信号仍可见。

Conclusion: 低频有效电容可作为探测边缘涡旋非阿贝尔任意子统计及其拓扑自旋、共形维度的电学探针；干涉仪臂长需在热长度尺度内匹配才能保持统计信号的可见性。

Abstract: Co-propagating chiral Majorana edge modes are predicted to exist in proximity-coupled quantum anomalous Hall systems. They combine the exciting prospects of edge vortices as flying non-Abelian Ising anyons, with their fully electrical detection through anyon fusion processes. In this setup, electrical transport arises from the interference of a pair of Majorana edge states. By bosonizing the model with an additional replica sector, we generalize previous treatments to fully asymmetric configurations. We analyze a Mach-Zehnder interferometer and show that while the DC conductance does not exhibit signatures of edge-vortex interference, at low frequencies, this interference manifests as an effective capacitance. This quantity is sensitive to the non-Abelian anyon statistics of edge vortices and, in particular, carries signatures of their topological spin and nontrivial conformal dimension. We demonstrate that the thermal length $l_T = \hbar v/(πk_B T)$ is the relevant scale at low temperatures $T$, analogous to quasiparticle transport in conventional quantum Hall edge states with velocity $v$. As long as the interferometer arm lengths match within this length scale, transport signatures of the non-Abelian statistics of edge vortices remain visible.

</details>


### [174] [Sector-Resolved Winding Selection Rules for Structured-Light-Driven dc Currents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01423)
*Tomohiro Tamaya,Kenichi L. Ishikawa*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文以石墨烯为二维平台，通过在电流算符层面将电流响应分解为局域项与梯度项，揭示了结构光驱动直流电流的方位角环绕序数 $m$ 的分区选择定则，并给出标量拉盖尔-高斯光束下的具体规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构光可产生具有方位角绕转的电子直流电流，但其绕转序数 $m$ 的支配规律尚不清楚，缺乏系统性的分类与调控原理。作者旨在建立一套分区（sector）分辨的环绕选择定则，以厘清 $m$ 究竟由哪些物理量决定。

Method: 选取石墨烯作为干净二维平台；在电流算符层面把电流响应分解为局域（local）扇区与梯度（gradient）扇区；结合电流算符的角向结构与在方位角方向上的投影进行解析推导；并采用数值时间演化计算对所得定则进行验证。

Result: 绕转序数不由光学轨道角动量 $\\ell$ 单独决定，而由相应电流算符的角向结构与方位角投影共同决定。对标量拉盖尔-高斯光束：线偏振下局域扇区为 $m=|\\ell\\pm1|$，梯度扇区为 $m=|\\ell|,|\\ell\\pm2|$；圆偏振（螺旋度 $\\sigma=\\pm1$）下局域扇区选择 $m=|\\ell-\\sigma|$，梯度扇区选择 $m=|\\ell+2\\sigma|$。数值时间演化计算验证了上述规则，并进一步表明螺旋度可选出 $m=0$ 分支，形成方位角均匀的环流，其径向分布决定轴向磁场 $B_z(z)$。

Conclusion: 研究提供一个分区分辨的组织原理，可用于对结构光驱动的直流电流及其定制化绕转结构进行分类与调控。

Abstract: Structured light can generate electronic dc currents with azimuthal winding, yet the rules governing their winding order $m$ remain unclear. Here we identify sector-resolved winding selection rules using graphene as a clean two-dimensional platform. By decomposing the current response into local and gradient sectors at the current-operator level, we show that the winding order is determined not by the optical orbital angular momentum $\ell$ alone, but by the angular structure of the corresponding current operators together with projection onto the azimuthal direction. For scalar Laguerre-Gaussian beams, linear polarization yields $m=|\ell\pm1|$ in the local sector and $m=|\ell|,|\ell\pm2|$ in the gradient sector, whereas circular polarization with helicity $σ=\pm1$ selects $m=|\ell-σ|$ in the local sector and $m=|\ell+2σ|$ in the gradient sector. Numerical time-evolution calculations verify these rules and further show that helicity can select an $m=0$ branch, producing an azimuthally uniform circulating current whose radial profile determines the axial magnetic field $B_z(z)$. Our results provide a sector-resolved organizing principle for classifying and controlling structured-light-driven dc currents with tailored winding structures.

</details>


### [175] [Parafermions in fractional Chern insulator-superconductor heterostructures: the role of spin polarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01486)
*Aaron Amire*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文分析了自旋-谷极化 ν=2/3 FQAH 边缘之间的沟槽结构，发现配对与隧穿双线性算符各自恰有两个零共形自旋分量；配对只与中性模式超导通道兼容，隧穿只与中性模式背散射兼容。两通道凝聚相同的中性任意子（相差局域算符），因此畴壁承载 Z₃ parafermion（每对3个受保护态），且固定费米子宇称不改变该计数。论文进一步讨论了中性能隙形成前后的相关性与边缘间库仑耦合，并指出需要等自旋、谷内、偶频配对（手征 p+ip 与 f+if 可行，仅含 Ising 自旋轨道耦合的 s 波单态超导体在领头阶不行）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往在分数量子霍尔-超导体结构中实现 Z₃ parafermion 的方案多基于自旋非极化的 ν=2/3 Halperin (1,1,2) 态。而扭转 MoTe₂ 与菱面体石墨烯的 FQAH 态被认为是自旋-谷极化的 Jain 态，具有相同拓扑序但不同自旋结构。作者希望澄清这种不同自旋结构对 Z₃ parafermion 方案的影响，并确定在极化 ν=2/3 边缘沟槽中实现畴壁 parafermion 的条件与鲁棒性。

Method: 构建两条自旋-谷极化 ν=2/3 边缘之间的沟槽模型，分析边缘模式电子的配对与隧穿双线性算符的共形自旋；利用共形场论（CFT）论证哪些通道可与超导/背散射耦合。通过中性任意子凝聚的图像比较两通道的等价性（相差局域算符），并研究中性能隙形成前配对算符的标度维数与边缘间库仑耦合（含吸引中性耦合）对相关性的影响；计算能隙形成后的电荷项相关性与阈值（边缘间库仑耦合 < 7/25 的边内耦合）。同时考察超导体配对对称性要求（等频、等自旋、谷内配对），比较 s 波单态+Ising 自旋轨道耦合与手征 p+ip、f+if 态；并分析 Z₃ 标签的载体及约瑟夫森电流周期性。

Result: 配对与隧穿双线性算符中各自恰有两个零共形自旋分量。配对仅兼容中性模式超导通道，隧穿仅兼容中性模式背散射；而在非极化态中，单态配对与隧穿选择同一通道。两通道凝聚相同的中性任意子（相差局域算符），域壁承载 Z₃ parafermion，每对3个受保护态，且与中性通道是否在区域间变化无关；固定费米子宇称不扩大计数。中性能隙形成前，对排斥性边缘间耦合，配对最多为边缘相关；除非吸引性中性耦合倾向于其通道。能隙形成后，当边缘间库仑耦合低于边内耦合的 7/25 时，两个电荷项均为相关。超导体须提供偶频、等自旋、谷内配对：含仅 Ising 自旋轨道耦合的 s 波单态超导体在领头阶不能提供，而手征 p+ip 与 f+if 态可以。Z₃ 标签由 FQAH 态的准粒子携带，因此任何局限于隧穿段非 FQAH 的结的物理过程都无法改变它，预期无论是否固定费米子宇称都呈 6π 周期约瑟夫森电流。

Conclusion: 在自旋-谷极化的 ν=2/3 FQAH 边缘沟槽中，配对与隧穿分别对应中性模式超导与中性模式背散射两个不同通道，但二者凝聚同一中性任意子，因而仍可实现 Z₃ parafermion 畴壁态，每对3个受保护态且不受固定费米子宇称影响。实现需要超导体提供偶频、等自旋、谷内配对（手征 p+ip 或 f+if 合适），且 Z₃ 标签由 FQAH 准粒子携带，导致预期的 6π 周期约瑟夫森电流。

Abstract: Most proposals for $Z_3$ parafermions in fractional quantum Hall-superconductor structures used the spin-unpolarized $ν= 2/3$ Halperin $(1,1,2)$ state. The fractional quantum anomalous Hall (FQAH) states of twisted MoTe$_2$ and rhombohedral graphene are believed to be spin- and valley-polarized Jain states, with the same topological order but a different spin structure. We analyze a trench between two polarized $ν= 2/3$ edges. Among the pairing and tunneling bilinears of the edge-mode electrons, exactly two of each have zero conformal spin. Pairing is compatible only with the neutral-mode superconductivity channel and tunneling only with neutral-mode backscattering, whereas in the unpolarized state singlet pairing and tunneling select the same channel. Since the two channels condense the same neutral anyon up to a local operator, the domain walls carry $Z_3$ parafermions, with 3 protected states per pair, whether or not the neutral channel changes between regions; fixing the fermion parity does not enlarge this count. Before the neutral gap forms, pairing is at best marginal for repulsive inter-edge coupling unless an attractive neutral coupling favors its channel; once the gap has formed, both charge terms are relevant for an inter-edge Coulomb coupling below 7/25 of the intra-edge one. The superconductor must supply even-frequency, equal-spin, intravalley pairing, which an s-wave singlet superconductor with only Ising spin-orbit coupling does not provide at leading order, whereas chiral $p+ip$ and $f+if$ states do. The $Z_3$ label is carried by a quasiparticle of the FQAH state, so no process confined to a junction whose tunneling segments are not FQAH can change it, and we expect a $6π$-periodic Josephson current with or without fixed fermion parity.

</details>


### [176] [Single-Pulse Optical Switching combined with Current-Induced Motion of Skyrmionic Spin Textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01568)
*Edoardo Mangini,Duc Minh Tran,Boonthum Kunyangyuen,Jun Xiao Lin,Raphael Gruber,Michel Hehn,Stéphane Mangin,Mathias Kläui*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在Pt/Ir/CoB/Gd/Pt多层膜中，通过单发飞秒激光脉冲实现磁斯格明子自旋结构（极性）的确定性全光翻转，并可与电流驱动运动结合，形成光编程内部状态、电流控制位置的光自旋电子器件方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁斯格明子可作为纳米信息载体，其位置可由电流操控，但若还能确定性控制其自旋结构（极性），则可作为多态比特，进一步扩展斯格明子器件功能。作者希望将全光无螺旋度依赖磁化翻转（AO-HIS）与磁斯格明子耦合，实现这一附加控制通道。

Method: 设计并制备同时能稳定磁斯格明子且支持AO-HIS的Pt/Ir/CoB/Gd/Pt多层膜。用激光激发写入斯格明子织构，其极性由小幅面外磁场选择；再用单发30 fs激光脉冲对含斯格明子织构的照明区域进行磁化翻转（toggle），无需磁场或电流脉冲即可反转其极性。随后将材料图形化为线状结构，将光学操控与电流驱动斯格明子运动相结合。

Result: 单发30 fs激光脉冲可在无磁场、无电流脉冲条件下反转斯格明子织构的极性，实现斯格明子自旋结构的确定性光学翻转；该机制不同于此前报道的光学成核与湮灭。图形化为线后，可与电流诱导的斯格明子运动相集成。

Conclusion: 确定性光学翻转斯格明子自旋结构构成了新的操控通道，为光自旋电子斯格明子器件奠定路线：光编程斯格明子内部状态，电流控制其位置。

Abstract: Magnetic skyrmions are promising nanoscale information carriers because their position can be manipulated by electrical currents. The additional ability of deterministic control of the spin structure would allow the use of magnetic skyrmions as multi-state bits, expanding the capabilities of skyrmion devices further. Such control could be realized by coupling all-optical helicity independent switching of magnetization (AO-HIS) to magnetic skyrmions. This is realized in a Pt/Ir/CoB/Gd/Pt multilayer that is engineered to stabilize magnetic skyrmions and, at the same time, enable AO-HIS by single ultrafast laser pulse. Laser excitation is used to write skyrmionic textures, whose polarity is selected by a small applied out-of-plane field. A single 30 fs laser pulse is then used to toggle the magnetization of an illuminated region containing skyrmionic textures, reversing their polarities without requiring any magnetic field or current pulses. This establishes deterministic optical switching of the skyrmion spin structure as a new manipulation channel, distinct from previously reported optical nucleation and annihilation. The material is then patterned into a wire, to combine the optical manipulation with current-induced skyrmion motion. These results establish a route toward opto-spintronic skyrmion devices in which light programs the internal state of a skyrmion and electrical currents control its position.

</details>


### [177] [Flux- and tunnel-parity-controlled Josephson nonreciprocity from singlet-triplet competition in a parallel double quantum dot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01830)
*Debika Debnath,Fernando Dominguez,Patrik Recher*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文研究平行双量子点约瑟夫森结中的非互易超电流，揭示多体分支竞争对约瑟夫森二极管效应的调控机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易超电流可由正负临界电流探测相位依赖多体谱的不同部分而产生，但强库仑相互作用下局域与非局域库珀对输运共存时的具体机制尚不清晰。

Method: 采用零带宽哈密顿量的精确对角化方法，显式包含超导轨道，研究强格点内库仑排斥的平行双量子点约瑟夫森结。

Result: 轨道磁通与规范不变的隧穿符号宇称控制局域约瑟夫森过程与非局域交换的干涉；失谐量子点产生磁通可调的单态、三态与双态基态分支及两种整流区间。(1,1)电荷区边界处双态分支与单态或三态分支竞争，二极管效率接近40%；三态主导区中邻近单态交叉非对称重塑基态包络，产生约10-15%的更宽响应；有限温度通常抑制电荷交叉整流，但中等温度下三态区的归一化效率与绝对临界电流不对称性均增强。

Conclusion: 相位依赖的多体分支竞争是约瑟夫森非互易性的来源，且对栅压、磁通和隧穿宇称敏感。

Abstract: Nonreciprocal supercurrents in interacting nanostructures can arise when the positive and negative critical currents probe inequivalent portions of a phase-dependent many-body spectrum. We study this mechanism in a parallel double-quantum-dot Josephson junction with strong intradot Coulomb repulsion, where local and non-local Cooper-pair transfer coexist. Using exact diagonalization of a zero-bandwidth Hamiltonian with explicit superconducting orbitals, we show that orbital flux and the gauge-invariant tunnel-sign parity control the interference between local Josephson processes and non-local exchange. For detuned dots, this produces flux-tunable singlet, triplet, and doublet ground-state spectral branches and two rectification regimes. Near the boundaries of the charge sector with singly occupied dots [(1,1) sector], doublet branches compete with singlet or triplet branches, yielding diode efficiencies approaching 40%. Within the triplet-dominated (1,1) sector, nearby singlet crossings asymmetrically reshape the ground-state envelope even when both critical-current extrema lie on the triplet-like branch, producing a broader response of order 10-15%. Finite temperature generally suppresses charge-crossover rectification, however, for intermediate temperatures we find an enhancement of both the normalized efficiency and the absolute critical-current asymmetry in the triplet regime. These results establish phase-dependent many-body branch competition as a gate-, flux-, and tunnel-parity-sensitive source of Josephson nonreciprocity.

</details>


### [178] [Symmetry considerations in chirality-induced spin selectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01880)
*Dmitry Budker,Angela Wittmann*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文以粒子物理中常用的对称性分析为框架，论证手性诱导自旋选择性（CISS）效应并不违反宇称与时间反演等基本对称性，从而为该效应提供统一的对称性理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CISS效应（结构手性与电子自旋极化的耦合）已在多种体系中被实验观测到，但尽管有大量理论研究，仍缺乏统一的机理理解，亟需一个稳健的概念框架来解释实验并指导后续研究。

Method: 采用基础粒子物理中常用的对称性分析（对称性论证），系统考察CISS效应与宇称对称性及时间反演对称性的关系，而非提出新的微观动力学模型。

Result: 分析表明CISS效应不违反任何基本对称性，包括宇称对称性与时间反演对称性。

Conclusion: 通过将CISS效应纳入对称性的通用语言，本文为其提供了稳健的概念基础，可用于解释实验结果并指导未来的理论与实验设计。

Abstract: The chirality-induced spin selectivity (CISS) effect, the coupling between structural chirality and electron spin polarization, has been experimentally observed across many diverse systems. However, despite extensive theoretical effort, a unified mechanistic understanding remains elusive. In this perspective, we demonstrate how some of the basic properties of the fascinating effects of CISS can be understood based on straightforward symmetry considerations commonly employed in fundamental particle physics. In particular, we show that CISS does not violate any fundamental symmetries including parity and time-reversal. By anchoring CISS within the universal language of symmetry, we offer a robust conceptual foundation for interpreting experiments and guiding future theoretical and experimental designs.

</details>

### [179] [Synthetic SU(3) non-Abelian gauge fields in photonic synthetic frequency dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00304)
*Bengy Tsz Tsun Wong,Shu Yang,Zehai Pang,Yi Yang*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出利用三个耦合环形谐振器结合调制与干涉调控的模式转换，在合成频率维度实现SU(3)规范场，并给出可分解为光学元件的紧束缚哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有合成规范场研究多集中于阿贝尔或低秩非阿贝尔结构，合成频率维度中的SU(3)规范场实现仍属空白。

Method: 构建以Gell-Mann矩阵生成矩阵值跃迁的紧束缚哈密顿量，并将其演化分解为实验可实现的光学元件；采用三个耦合环形谐振器实现调制与干涉调控的模式转换。

Result: 平台支持具有色分辨纹理的SU(3)结构能带色散，以及由SU(3)规范场塑造的波包动力学；全波启发数值模拟验证了预期光谱响应与SU(3)子空间中的类Zitterbewegung运动。

Conclusion: 将SU(3)规范场引入合成频率光子学，为调制谐振器系统中更高秩非阿贝尔光调控提供路径。

Abstract: Synthetic gauge fields provide a powerful route to engineer photonic wave dynamics, especially in synthetic frequency dimensions formed by modulated resonator modes. While most previous realizations have focused on Abelian or lower-rank non-Abelian structures, a photonic implementation of SU(3) gauge fields in the synthetic frequency dimension has remained lacking. Here we propose a route to realize SU(3) gauge structure using three coupled ring resonators with modulation- and interference-engineered mode conversion. We formulate a tight-binding Hamiltonian with matrix-valued hopping generated by the Gell-Mann matrices and show how its evolution can be decomposed into experimentally implementable optical elements. The resulting platform supports SU(3)-structured band dispersions with color-resolved texture and wave-packet dynamics shaped by SU(3) gauge fields. Full-wave-inspired numerical modeling further confirms the expected spectral response and Zitterbewegung-like motion in SU(3) subspaces. Our results introduce SU(3) gauge fields into synthetic frequency photonics and establish a route toward higher-rank non-Abelian control of light in modulated resonator systems.

</details>


### [180] [High-power photoconductive THz emitters for fast parallel electro-optic detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00744)
*Mohsen Khalili,Robin Löscher,Anna Martinez,Abhishek Singh,Stephan Winnerl,Clara J. Saraceno*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种高光谱太赫兹相机，基于高平均功率（38.3 mW、400 kHz）脉冲宽带太赫兹源与并行电光探测，在约14.5 mm视场内以2 Hz速率、145,000像素同步记录太赫兹电场的完整波形（幅度与相位）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太赫兹可穿透多种光学不透明材料并依据成分进行区分，太赫兹时域光谱（THz-TDS）能记录完整电场波形、高灵敏获取幅度与相位信息。但将这一能力转化为快速、宽场成像仍面临重大挑战，主要原因是传统TDS源平均功率低，迫使采集速度、视场与灵敏度之间相互权衡。

Method: 采用高平均功率、脉冲宽带太赫兹源（38.3 mW，重复频率400 kHz）驱动并行电光探测，构建宽视场高光谱太赫兹相机，实现高通量采集。

Result: 系统在约14.5 mm视场内覆盖145,000像素，以2 Hz采集速率同步记录幅度与相位的完整太赫兹波形。

Conclusion: 结果验证了高平均功率THz-TDS用于宽带、高速成像的潜力，可推动在线材料分辨检测以及对不可逆、秒级过程（如生物相关体系中的水合作用）的光谱成像等应用。

Abstract: The terahertz (THz) region is highly attractive for imaging because it penetrates many optically opaque materials and can distinguish them by composition. Among the various methods that allow us to access this spectral region, THz time-domain spectroscopy (THz-TDS) is uniquely suited to retrieve broadband information, recording the full waveform of the THz electric field to recover both amplitude and phase with high sensitivity. Translating this capability into fast, wide-field imaging has however remained a major challenge, largely because the low average power of conventional TDS sources forces a trade-off between acquisition speed, field of view, and sensitivity. Here we demonstrate a hyperspectral THz camera in which a high-average-power, pulsed broadband THz source, delivering 38.3 mW at a 400 kHz repetition rate, drives parallel electro-optic detection over a wide field of view at high acquisition rates. The system simultaneously records the full THz waveform, in amplitude and phase, across 145,000 pixels over a ~14.5 mm field of view at an acquisition rate of 2 Hz. These results demonstrate the potential of high-average-power THz-TDS for broadband, high-speed imaging, opening applications such as inline material-resolved inspection and the spectroscopic imaging of irreversible, second-timescale processes such as hydration in biologically relevant systems.

</details>


### [181] [Three-Wave Mixing as a High-Throughput Diagnostic Tool for Nonlinear Waveguides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00747)
*Yi-Yang Liu,Kevin Chen,Kyungduk Kim,Ryotatsu Yanagimoto,Edwin Ng,Timothy P. McKenna,Marc Jankowski*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于近简并三波混频的高通量二阶非线性波导诊断方法——和频层析（SFT），可同时表征色散与纵向相位失配，并在PPLN波导上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波长尺度非线性波导的发展需要新的诊断手段，既能表征波导色散，又能表征制备引起的空间不均匀性。强约束波导虽利于紧凑高效的非线性器件，但纳米级不均匀性会限制有效相互作用长度与转换效率。传统基于二次谐波产生的诊断难以将色散与纵向相位失配变化分离。

Method: 建立基于近简并三波混频的高通量诊断方法，称为和频层析（sum-frequency tomography, SFT）。与基于二次谐波产生的传统方法不同，SFT产生二维传递函数，可将色散阶数与纵向相位失配变化分别表征。使用商用PPLN波导进行验证。

Result: SFT成功恢复了相互作用波之间的群速度失配、基频波的群速度色散，以及纵向相位失配剖面的低阶多项式近似。该方法可在数秒内完成单根波导的表征，避免了相位敏感或空间分辨测量带来的实验复杂性。

Conclusion: SFT为二阶非线性波导提供了一种高通量、快速且实验简便的诊断方案，可分离色散效应与纵向相位失配变化，适用于单根波导的逐个表征。

Abstract: The recent development of wavelength-scale nonlinear waveguides highlights the need for new diagnostic methods that characterize both waveguide dispersion and fabrication-induced spatial inhomogeneities. While tightly confining waveguides enable compact and highly efficient nonlinear devices, nanometer-scale waveguide nonuniformities can limit the effective interaction length and conversion efficiency. In this work, we establish a high-throughput diagnostic method for second-order nonlinear waveguides based on near-degenerate three-wave mixing, hereafter referred to as sum-frequency tomography (SFT). In contrast with conventional diagnostics based on second-harmonic generation, SFT produces two-dimensional transfer functions that allow dispersion orders to be characterized separately from longitudinal phase-mismatch variations. We validate this technique using commercially available PPLN waveguides and show that SFT recovers the group-velocity mismatch between the interacting waves, the group-velocity dispersion of the fundamental, and a low-order polynomial approximation of the longitudinal phase-mismatch profile. SFT avoids the experimental complexities of phase-sensitive or spatially resolved measurements, enabling the characterization of individual waveguides in seconds.

</details>


### [182] [Calibration-Free Photonic Memory in a Silicon Photonics Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00748)
*Ankur Singh,Akhilesh Jaiswal*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种基于非谐振可变光衰减器的免校准光子存储位单元，无需腔共振对准即可维持互补电状态，并实现选择性写入与共享式光读出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光子存储位单元多依赖谐振腔结构，需要精确的腔共振对准与校准，工艺敏感且开销大；同时读出通常需要额外衰减器或独立激光源，增加面积与功耗。

Method: 在GlobalFoundries 45SPCLO平台上，采用非谐振可变光衰减器构建位单元，通过电光反馈维持互补电状态；用字线控制的衰减器配合差分光学位线实现选择性写入；光读出复用保持态的输入与输出分路器，避免附加读出衰减器和独立激光源。通过电路仿真评估写入、保持与能耗。

Result: 仿真显示可用10 ps、峰值功率2 mW的光脉冲完成写入；在光与电功率持续供给下可保持数据，每个逻辑状态的保持时间仿真达5 s；所评估的翻转增量写入能量为0.562 pJ（含电路电能及20%墙插效率下的激光贡献）；位单元版图面积为147.92×219.93 μm²。

Conclusion: 该免校准光子存储位单元通过非谐振结构与复用读出方案，降低了对腔共振对准的依赖，减少了额外光学器件需求，并在给定工艺平台上展示了可行的写入、保持性能与能耗指标。

Abstract: We propose a calibration-free photonic memory bitcell based on nonresonant variable optical attenuators in the GlobalFoundries 45SPCLO platform. The cell maintains complementary electrical states through electro-optical feedback without cavity-resonance alignment. Wordline-controlled attenuators enable selective writing through differential optical bitlines, while optical readout shares the holding input and output splitters, avoiding additional read attenuators or a separate laser source. Circuit simulations demonstrate writing with $10~\mathrm{ps}$ optical pulses at $2~\mathrm{mW}$ peak power. The cell retains data as long as optical and electrical power are supplied, with retention simulated for $5~\mathrm{s}$ per logic state. The evaluated transition consumes an incremental write energy of $0.562~\mathrm{pJ}$, including circuit electrical energy and the laser contribution at 20\% wall-plug efficiency. The bitcell layout occupies $147.92\times219.93~μ\mathrm{m}^{2}$.

</details>


### [183] [Parallax Depth Sectioning and 3D Reconstruction in 4D-STEM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01057)
*Desheng Ma,Chia-Hao Lee,Zixiao Shi,David A. Muller,Steven E. Zeltmann*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文将4D-STEM中深度信息的编码与检索与层析成像和光场摄影联系起来，推导了3D相位衬度传递函数，证明tcBF深度切片等价于非迭代层析重建，并提出利用单次4D FFT快速提取二维切片的光场算法；进一步展示acBF深度切片能消除衬度振荡、提升3D重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D-STEM通过不同探测器像素所成虚拟像之间的视差编码三维信息，但其深度信息编码与检索机制与既有三维成像方法的关系尚不清晰，缺乏统一的物理与算法框架，且现有深度切片重建在速度与深度响应均匀性方面存在不足。

Method: 将4D数据视为角度切片集合得到一组正弦图；在小角近似下证明倾斜校正明场（tcBF）深度切片等价于非迭代层析重建；同时把4D数据映射为光场（全光）成像，借用光场算法，通过对数据集做单次四维快速傅里叶变换（4D FFT）提取二维切片获得tcBF深度切片；并采用深度分辨的像差校正明场（acBF）成像进行重建。

Result: tcBF深度切片与光场算法通过单次4D FFT即可实现大幅加速，尤其在重建大量深度切片时；acBF相比tcBF去除了衬度振荡并减小了响应随深度的变化，获得改进的3D重建；在模拟与实验数据中实现了acBF深度切片，分辨出堆叠氧化物薄膜和纳米颗粒组装体中的不同层。

Conclusion: 4D-STEM的深度成像可从层析与光场两种视角统一理解，几何深度分辨率与相位衬度成像的波动光学效应得以区分；基于单次4D-STEM数据集的无迭代体重建有望用于厚、弱散射样品的成像以及低剂量采集的重建。

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) information is encoded in four-dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy (4D-STEM) through parallax between the virtual images formed at each detector pixel. In this paper we connect the encoding and retrieval of depth-dependent information in 4D-STEM to two other widely-used 3D imaging methods, tomography and light-field photography, and derive its 3D phase contrast transfer function. Viewing the 4D data as a collection of angular slices yields a collection of sinograms, providing an intuitive visual representation of the 3D information transfer. Within the small-angle approximation, tilt-corrected bright-field (tcBF) depth sectioning is equivalent to non-iterative tomographic reconstruction. Alternatively, the 4D data also can be mapped to light-field (or plenoptic) imaging, which acquires spatially resolved diffraction data in parallel using an array of lenslets. Adapting a light-field imaging algorithm, we show that tcBF depth sections can be obtained by extracting two-dimensional slices from a single four-dimensional fast Fourier transform (4D FFT) of the dataset, yielding a substantial speedup when reconstructing a large number of depth slices. These differing perspectives distinguish the geometric aspects of depth resolution from wave-optical effects that are present in phase-contrast imaging. Depth-resolved aberration-corrected bright-field (acBF) imaging yields improved 3D reconstructions compared to tcBF, removing contrast oscillations and reducing the variation in response with depth. We demonstrate acBF depth sectioning using simulated and experimental data and resolve distinct layers in stacked oxide films and nanoparticle assemblies. These non-iterative volumetric reconstructions from a single 4D-STEM dataset show promise for improved imaging of thick, weakly-scattering samples and for reconstructing low-dose acquisitions.

</details>


### [184] [Cross-platform frequency-domain physical neural networks with identical models and parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01059)
*Zichen Xi,Jun Ji,Liyang Jin,Hsuan-Hao Lu,Wenjie Xiong,Linbo Shao*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨平台频域物理神经网络（PNN），利用共享的二阶非线性过程，在光学、微波和声学平台上使用同一组预训练参数实现无需微调的统一部署，并在四分类任务上取得97.6%（光学）、98.4%（电子）、98.2%（力学）的推理精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有频域物理神经网络虽具备器件数量少、鲁棒性强、推理精度高等优势，但其在光学、声学、电子等不同物理域的实现大多依赖特定器件或平台，通常需要硬件相关模型和制造后参数调优，缺乏通用性。

Method: 利用各平台共享的二阶非线性过程，在光学、微波和声学波平台上实现同一预训练的频域PNN模型，无需对参数进行微调，并在统一的四分类任务上进行评估。

Result: 频域PNN在光学平台达到97.6%、电子平台98.4%、力学平台98.2%的高推理精度；同时系统比较了三种物理域的相关性能指标。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了跨物理平台的频域物理神经网络通用部署，为构建跨不同物理平台的通用神经网络奠定了基础。

Abstract: Frequency-domain physical neural networks (PNNs) have emerged as a promising analog computing paradigm, achieving a reduced number of devices, enhanced robustness, and high inference accuracy. However, existing demonstrations across various physical domains, such as optics, acoustics, and electronics, are mostly specific to the target devices or platforms, usually requiring hardware-dependent models and post-fabrication parameter tuning. Here, we demonstrate cross-platform implementations of frequency-domain PNN across different wave-based computing platforms using identical, pre-trained parameters. By exploiting shared second-order nonlinear processes, the PNN model is implemented on optical, microwave, and acoustic-wave platforms without the need of fine tuning the parameters. Evaluated on a unified four-class classification task, the frequency-domain PNN achieves high inference accuracies of 97.6% in optics, 98.4% in electronics, and 98.2% in mechanics. Further, we systematically compare related performance metrics across these three distinct physical domains, paving the way to a universal neural network across different physical platforms.

</details>


### [185] [Independent Amplitude and Phase Modulation in Active Transmissive Metasurfaces via Complex Permittivity Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01085)
*Christopher M. Yi,Ruzan Sokhoyan,Juyoung Kim,Sungmin Hong,Jinwoo Song,Harry A. Atwater,Min Seok Jang*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文理论提出一种中红外主动透射式超表面，通过高Q锗Mie谐振器与石墨烯条带集成，利用石墨烯带内/带间跃迁与锗热光效应，在同一波长实现近100%透射率调制和282°纯相位调制，并验证了相对衍射效率超过90%的光束偏转器件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动超表面是空间光调制器（SLM）的重要平台，可在亚波长尺度动态调控光的振幅与相位。透射式超表面因可与芯片级光源单片集成而具有紧凑优势，但此前独立振幅与相位调制仅在主动反射式超表面中实现，透射式受设计挑战所限尚未达成该目标。

Method: 提出由高Q立方形锗（Ge）Mie谐振器与石墨烯条带集成的主动透射式超表面。利用石墨烯带内与带间跃迁，通过静电栅压调控透射率；并借助锗的热光效应作为全局调谐机制，对不同基础温度下的谐振频率进行光谱对准，使透射率调制与纯相位调制可在同一工作波长实现。最后通过逐单元寻址构建光束偏转器件。

Result: 静电栅压可将某一光谱频率处透射率从近0%调制至5%，并在另一频率实现281°纯相位调制。通过热光效应光谱对准后，双机制架构在同一波长实现约100%的透射率调制效率和282°纯相位调制。逐单元寻址的光束偏转器件相对衍射效率超过90%。

Conclusion: 所提出的双机制、可独立寻址主动超表面在中红外实现了独立振幅与相位调制，为紧凑、动态可重构的元光子器件开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Active metasurfaces are a promising platform for spatial light modulators (SLMs) in applications such as holography, beam shaping, and light detection and ranging (LiDAR), as they enable dynamic control over the amplitude and phase of light at subwavelength scales. Among these, transmissive metasurfaces offer a compact alternative to conventional SLMs, since they allow monolithic integration with chip-scale light sources. While independent amplitude and phase modulation have been previously demonstrated in active reflective metasurfaces, this important milestone has not been achieved in transmission due to the inherent challenges in designing active transmissive metasurfaces. Here, we theoretically demonstrate an active transmissive metasurface that enables independent amplitude and phase modulation in the mid-infrared. The proposed metasurface consists of high-Q cuboid germanium (Ge) Mie resonators integrated with graphene stripes. Utilizing the intraband and interband transitions of graphene, electrostatic gating enables modulation of the transmittance from near 0% to 5% at one spectral frequency and achieves 281° phase-only modulation at another frequency in the same device. To achieve both types of modulation at the same operating wavelength, we use the thermo-optic effect of Ge as a global tuning mechanism to spectrally align the resonant frequencies, enabling both transmittance and phase-only modulation at different base temperatures. The proposed dual-mechanism architecture achieves transmittance modulation efficiency of ~100% and 282° phase-only modulation. Finally, we demonstrate the efficacy of phase-only modulation by individually addressing each unit cell to realize a beam-steering device with relative diffraction efficiencies over 90%. Our individually addressable active metasurface opens a route toward compact, dynamically reconfigurable metaphotonic devices.

</details>


### [186] [Vector solitons in nonlinear lattices with competing SPM and XPM terms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01212)
*Liangwei Zeng,Boris A. Malomed,Dumitru Mihalache,Xing Zhu*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 具有反相空间调制且SPM与XPM系数符号相反的双组分光子非线性晶格可支持基态和混合两类光矢量孤子，两者均稳定，仅在小传播常数区间失稳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索双组分光子非线性晶格中，当自相位调制（SPM）与交叉相位调制（XPM）系数采用反相空间调制且符号相反时，能否形成并稳定存在光矢量孤子（VSs），以拓展矢量孤子的调控自由度。

Method: 构建具有反相空间调制SPM与XPM系数、且两系数符号相反的双组分光子非线性晶格模型，求解其矢量孤子解，并分析基态与混合两类孤子的结构与线性稳定性。

Result: 该晶格支持两类光矢量孤子：基态孤子由两个类高斯分量构成，混合孤子由类高斯分量与偶极分量组合而成。稳定性分析表明两类孤子均稳定，仅在小传播常数取值处存在窄失稳区间。

Conclusion: 反相空间调制且SPM与XPM符号相反的双组分非线性晶格是承载稳定基态与混合矢量孤子的有效平台，其失稳仅局限于小传播常数附近的狭窄参数区间。

Abstract: We demonstrate that two-component photonic nonlinear lattices with opposite signs of the out-of-phase spatially modulated SPM and XPM coefficients can support two types of optical vector solitons (VSs), the fundamental and mixed ones. The fundamental VSs are composed of two Gaussian-like components, while the mixed ones combine Gaussian-like and dipole components. The structures and stability of both types of the VSs are investigated. The VSs are stable, except for narrow intervals at small values of the propagation constant.

</details>


### [187] [A Mid-Infrared Absorber Inspired by the Wing-Scale Photonic Structure of Troides magellanus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01274)
*Amandine Marchand,Kevin Delmote,Olivier Deparis,Sébastien R. Mouchet*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 通过仿生蝴蝶翼鳞结构并进行几何尺度缩放，设计优化出宽带中红外吸收器，吸收增强达391%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境与能源挑战加剧，亟需提升能量回收与资源效率的技术；仿生光子结构为高效红外吸收器提供了新思路。

Method: 以Troides magellanus蝴蝶高吸收翼鳞特征几何为原型，将其尺度缩放并适配，使光学响应从可见光移至红外波段；通过数值模拟研究主要几何参数对光谱吸收率的影响并优化构型。

Result: 优化结构相较平坦钢参考实现391%的吸收增强；性能提升归因于高长径比几何增强多重散射与光捕获，增大有效光-物质相互作用面积。

Conclusion: 仿生几何尺度缩放是工程化高效红外吸收器并调控其光谱响应的有效策略，可用于热辐射管理与红外光子应用。

Abstract: Growing environmental and energy-related challenges have intensified the search for technologies capable of improving energy recovery and resource efficiency. Bioinspired photonic structures offer promising opportunities for the development of efficient infrared absorbers by exploiting optical mechanisms found in natural systems. In this work, we present a bioinspired approach for the design and optimization of a broadband mid-infrared absorber based on the highly absorbing wing scales of the Troides magellanus Magellan birdwing butterfly. The characteristic geometry of the biological structure is rescaled and adapted to shift its optical response from the visible to the infrared spectral range. Numerical simulations are used to investigate the influence of the main geometrical parameters on the spectral absorptance and to identify an optimized configuration. The optimized structure achieves an absorption enhancement of 391% compared with a flat steel reference. The improved optical response is attributed to the optimized high-aspect-ratio geometry, which enhances multiple scattering and light trapping, thereby increasing the effective light-matter interaction area. These results demonstrate the potential of bioinspired geometric scaling as an effective strategy for engineering efficient infrared absorbers and tailoring their spectral response for thermal radiation management and infrared photonic applications.

</details>


### [188] [Inverted Leakage Radiation Microscopy for measurement of nonreciprocity induced by a plasmonic metasurface with false chirality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01374)
*Ahmed Lafeef Ettapuram Naduvilepurayil,Sahil Sahoo,Yuri Gorodetski*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文在等离激元超表面上实验检测非互易性并确定其方向，利用倒置漏辐射显微成像系统，将非互易性关联到介质的"伪手性"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易性（光在相反方向传播时经历不同光学行为）是信号处理、降噪、单向传播与传感等应用的基础；因此需要在纳米光子学中实现对非互易性的检测及其方向判定。

Method: 采用倒置漏辐射显微（Inverted Leakage Radiation Microscopy）装置，在等离激元超表面上进行非互易性检测。该装置等效于经典萨格纳克环（Sagnac loop），在像平面产生干涉图样；通过考察所得图像的拓扑序来判定非互易性及其方向。

Result: 实验表明非互易性与介质的"伪手性"（false chirality）相关联，装置可产生干涉图样，并通过图像拓扑序实现对非互易性的检测。

Conclusion: 该方案为在等离激元学中检测伪手性提供了一种紧凑而通用的方法，并暗示其在纳米光子学、化学与应用物理等领域具有多种潜在应用前景。

Abstract: A system has a nonreciprocity when the light propagating through it in opposite directions encounters different optical adventures. This effect lies in the origin of various applications, including signal processing, noise reduction, unidirectional propagation, and sensing. Here, we experimentally demonstrate the detection of nonreciprocity in plasmonic metasurfaces and a method to determine its direction. We use the Inverted Leakage Radiation Microscopy setup to show that the non- reciprocity is linked to a false chirality of the medium. The setup behaves as an equivalent of the classical Sagnac loop providing an interference pattern at the image plane. The non-reciprocity is then detected by examining the topological order of the resulting image. Our system represents a compact and versatile method to detect false chirality in plasmonics and suggests a variety of potential areas of implementation in nanophotonics, chemistry and applied physics.

</details>


### [189] [Stochastic switching in quadrupole-trap-based levitodynamic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01387)
*Vadim Rybin,Semyon Rudyi,Dmitrii Shcherbinin*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文理论研究了大气压下四极阱中带电微粒在主参量共振附近的随机切换动力学，揭示其源于热噪声激活逃逸与耗散回归之间的平衡，并可用有效Gibbs分布描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究四极阱中带电微粒在参量共振附近的随机切换行为机制，并评估其作为精密探测与激活动力学研究平台的潜力。

Method: 基于主参量共振条件，对微粒在线性与非线性动力学区域之间的热噪声激活逃逸和耗散回归过程进行理论建模与分析，引入有效Gibbs分布、有效势垒和有效系统温度描述切换统计。

Result: 随机切换服从有效Gibbs分布，其有效势垒与有效系统温度均可实验测量；有效势垒依赖于微粒的质量与电荷。

Conclusion: 热噪声在此过程中揭示而非掩盖跃迁，其指数依赖关系可为噪声感知的精密探测方案提供基础；该体系也是研究非平衡参量系统激活动力学的可调平台。

Abstract: We have theoretically investigated the stochastic switching dynamics of a charged microparticle in a quadrupole trap near the principal parametric resonance at atmospheric pressure. We show that this behavior arises from a balance of thermal noise-activated escape and dissipative return between linear and nonlinear dynamical regimes of the particle motion. The switching follows an effective Gibbs distribution with an effective potential barrier between the regimes and effective system temperature, which are both experimentally accessible. Because the effective barrier depends on particle mass and charge, and since thermal noise reveals rather than obscures the transition, this exponential dependence could provide the foundation to precision noise-aware detection schemes. The system further offers a highly adjustable platform for studying activation dynamics in non-equilibrium parametric systems.

</details>


### [190] [Engineering photonic crystal slab modes for strong exciton-photon coupling and polariton dispersion control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01473)
*Natalia Salakhova,Andrey Demenev,Vladimir Kulakovskii,Nikolay Gippius*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该研究数值模拟了周期加倍Si3N4光子晶体板与hBN封装MoSe2单层的杂化结构，通过周期加倍与hBN厚度调控实现激子-光子耦合增强（Rabi分裂达8.5 meV）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何在基于过渡金属硫族化合物单层的杂化结构中灵活调控辐射耦合、激子-光子重叠及极化激元色散。

Method: 对周期加倍的Si3N4光子晶体板与hBN封装MoSe2单层构成的杂化结构进行数值模拟，利用周期加倍将准导模折叠入光锥产生亮态与类BIC模式，并将hBN厚度作为独立调节垂直场分布的参数，研究有限动量下TE-like模的杂化及极化激元形成。

Result: 周期加倍产生两个光谱分离的光子分支（一亮态、一类BIC）；增大hBN厚度使共振场向MoSe2重分布，Rabi分裂由约3-4 meV增至8.5 meV，耦合强度由约1.5-1.7 meV增至4.3-4.4 meV；亮态与类BIC对比显示品质因子主要决定辐射可见性与线宽，耦合强度主要由单层处场幅决定；hBN控制有限动量下折叠TE-like模杂化，产生附加光子带隙，其大小可由hBN厚度调节并在约113 nm处闭合；观察到有限动量激子-极化激元态。

Conclusion: 周期加倍光子晶体板是调控杂化结构中辐射耦合、激子-光子重叠及极化激元色散的灵活平台。

Abstract: We numerically study exciton-photon coupling in a hybrid structure composed of a period-doubled Si3N4 photonic crystal slab and an hBN-encapsulated MoSe2 monolayer. Period doubling folds quasi-guided modes into the light cone and produces spectrally separated photonic branches whose radiative character is controlled by the symmetry of the doubled unit cell, resulting in one bright and one bound state in the continuum (BIC)-like mode at normal incidence. We then use the hBN thickness as an independent tuning parameter for the vertical field profile. Increasing the hBN thickness redistributes the resonant field toward the MoSe2 monolayer and enhances the Rabi splitting from about 3-4 meV to 8.5 meV, corresponding to an increase in the coupling strength from approximately 1.5-1.7 meV to 4.3-4.4 meV. Comparison between bright and BIC-like configurations shows that the quality factor primarily determines radiative visibility and linewidth, whereas the coupling strength is mainly governed by the field amplitude at the monolayer. We further show that the hBN layer controls the hybridization of folded TE-like modes at finite momentum, leading to an additional photonic gap whose size can be tuned by the hBN thickness and closes at an hBN thickness of approximately 113 nm. In the hybrid structure, we observe finite-momentum exciton-polariton states that, depending on the hBN thickness, form either through coupling to an isolated photonic branch or through coupling to two nearly degenerate photonic modes. These results identify period-doubled photonic crystal slabs as a flexible platform for controlling radiative coupling, exciton-photon overlap, and polariton dispersion in hybrid structures based on transition-metal dichalcogenide monolayers.

</details>


### [191] [Inherent Turbulence Immunity of Vector Vortex Beams in Free Space Quantum Key Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01523)
*Behnam Talari,Rouhollah Karimzadeh*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文提出利用混合偏振-轨道角动量（OAM）纠缠的矢量涡旋光束（VVB），在大气湍流下实现无需硬件的抗退相干编码，通过数值模拟将QBER从42.0%抑制至4.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由空间量子密钥分发（QKD）中，OAM复用提供高维Hilbert空间，但纯标量空间模式（拓扑荷|ℓ|≥1）在陆地大气湍流中严重退相干：湍流折射率涡旋劈裂高阶涡旋奇点、引发相邻拓扑信道间灾难性串扰，使QBER迅速超过无条件11%安全阈值。因此需要一种鲁棒的编码方案。

Method: 提出采用VVB（即混合偏振-OAM纠缠态）编码。理论依据是空气光学各向异性极小（Δn<10⁻⁹），折射率涨落对正交圆偏振模式表现为相同的共模标量相位屏，在相对偏振相位自由度上相消。通过在改进的功率谱相位屏上对不同湍流强度（D/r₀=0至3.0）进行数值传播模拟验证。

Result: VVB编码协议将渐近QBER从42.0%降至4.8%，误差抑制因子约11.6，且无需主动自适应光学或变形镜。

Conclusion: 混合偏振-OAM纠缠的VVB编码可提供对湍流扰动的内在免疫，是高容量自由空间QKD的有效且硬件简化的方案。

Abstract: Orbital angular momentum (OAM) multiplexing provides an infinite-dimensional discrete Hilbert space ideally suited for high-capacity free-space quantum key distribution (QKD). Nevertheless, pure scalar spatial modes carrying topological charge ($|\ell| \ge 1$) undergo severe decoherence when transmitted through terrestrial atmospheric turbulence. Turbulent refractive-index eddies split high-order vortex singularities, induce catastrophic intermodal crosstalk across adjacent topological channels, and rapidly drive the quantum bit error rate (QBER) well above the unconditional 11% security threshold associated with individual cloning attacks. Here, we demonstrate that hybrid polarization-OAM entangled states, known as vector vortex beams (VVBs), provide intrinsic, hardware-free immunity against turbulent perturbations. Because the optical anisotropy of terrestrial air is exceedingly small ($Δn < 10^{-9}$), refractive-index fluctuations couple symmetrically to orthogonal circular polarization modes as an identical common-mode scalar phase screen that cancels in the relative polarization-phase degree of freedom. By numerically propagating modal fields through modified power-spectral phase screens over turbulence strengths ranging from $D/r_0 = 0$ to $3.0$, we show that the VVB encoding protocol suppresses the asymptotic QBER from 42.0% to 4.8%, yielding an error-suppression factor of approximately 11.6 without requiring active adaptive optics or deformable mirrors.

</details>


### [192] [Measuring Geometric Phase based on Indefinite Causal Order in a Sagnac Interferometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01540)
*Lucas Marques Fagundes,Finlay Campbell,Richard Aguiar Maduro,Renné Medeiros de Araújo,Stephen M. Barnett,Sarah Croke,Sonja Franke-Arnold*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文指出萨格纳克干涉仪中顺时针与逆时针光路遍历光学元件的顺序相反，从而可作为一种经典的不定因果序实现，并用单次测量识别两个非对易偏振操作对应的几何相位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 萨格纳克干涉仪的共路噪声抵消带来高稳定性，其旋转敏感性是激光陀螺仪的基础；作者希望挖掘其较少被探索的特性——正反方向光遍历光学元件顺序相反，以此探索经典的不定因果序。

Method: 利用萨格纳克干涉仪中顺、逆时针分量经过光学元件顺序相反的机制，将其视为不定因果序的经典实现，并据此以单次测量提取两个非对易偏振操作所关联的几何相位。

Result: 证明了可通过单次测量识别两个非对易偏振操作对应的几何相位，验证了该方案对不定因果序的经典演示能力。

Conclusion: 该思路可应用于快速测定偏振操控或手性介质中的光学活性，但首要意义在于为不定因果序提供了一种几何化的直观图示。

Abstract: Sagnac interferometers are important tools for precision measurements. Cancellation of common-path noise ensures a high degree of stability against external perturbations, while their sensitivity to rotations provides the basis of laser gyroscopes. Here we highlight another, less explored feature of Sagnac interferometers: the fact that optical elements are traversed in reverse order for clockwise and counterclockwise propagating light components. This provides the opportunity to explore a classical realization of indefinite causal order, where the order of events within a sequence is not fixed. We explore this concept to identify the geometric phase associated with two non-commuting polarization operations with a single measurement. This idea may have applications for the rapid determination of polarization manipulations or optical activity within chiral media, but foremost it provides a geometric illustration of indefinite causal order.

</details>


### [193] [Digitally enhanced Multi-wavelength Stabilization using a Passive Fiber Frequency Reference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01575)
*Hilma Karlsson,Vaishali Adya,Robert L. Ward,Chathura P. Bandutunga*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出基于编码复用的被动光纤干涉仪方案，用单一共享参考实现85 GHz间隔激光间的频率稳定与传递，并给出向多光源扩展的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高稳定激光频率稳定在精密计量、频率传递与量子通信中至关重要；传统方案在多光源场景常依赖光学频率梳，开销大，需要可扩展且频率灵活的低成本架构。

Method: 采用基于编码复用（code-based multiplexing）的被动光纤干涉仪作为共享参考，通过同一无源参考对频率间隔85 GHz的两束激光进行频率稳定与传递；并进行环外（out-of-loop）噪声表征、连续激光可调谐性测试以及残余长度噪声耦合分析，最后给出向更高激光数目扩展的架构框架。

Result: 环外表征给出传递频率噪声在40 mHz以上傅里叶频率处的上限为sub-kHz/√Hz，1 s处差分分数稳定度为7×10^-13；同时验证了连续可调谐性并表征了残余长度噪声耦合。

Conclusion: 编码复用的被动光纤干涉仪可在无需光学频率梳的情况下，对多光源实现可扩展、频率灵活的频率稳定与传递。

Abstract: High-stability frequency stabilization between lasers is essential for applications such as precision metrology, frequency dissemination, and quantum communications. We demonstrate the use of code-based multiplexing of a passive fiber-based interferometer for frequency stabilization and transfer between lasers separated by 85 GHz in frequency using a single shared passive reference. Out-of-loop characterization places an upper bound of sub-kHz/$\sqrt{\text{Hz}}$ on the transferred frequency noise at Fourier frequencies above 40 mHz, with a differential fractional stability of $7\times10^{-13}$ at 1 second. We also demonstrate continuous laser tunability and characterize the residual length noise coupling. Finally, we provide a framework for scaling the architecture to higher laser counts, offering a pathway to scalable, frequency agile stabilization of multiple light sources without the overhead of an optical frequency comb.

</details>


### [194] [Programmable nonlinearity within nanophotonic waveguides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02024)
*Benjamin A. Ash,Ryotatsu Yanagimoto,Mandar M. Sohoni,Yang Xu,Yiqi Zhao,Martin M. Stein,Fan O. Wu,Marc Jankowski,Logan G. Wright,Tatsuhiro Onodera,Peter L. McMahon*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 通过在纳米光子波导内构建可编程的χ⁽²⁾全息图，实现约1秒内可重构的准相位匹配，补偿相位失配并进行原位诊断，从而规避制备误差带来的良率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性纳米光子学依赖相位匹配来实现高效的目标非线性过程，但在纳米光子波导中可靠实现相位匹配十分困难。传统方法通过纳米加工（如用固定几何形状的电极对非线性材料进行极化）实现，存在两个缺陷：器件的光学功能在制备时即被固定，且器件性能会因制备缺陷而退化。因此作者希望规避这些限制。

Method: 将结构化光照明投射到光电导电极上，产生电场诱导χ⁽²⁾的空间图案，从而在纳米光子波导内部构建可编程χ⁽²⁾全息图，实现可在器件制备后更新的可重构准相位匹配；并利用闭环原位优化补偿复杂波导结构中的相位失配。

Result: 可编程χ⁽²⁾对三波混频过程实现了光谱、模式与偏振调控；可重构准相位匹配可在约1秒内更新；闭环原位优化成功补偿了宽度调制波导和长度超过10 cm的螺旋波导等复杂结构中的相位失配；并实现了原位诊断，包括多种横模的直接色散测量和片上波前相位层析。

Conclusion: 该工作为构建级联非线性纳米光子系统开辟了可能，使其在规避制备差异导致的良率问题的同时，仍保留刻蚀波导在光场限制和色散工程方面的优势。

Abstract: Nonlinear nanophotonics enables the engineering of optical functions using light-matter interactions in nanoscale structures with carefully designed geometries. Phase matching in nonlinear optics is typically essential for realizing high efficiency for desired processes and engineering of sophisticated functions, but it can be challenging to reliably achieve in nanophotonic waveguides. Conventionally, phase matching in nanophotonics is achieved through nanofabrication, e.g., by poling a device's nonlinear material using electrodes with fixed geometry. This approach has two drawbacks: the optical functions a device can perform are fixed at the time of fabrication, and the device's performance can be degraded by fabrication imperfections. Here, we circumvent these limitations by engineering programmable $χ^{(2)}$ holograms inside nanophotonic waveguides, enabling reconfigurable quasi-phase matching that can be updated after device fabrication in approximately one second. By projecting structured optical illumination onto photoconductive electrodes, we generated spatial patterns of electric-field-induced $χ^{(2)}$ to exert spectral, modal, and polarization control over three-wave mixing processes. This programmability also allowed us to employ closed-loop in situ optimization to compensate for phase mismatch in complex waveguide structures, such as width-modulated waveguides and spiral waveguides exceeding 10 cm in length. Furthermore, we show that programmable nonlinearity enables powerful in situ diagnostics, including direct dispersion measurements of various transverse modes and on-chip wavefront phase tomography. Our work opens up the possibility of building cascaded nonlinear nanophotonic systems where yield issues due to fabrication variations can be sidestepped while retaining the benefits of etched waveguides such as confinement and dispersion engineering.

</details>


### [195] [Thin-capping layer epitaxial quantum dots for near-field quantum photonics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02080)
*Yuting Guo,Jonathan Bar-David,Pasquale Cilibrizzi,Sung-Yul L. Park,Jin Dong Song,Luca Sapienza*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 外延InAs/GaAs量子点在10nm和20nm薄GaAs盖层下仍保持高光学质量，线宽比以往报道小至5倍，激子寿命未受扰动，为近场等离激元光-物质相互作用与量子技术应用铺路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外延量子点是最优量子光源之一，但为隔离表面态、维持其稳定性、亮度、量子效率和相干性，通常需要超过50nm的厚盖层。这使发射体远离金属结构，无法利用等离激元等近场效应来增强光-物质相互作用、调控自发辐射动力学和发射方向性。因此需要验证薄盖层下量子点能否保持高光学质量。

Method: 生长GaAs盖层厚度分别为10nm、20nm及标准95nm的InAs/GaAs量子点，并对其开展光学表征，重点关注发射线宽与激子寿命。

Result: 在薄盖层样品上观察到线宽比以往报道小至5倍，且激子寿命未受扰动，表明量子点光学质量得到保持。

Conclusion: 即使发射体距表面很近，外延量子点仍能维持高光学质量，从而可用于探索相干稳定发射体的近场光-物质相互作用，适用于量子技术应用。

Abstract: Epitaxial quantum dots (QDs) are widely recognised as one of the best quantum light sources, given their good stability, brightness, quantum efficiency and coherence. To reach such properties, QDs are protected from potentially detrimental surface states by a relatively thick capping layer, typically exceeding 50nm. This prevents the implementation of near-field effects, like plasmonic-based ones, that can dramatically increase the light-matter interaction, since the control of the spontaneous emission dynamics and directionality of the emission can only occur if the emitter is in close proximity (typically tens of nanometers or less) from the metallic structures. In this work, we report the growth and optical characterisation of InAs/GaAs QDs with GaAs capping layer thickness of 10nm, 20nm, and a more standard 95nm. Remarkably, we observe emission linewidths up to 5 times smaller than previously reported, with unperturbed excitonic lifetimes. These results demonstrate that the epitaxial QD's high optical quality can be maintained even when the emitters are at reduced distances from the surface, opening the path for the exploration of near-field light-matter interactions with coherent and stable emitters, suitable for quantum technology applications.

</details>
